### [1] [Near-focus intensity evolution of spatiotemporal optical vortex pulses and consequences for their nonlinear upconversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14640)
*Titouan Gadeyne,Vartika Vishnoi,Thierry Ruchon*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文实验与解析研究了脉冲整形器产生的STOV的聚焦动力学，发现圆对称STOV形成位置与脉冲峰值强度位置存在显著纵向偏移，并可利用该偏移在远离STOV形成面数个瑞利长度处最大化二次谐波产率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STOV脉冲具有奇特的横向OAM及传输特性，其应用潜力日益受到关注；尽管其空时包络从焦面到远场的重塑已明确，但其强度随传播的演化尚无定量研究，而这对其在非线性光学与强场物理中的应用至关重要。

Method: 结合实验与解析方法，对由脉冲整形器产生的STOV的聚焦动力学进行表征，测定圆对称STOV形成坐标与脉冲峰值强度坐标，并以二次谐波产生实验验证其应用后果。

Result: 观测到圆对称STOV形成坐标与脉冲峰值强度坐标之间存在显著的纵向偏移；实验表明在此STOV真正形成平面之外数个瑞利长度处，二次谐波产生产率可被最大化。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了对空时脉冲进行细致表征是非线性光学实验的必要前提，并为更精细地调控光与物质相互作用的复杂激光驱动源开辟了道路。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal optical vortex (STOV) laser pulses are attracting attention for their puzzling transverse orbital angular momentum (OAM) and propagation properties, which could determine their future applications. While the characteristic reshaping of their space-time envelope upon diffraction from the focal plane to the far-field is now well-established, the evolution of their intensity as they propagate has not been studied quantitatively, despite growing interest for their use in nonlinear optics and strong-field physics. Here, we explore experimentally and analytically the focusing dynamics of STOVs produced by a pulse shaper, and evidence a substantial longitudinal shift between the coordinate at which a circularly-symmetric STOV forms and that at which the pulse reaches its peak intensity. To illustrate some practical consequences for applications, we experimentally demonstrate how the yield of second-harmonic generation can consequently be maximized several Rayleigh lengths beyond the plane where the circular STOV really occurs. These results signal careful characterization of spatiotemporal pulses as a requisite for nonlinear optical experiments, and pave the way for finer control of complex laser drivers for light-matter interactions.

</details>


### [2] [Selective spatiotemporal mode-locking in multimode Mamyshev oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14690)
*Changfeng Wang,Jia Liu,Yutai Zhao,Ran Xia,Wenjun Ni,Chunyong Yang,Xiahui Tang,Gang Xu,Perry Ping Shum*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文提出多模Mamyshev振荡器中的"光谱-模式映射"策略，通过交叉相位调制与偏置光谱滤波协同作用，选择性同步特定横模，实现可控时空锁模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模光纤中的时空锁模为超短脉冲产生引入了空间自由度，但多模孤子中横模组成的灵活调控仍具挑战性。

Method: 提出光谱-模式映射策略：利用交叉相位调制与偏置光谱滤波的协同作用选择性同步特定横模；借助四波混频稳定模间相位关系，实现群速度匹配的可选择时空锁模。在腔内增益与滤波失谐构成的参数空间中，对纯LP01模、简并LP11模及LP02+LP21复合模进行选择与映射，并研究不同群速度共存模群间的腔内碰撞以动态选模。

Result: 成功选择性地实现含纯LP01模、简并LP11模或LP02+LP21复合模的时空孤子，并在增益-滤波失谐参数空间中给出映射；腔内碰撞可实现动态模式选择；在梯度折射率多模光纤六模模拟之外，于阶跃折射率多模光纤及支持更多横模的光纤中同样实现选择性时空锁模，验证了方法的普适性。

Conclusion: 该策略为横模调控提供了通用且鲁棒的途径，为多模超快光纤激光器中的全维度结构光操控提供了新见解。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal mode-locking (STML) in multimode fibers introduces spatial degrees of freedom for ultrashort pulse generation, yet flexible manipulation of transverse mode compositions within multimode solitons remains challenging. Here, we propose a spectrum-to-mode mapping strategy in multimode Mamyshev oscillators (MOs) to selectively synchronize specific transverse modes through synergistic cross-phase modulation and offset spectral filtering. Meanwhile, four-wave mixing acts to stabilize intermodal phase relationships, facilitating selective STML characterized by diverse mode compositions with matched group velocities. Spatiotemporal solitons containing the pure LP01 mode, degenerate LP11 modes, or LP02+LP21 composite modes are selectively realized and mapped across the parameter space spanned by intracavity gain and filter detuning. Furthermore, intracavity collisions between coexisting mode groups with disparate group velocities are demonstrated to enable dynamic mode selection. Beyond the simulation of six modes in graded-index multimode fibers, selective STML is also demonstrated in MOs based on step-index multimode fibers and fibers supporting more transverse modes, confirming the generality of our approach. This strategy offers a versatile and robust pathway for transverse mode regulation and provides new insights into full dimensional structured light manipulation in multimode ultrafast fiber lasers.

</details>


### [3] [Ultra-low-loss Ta2O5 waveguide for integrated nonlinear photonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14878)
*Xiaotong Zhang,Shuxuan Guo,Ruixuan Yi,Zheze Shi,Like Shui,Long Huang,Leiran Wang,Yang Wang,Junling Qu,Wenfu Zhang,Xuetao Gan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文利用Damascene工艺制备纯Ta2O5微环谐振器，在强束缚条件下实现超低损耗（Q>4.0×10^6，损耗<10 dB/m）并保持反常色散，成功产生108.32 GHz亮孤子微梳，展示7.5 GHz单孤子窗口与良好热稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成非线性光子学要求波导同时具备超低损耗、较强非线性与紧模式束缚。Ta2O5兼具宽透明窗口、较强三阶非线性与厚膜生长优势，但在紧束缚波导中光模与刻蚀侧壁相互作用增强，导致散射损耗，成为实现高Q反常色散谐振器的重要限制，即存在束缚-散射的权衡。

Method: 采用基于Damascene（大马士革）概念的制备流程，在纯Ta2O5波导中缓解束缚-散射权衡，制备截面为1.6 μm × 0.85 μm的微环谐振器（MRR），并评估其本征品质因子、传播损耗与色散特性；进而利用该谐振器并在优化双泵浦条件下产生亮耗散克尔孤子微梳，进行单孤子窗口与热双稳分析。

Result: MRR本征品质因子超过4.0×10^6，对应传播损耗低于10 dB/m，同时保持适于亮耗散克尔孤子的反常色散；产生重复频率108.32 GHz的相干亮单孤子微梳；优化双泵浦下可获得7.5 GHz的单孤子窗口，显示较大失谐容差，且与Ta2O5较弱热光响应一致；热双稳分析表明残余散射损耗与材料吸收损耗相当，而非主导损耗预算。

Conclusion: 结果表明，纯Ta2O5平台可同时实现低光学损耗、紧模式束缚与适于孤子形成的色散工程，为集成非线性光子器件及更复杂芯片级非线性光子系统提供了超低损耗平台基础。

Abstract: Integrated nonlinear photonics demands waveguides that simultaneously offer ultra-low-loss, relatively strong nonlinearity, and tight mode confinement. Tantalum pentoxide (Ta2O5) is attractive for this purpose because it combines a broad transparency window, relatively strong third-order nonlinearity, and favorable thick-film growth characteristics. In tightly confined Ta2O5 waveguides, however, enhanced interaction between the optical mode and etched sidewalls makes scattering loss an important limitation for achieving high Q anomalous-dispersion resonators. Here, we mitigate this confinement-scattering trade-off in pure Ta2O5 waveguides using a fabrication flow based on the Damascene concept. The resulting microring resonators (MRRs) with a cross-section of 1.6 μm x 0.85 μm exhibit intrinsic quality factors exceeding 4.0 x 106, corresponding to propagation losses below 10 dB/m, while maintaining anomalous dispersion suitable for bright dissipative Kerr soliton operation. Using these resonators, we generate a coherent bright single-soliton microcomb with a repetition rate of 108.32 GHz. Under optimized dual-pump operating conditions, the platform further exhibits an experimentally accessible single-soliton window spanning 7.5 GHz, indicating a large detuning tolerance for soliton operation; this broad operating window is consistent with the relatively weak thermo-optic response of Ta2O5. Thermal-bistability analysis further indicates that the residual scattering loss is comparable to the material-absorption loss, rather than dominating the loss budget. These results demonstrate that low optical loss, tight mode confinement, and dispersion engineering suitable for soliton formation can be jointly achieved in a pure Ta2O5 platform, providing an ultra-low-loss platform foundation for integrated nonlinear photonic devices and more complex chip-scale nonlinear photonic systems.

</details>


### [4] [Resonant dispersive wave emission: a route toward few-cycle, tunable strong-field drivers in the visible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15108)
*Martin Gebhardt,Joleik Nordmann,Nikoleta Kotsina,Michael Heynck,Teodora Grigorova,Christian Brahms,John C. Travers*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究利用大芯径充气毛细管光纤中的孤子动力学与共振色散波发射，产生多GW峰值功率、亚10飞秒、光谱可调（220–752 nm）的可见光脉冲，为高效HHG及强场物理提供新型驱动激光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高强度超快激光是强场物理的基础，并通过高次谐波产生（HHG）推动相干高光子能量辐射的桌面化应用。在HHG中采用短波长驱动有望高效转换至极紫外，但这要求极短的脉冲持续时间，现有驱动源难以同时兼顾高能量与短脉宽。

Method: 在大芯径、充气毛细管光纤中利用孤子动力学实现高能量共振色散波发射，从而产生多GW峰值功率、亚10 fs的可见光脉冲，并实现宽光谱调谐（220–752 nm）。

Result: 获得了多GW峰值功率、脉宽低于10 fs、光谱可在220–752 nm范围内调谐的可见光脉冲。

Conclusion: 共振色散波有望成为用于HHG与强场物理的新型驱动激光类别，同时在超快光谱学中具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Intense ultrafast lasers are the backbone of strong-field physics and enable table-top scale applications of coherent high-photon energy radiation through high-harmonic generation (HHG). Using short-wavelength drivers in HHG promises highly efficient conversion into the extreme ultraviolet, but short pulse durations are essential to this approach. We generate multi-GW peak power, sub-10 fs pulses in the visible with broad spectral tunability (220--752 nm) by harnessing soliton dynamics in large-core, gas-filled capillary fibers for high-energy resonant dispersive wave emission. This establishes the potential of resonant dispersive waves as a new class of driving lasers for HHG and strong-field physics in addition to widespread applications in ultrafast spectroscopy.

</details>


### [5] [Observation of quadrature-dependent unidirectional flow of light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15116)
*Jamison Sloan,Eran Lustig,Louise Schul,Trung Kien Le,Melissa A. Guidry,Daniil M. Lukin,Shanhui Fan,Jelena Vučković*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文首次实验实现了光的相位依赖性单向传输，通过多泵浦频率在高Q纳米微腔中借助克尔非线性进行哈密顿量编程，使光场的共轭正交分量解耦并反向传播，并在真空涨落层面验证该效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光的单向传输是光子学的核心能力，支撑隔离与路由等功能。但传统单向性依赖光的模式而非相位，缺乏对相位的调控维度，限制了相位敏感的方向性放大、传感与路由等新功能。

Method: 在高Q纳米光子微谐振器中引入多个泵浦频率，利用克尔非线性实现哈密顿量编程，构建使光场共轭正交分量解耦并沿相反方向传播的全光系统；并通过测量正交分量依赖传输对真空涨落的影响来验证性能。

Result: 实验首次观测到光的相位依赖性单向传输：共轭正交分量解耦并反向传播；该方法不依赖信号的最小功率要求，并在真空涨落测量中验证了该效应的极限表现。

Conclusion: 该工作赋予光控制一种全新的基本能力，为相位敏感的方向性放大、传感与路由等此前难以实现的领域开辟了可能。

Abstract: Unidirectional transport of light is a central capability in photonics, enabling critical functionalities such as isolation and routing. Ordinarily, this directionality depends on the mode of light, but not its phase. In this work, we experimentally demonstrate phase-dependent unidirectional transport of light for the first time. Specifically, we realize a system where conjugate quadratures of an optical field decouple and propagate in opposite directions. Our all-optical approach uses multiple pump frequencies in a high-$Q$ nanophotonic micro-resonator to achieve Hamiltonian programming through Kerr nonlinearity. Even though we rely on optical nonlinearity, our approach is not limited to any minimal power requirement on the signal. We demonstrate the ultimate limit of this by measuring the effect of quadrature-dependent transport on vacuum fluctuations. Our approach unlocks a fundamental new capability for controlling light, and can enable previously inaccessible regimes of phase-sensitive directional amplification, sensing, and routing.

</details>


### [6] [Dual deconvolution multifocal fluorescence response matrix microscopy for aberration-free high-throughput super-resolution imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15264)
*Sumin Lim,Sanghyeon Park,Tran Thinh Le,Jin Hee Hong,Seokchan Yoon,Wonshik Choi*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作提出自适应多焦点照明的荧光响应矩阵成像方法，通过按样本串扰调节焦点间距，将所需相机帧数降低约三个数量级，在数秒至数分钟内获取约10^6照明位置的响应矩阵，并保持双反卷积像差校正能力，实现约100 nm横向分辨率及小鼠脑、肾组织的高分辨深度分辨体积成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 荧光响应矩阵成像可通过双反卷积分别校正激发与发射像差，但单焦点采集需大量相机帧，限制实际应用；多焦点并行可加速采集，但焦点越密，焦点间串扰越强，会偏置激发与发射光学传递函数估计，进而劣化像差校正。因此需要一种在提速与串扰之间取得平衡的采集策略。

Method: 提出高通量无像差荧光响应矩阵成像（自适应多焦点照明）：用可编程数字微镜器件（DMD）产生多焦点激发晶格，并根据样本相关串扰调节晶格焦点间距；探测器分辨的荧光响应被组装成双坐标荧光响应矩阵；通过双反卷积分别估计并校正激发与发射光学传递函数；将像差校正后的矩阵在空间频率域组合，重建无像差图像。

Result: 所需相机帧数减少约三个数量级；可在数秒至数分钟内获取约10^6个照明位置的响应矩阵；保留双反卷积的像差校正能力；横向分辨率估计约100 nm；应用于异质小鼠脑与肾组织，实现高分辨、深度分辨的体积成像并校正样本相关像差。

Conclusion: 结果确立了自适应多焦点照明是实现高通量、无像差荧光响应矩阵成像的实用途径。

Abstract: Fluorescence response matrix imaging enables separate computational correction of excitation and emission aberrations through dual deconvolution, but single-focus acquisition requires a large number of camera frames, limiting its practical use. Multifocal excitation can accelerate acquisition by recording many illumination positions in parallel, but denser foci also increase inter-focus crosstalk, which can bias the estimated excitation and emission optical transfer functions and degrade aberration correction. Here, we introduce high-throughput aberration-free fluorescence response matrix imaging with adaptive multifocal illumination, in which the inter-focus spacing of the multifocal lattice is adjusted according to specimen-dependent crosstalk. A programmable digital micromirror device generates multifocal excitation lattices, and the resulting detector-resolved fluorescence responses are assembled into a two-coordinate fluorescence response matrix. Dual deconvolution separately estimates and corrects the excitation and emission optical transfer functions, and the aberration-corrected matrix is then combined in the spatial-frequency domain to reconstruct the aberration-free image. This strategy reduces the number of required camera frames by approximately three orders of magnitude, allowing response matrices with $\sim 10^6$ illumination positions to be acquired within seconds to a few minutes. The accelerated acquisition preserves the aberration-correction capability of dual deconvolution and achieves an estimated lateral resolution of $\sim$100 nm. Applied to heterogeneous mouse brain and kidney tissue, the method enables high-resolution, depth-resolved volumetric imaging while correcting specimen-dependent aberrations. Together, these results establish adaptive multifocal illumination as a practical route to high-throughput aberration-free fluorescence response matrix imaging.

</details>


### [7] [Comparative Robustness of Mach-Zehender and Microring Photonic Matrix Multipliers Under Laser RIN and Thermal Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15400)
*Andreas Falkenberg*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该论文构建系统级仿真框架，比较MZM与MRR光子矩阵乘法器在激光RIN噪声、列向热/相位漂移及二者联合作用下的鲁棒性，以CIFAR-10上的Small CNN和ResNet-8为负载，在0.5%–20% RMS扰动范围内评估分类精度与响应曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子矩阵乘法器可加速神经网络推理，但其实际可用性取决于光学权重元件对源噪声、热变化和校准误差的响应，需在统一假设下对干涉型与谐振型权重元件进行受控比较。

Method: 将两种CIFAR-10分类器（四层紧凑CNN与ResNet-8）映射到匹配的光子乘累加链，采用统一的源分布、探测、数值与校准假设；激光波动建模为相关乘性噪声，慢漂移建模为每个输出列对已校准权重的扰动；分别进行仅RIN、仅漂移的一维扫掠及完整二维RIN-漂移网格评估，扰动RMS水平为0.5%至20%，器件方程与参数遵循QPI Labs Photonic Inference Simulator实现。

Result: 两种架构在零扰动基线下分别达到79.5%（Small CNN）和70.0%（ResNet-8）分类精度；输出包括分类精度、类别归一化混淆矩阵、滚动精度轨迹、一维鲁棒性曲线及多维响应曲面。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了比较干涉式与谐振式光子权重元件的受控系统级框架，结论限于所实现的器件模型、扰动范围、校准策略与神经网络负载。

Abstract: Photonic matrix multipliers can accelerate neural-network inference, but their practical utility depends on how optical weight elements respond to source noise, thermal variation, and calibration error. This paper compares Mach-Zehnder modulator (MZM) and microring-resonator (MRR) implementations under laser relative-intensity noise (RIN), column-wise thermal/phase drift, and their joint action. Two CIFAR-10 classifiers - a compact four-layer CNN and ResNet-8 - are mapped to matched photonic multiply-accumulate chains using common source-distribution, detection, numerical, and calibration assumptions. Both architectures reproduce the same zero-perturbation baseline for each workload: 79.5% classification accuracy for the Small CNN and 70.0% for ResNet-8. Robustness is then evaluated using separate RIN-only and drift-only sweeps and a full two-dimensional RIN-drift grid, with dimensionless RMS perturbation levels extending from 0.5% to 20%. The analysis reports classification accuracy, class normalized confusion matrices, rolling-accuracy traces, one-dimensional robustness curves, and multidimensional response surfaces. Device equations and parameter definitions follow the models implemented in the QPI Labs Photonic Inference Simulator, with shared laser fluctuations propagated as correlated multiplicative noise and slow drift represented as a per-output-column perturbation of calibrated weights. All numerical results are generated from recorded experiment configurations and random seeds. The study provides a controlled system-level framework for comparing interferometric and resonant photonic weight elements while limiting conclusions to the implemented device models, perturbation ranges, calibration policy, and neural-network workloads.

</details>


### [8] [From Microresonator to Packaged Microcomb System: Fiber-Coupled Module and System Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15519)
*Yudai Matsumura,Yoshihiro Makimoto,Tsutomu Mizuguchi,Masayuki Higaki,Yu Tokizane,Naoya Kuse,Takeshi Yasui*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作研制了面向现场部署的紧凑、鲁棒孤子微梳光源：将光纤直接耦合的SiN微谐振器与精密热控集成于掌上模块，并进一步封装进19英寸机架系统，实现长期稳定与自动化启动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤子微梳在实验室已成熟，但体积庞大、启动依赖人工、对环境扰动敏感，阻碍其现场部署应用，需要紧凑化、高鲁棒性且可自动化的集成光源方案。

Method: 采用光纤直接耦合的SiN微谐振器并集成精密热控，制成掌上模块；再与微梳产生所需的激光源和光放大器共同集成到19英寸机架外壳中；开发基于离散微梳状态评估的状态机自动启动方案，并在温度变化和随机机械振动条件下测试启动与持续运行。

Result: 模块体积与占地分别缩小16倍和81倍，系统级分别缩小2.3倍和3.7倍；人工启动成功率90%，连续孤子运行达18.8天，且在较大温度波动下仍可工作；自动化方案可在无人工干预下产生单孤子，2分钟内成功率50%、3分钟内60%；在模块直接承受随机机械振动时仍实现自动孤子启动与持续运行。

Abstract: We predict an intrinsic orbital Nernst effect in reciprocal photonic lattices with real Bloch modes. Abstract: Levitated dielectric sensors are often treated as perturbative probes of a pre-existing optical field. Even an optically thin sensor with modest refractive index can heavily restructure the intracavity fields, effectively partitioning a cavity into two subcavities with substantial cross-coupling and producing new optical resonances and trapping sites with properties which deviate from the standard intuition. Conventional perturbative expressions for the cavity frequency shift and optomechanical coupling depart from the exact solution at thicknesses as small as $\simλ/(10n)$. Furthermore, cavity locking decreases the coupling and increases the trap stiffness by orders of magnitude, and the coupled subcavity structure permits appreciable optical cooling at zero detuning, a distinct behavior unique to this nonperturbative regime. Abstract: Low-loss stoichiometric silicon nitride on oxidized silicon has emerged as the major wide-band photonics platform for nonlinear and quantum photonics. Although various stress-mitigation approaches can isolate or prevent cracking, this stress issue remains a major fabrication challenge. Low-residual-stress LPCVD stoichiometric silicon nitride on sapphire has also been reported and recently explored for photonics at telecommunication wavelengths. Here, we advance its development through systematic characterization, including thin-film stress analysis, ellipsometry and prism-coupling measurements of refractive index and loss, and swept-wavelength spectroscopy of fabricated photonic devices across a broad range of wavelengths. Abstract: Optical cavity arrays are an emerging tool for efficiently interfacing optical photons to quantum emitters for applications such as quantum computing. In this work, we demonstrate a metrology technique for such cavity arrays by interfering cavity array outputs against a tilted reference beam on a camera, constituting a spatial heterodyne. Both the amplitude and phase of each cavity's transmitted field can be extracted from the resulting images. Enabled by the improved scaling of heterodyne detection over intensity detection in the presence of technical noise, we demonstrate signal-to-noise ratio greater than one down to the single detected photon level, even in the presence of hundreds of noise equivalent photons. This readout technique holds promise for fast, non-destructive imaging of atomic qubits in cavity arrays by requiring fewer scattered photons while allowing the use of fast cameras with higher electronic readout noise.

</details>

Abstract: Light reflected from a deforming solid depends on boundary motion and strain-induced modulation of the dielectric response. For a propagating acoustic field, scattering from different depths interferes coherently, so the measured optical amplitude and phase depend jointly on the strain profile, dielectric constant, and photoelastic constant. Optical tomography often makes such reconstructions tractable through independent projections. To probe mechanical strain, however, the optical measurement also depends on the material properties that mediate its observation, requiring those properties to be known or assumptions about the field. Here we show that broadband picosecond ultrasonic echoes in an opaque solid can supply the missing information. Fourier transformation of the measured complex relative reflectance change obtained with monochromatic light at normal incidence gives access to many acoustic frequency components. We recover the complex dielectric and photoelastic constants together with the nanoscale depth-dependent strain profile by formulating the reconstruction as a multiparameter nonlinear eigenvalue problem. We demonstrate the method using experimental echoes from a chromium film. The recovered refractive index agrees with ellipsometry, whereas the recovered strain profile closely follows a two-temperature-model prediction including electron and thermal diffusion and frequency-dependent ultrasonic attenuation. Synthetic echoes test robustness to added noise. Broadband response thus replaces additional optical projections, making the measurement self-calibrating and enabling simultaneous recovery of an unknown physical field and the material properties that mediate its optical observation.

</details>

Abstract: In this study, an artificial intelligence-assisted evaluation of the refractive indices of different materials is performed. The evaluation of the refractive index is necessary to design photonic devices for various classical and quantum photonic applications, and to theoretically analyse experimental results. The refractive index is one of the crucial parameters that affect the interaction of light with matter. Although the refractive index can be measured experimentally, its estimation via computational means provide the advantage of not requiring costly instrumentation, trained researchers and could avoid potential laboratory-based uncertainties. Therefore, in this study, artificial intelligence is implemented to estimate the optical constants of two types of materials, i.e., noble metals, and semiconductors. To this end, an artificial neural network is used to estimate the real and the imaginary parts of the refractive index of a specific material for various wavelengths of light. Then, the knowledge of the trained neural network is employed to initiate and train new neural networks to estimate the refractive indices of other materials. For the training of a neural network, various methods can be deployed. However, the application of the training can be a time-consuming process. A transfer learning approach is here implemented to provide a faster training solution for the problem under study. The implementation of transfer learning allows training the neural networks for a new material with higher speed or accuracy when compared to non-knowledge transfer-based training. Our results show the efficiency of the proposed technique in accurately evaluating the real and imaginary parts of the refractive index of different materials.

</details>

Although free-space and fibre-optic isolators are mature, no broadly adopted on-chip technology yet combines strong isolation, low insertion loss, broad bandwidth, compact footprint, and scalable fabrication. The difficulty is twofold: Lorentz reciprocity must be broken, and every relevant backward spatial, spectral, and polarization channel must be suppressed. This Review develops a common framework for assessing proposed solutions. We first distinguish pairwise nonreciprocity from intensity-asymmetric transmission and from true isolation using the constitutive relations and scattering matrix. We then compare three physical routes to an integrated nonreciprocal response: magneto-optical bias, externally driven space-time modulation, and nonlinear optical processes. Topological and non-Hermitian systems are treated as cross-cutting design frameworks that can route, protect, or enhance an underlying nonreciprocal response, but do not by themselves break reciprocity. Finally, we examine fundamental constraints and practical trade-offs among isolation, insertion loss, bandwidth, footprint, modal coverage, drive requirements, and fabrication compatibility. Abstract: Traditional optical system design for imaging applications has long been guided by image quality metrics such as geometrical RMS spot size, wavefront error, and modulation transfer function (MTF). However, direct experimental demonstrations of topology-driven effects, particularly in nanostructures, are rarely proven. In this work, we experimentally investigate multilayer photonic nanostructures composed of alternating subwavelength dielectric layers, arranged according to the topological Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model. By engineering different edge truncations, we probe different supported topological phases. Our theoretical predictions and experimental measurements reveal strong field confinement at the surface exclusively in the configuration supporting topological edge states, providing their localized character and topological origin. First, we demonstrate enhanced absorption in an ultrathin absorbing overlayer deposited on the surface when the topological surface mode is present, highlighting the potential for surface-sensing applications. Second, we observe enhanced second-harmonic generation in reflection from the surface, demonstrating the potential of topological states for surface enhanced nonlinear optics enabled by the strong localization of the electromagnetic field at the interface.

Abstract: Coloring building-integrated photovoltaics poses a dilemma: absorptive pigments cost photocurrent, while ordered photonic structures are iridescent. A non-absorbing scatterer needs a high index with visible transparency, which the Moss rule caps near 2.35. Coupling Mie scattering, the Percus-Yevick structure factor, the exact phase function, and two-flux transfer with absorption and refraction at the front glass, we fix four target colors that a crowd-sourced naming survey calls unambiguously blue, green, gold, and terracotta, and map the recipes reaching them across four metal oxides, four encapsulants, two cell technologies, and wide ranges of particle size, packing fraction, and layer number. All four are reached in a laminated three-layer coating, to within DeltaE00 of 0.8 to 2.5, but not from one oxide: transparent TiO2 renders the blue and only pale warm tones; the green, gold, and terracotta require the blue-band absorption of V2O5, and the low-contrast oxides turn grey at the warm end. The encapsulant governs the color, and the cell technology does not. Disorder does not confer angle independence: the colors hold for an observer moving under a fixed sun but drift by DeltaE00 of 5 to 18 over the day, terracotta least, and a facade lit from above and viewed from below shifts every color towards the blue. Oxide, layer number, and facade geometry must therefore be designed together.

Abstract: The nuclear spin of a phosphorus atom in silicon is considered as a promising quantum information carrier for quantum computing due to its long coherence time. Coherent control of the nuclear spin is typically realized using nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR). In this work, we theoretically propose that with the aid of hyperfine interactions and magnetic field gradients, fast electric-dipole-induced nuclear electric resonance (EDNER) can be achieved. The stronger EDNER (compared with NMR) arises from the combined effects of hyperfine interaction between the nuclear and electron spins and coupling between the electron spin and charge degrees of freedom mediated by magnetic field gradients in this scheme. By operating at specific working points, the nuclear-spin splitting becomes less sensitive to electrical noise, enabling long coherence times and high-fidelity quantum operations. All elctrical, addressable control of nuclear spins paves the way for scalable silicon-based quantum computing.

Abstract: In electronic devices, the quality of the dielectric material is determined by figures of merit such as the band gap, leakage onset field, breakdown field and the number of built-in defects, which can all limit device performance. An additional and important metric to consider is the strength of the dielectric constant. Hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) is the benchmark gate dielectric for two-dimensional van der Waals heterostructures, owing to its wide bandgap, and atomically flat interface. At the same time, its dielectric constant (2--4) highlights the opportunity to explore alternative higher-permittivity van der Waals dielectrics that provide stronger electrostatic control. Previous work established lanthanum oxybromide (LaOBr), a layered dielectric with a predicted dielectric constant of 12.5, as a potential dielectric for van der Waals heterostructures. Using graphene field-effect devices with LaOBr, we show that the leakage-onset and breakdown fields decrease systematically with thickness. Dual-gate capacitance measurements combined with a dead-layer model reveal a bulk dielectric constant of $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{bulk}} = 12.2$, in agreement with theory, while interfacial dead-layers cause the effective dielectric constant to vary from 6 to 11 across multiple thicknesses. Benchmarked against established gate dielectrics, LaOBr combines breakdown fields comparable to hBN with dielectric constants 2 to 3 times larger. By establishing the operating regime of devices with various thicknesses, we further confirm the advantage of LaOBr over hBN for high-capacitance gate-dielectric operation. These results validate LaOBr as a promising dielectric for integration into two-dimensional heterostructures, and establish thickness-dependent benchmarking as essential for evaluating emerging layered dielectric materials.

Abstract: Quantum geometric quantities, such as Berry curvature (BC) and quantum metric, are known to govern the physical and topological properties of materials, making access to these quantities critically important for the comprehensive understanding of the materials. Chiral crystals offer a natural platform in which structural handedness can control quantum geometry and nonlinear transport, but direct experimental access to the quantum geometric quantities has remained elusive. Here, we show that trigonal tellurium (Te) hosts an enantiomer-dependent Berry curvature dipole (BCD) that can be accessed through the measurement of cross-axial nonlinear Hall effect (NHE), unlike previously studied in-plane configurations where the BCD contribution vanishes. Angle-resolved measurements on left- and right-handed Te devices reveal a second-order transverse response whose sign reverses with crystal chirality. The extracted cross-axial nonlinear susceptibility, $χ_{yxz}$, exceeds the in-plane susceptibility, $χ_{xxx}$, by more than two-folds, indicating a strong BCD-driven contribution. First-principles calculations attribute this behavior to a topologically nontrivial hedgehog-like BC texture near the H points and predict a BCD that reverses sign between opposite enantiomers, consistent with experiment. These results establish cross-axial nonlinear Hall transport as a symmetry-selective probe of chirality-controlled quantum geometry in tellurium and related chiral materials.

Abstract: Indirect excitons (IXs) in a periodic moiré potential in a van der Waals heterostructure form an experimental platform for exploring Bose-Hubbard physics, including the superfluid state and the Mott insulator state. This high-mobility transport demonstrates that disorder-induced localization is negligible. We found a remarkable suppression of this efficient IX transport, directly indicating the Mott insulator. As the IX density increases during the excitation pulse, the IX cloud initially expands and then contracts upon entering the Mott insulator phase. The emergence of IX cloud contraction is accompanied by a peak in the IX spatial distribution at the origin. Method: 从自旋波的能量输运出发进行分析。论证核心：即便基态不稳定，任何携带能量的波在被动界面上的反射率都不可能大于1，否则违反能量守恒。据此对文献中透射自旋波的符号设定进行审查。

Conclusion: 磁振子Klein悖论并非真实物理效应，而是透射波符号错误造成的假象；能量守恒原则排除了任何大于1的反射率。

Abstract: By considering the energy transport of spin wave, we show that, like the electron Klein paradox, there is no magnon Klein paradox for reflectivity. Heuristically, this can be understood according to the fact that, even if the ground state is unstable, any energy-carrying wave cannot have greater-than-one reflectivity off a passive interface, which will violate the conservation of energy. The magnon Klein paradox discussed in the literature is derived from assigning a wrong sign to the transmitted spin wave.

</details>


### [49] [Valley-resolved high harmonics probe symmetry breaking beyond the Dirac cone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15251)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯低能电子遵循涌现的、旋转不变的狄拉克方程，蜂窝晶格只通过以三角翘曲为首的高阶对称性破缺项体现。这为量子引力中的核心难题——如何在涌现的红外相对论连续谱之上探测被基本标度压低的高能（紫外）对称性破缺颗粒性修正——提供了独特的固态实验平台。

Conclusion: 谷分辨谐波比残余谷布居灵敏得多，可作为探测隐藏于涌现狄拉克理论之下的晶格尺度物理的实用探测器。

Abstract: Low-energy electrons in graphene obey an emergent, rotationally invariant Dirac equation, while the honeycomb lattice enters only through higher-order, symmetry-breaking corrections, the leading one being trigonal warping. This provides a unique solid-state arena to address a cornerstone challenge in quantum gravity: how to detect ultraviolet symmetry-breaking granular corrections, suppressed by a fundamental scale, atop an emergent infrared relativistic continuum. We show that intense counter-rotating $ω$-$2ω$ fields and valley-resolved high-harmonic generation (HHG) provide such a probe. Within a family of models that interpolates continuously between the isotropic Dirac cone and nearest-neighbour graphene at fixed Fermi velocity, the valley contrast of the harmonic yield vanishes identically in the symmetric theory, grows linearly with the warping coupling, and its valley-even counterpart appears only at second order, as expected from effective-field-theory power counting. The stronger the laser, the farther it drives electrons from the Dirac points, so the laser intensity plays the role of the collision energy. For the fourth harmonic, the leading warping correction alone accounts for the valley contrast of real graphene up to fundamental intensities of about $10^{11}$ W/cm$^2$, below the damage threshold of monolayer graphene, whereas the fifth harmonic already reveals higher-order lattice corrections at intensities around $10^{10}$ W/cm$^2$. The two harmonics, which rotate in opposite directions, are emitted by opposite valleys. Valley-resolved harmonics are far more sensitive than the residual valley populations, making them a practical detector of lattice-scale physics hidden beneath an emergent Dirac theory.

This naturally raises the question of whether quantization can extend beyond rational numbers to an irrational value. Here we show that such a quantized response can emerge from the limiting behavior of a finite, experimentally realizable geometric network. We develop a method based on infinite geometric structures, in which a sequence of finite networks rapidly converges toward an infinite limit. Following this approach, we identify a planar network whose normalized resistance converges to the irrational value $\sqrt{3}$, and realize a finite approximation with high-precision geometric control. The measured normalized resistance is $1.735 \pm 0.001\, h/e^2$ for a fully occupied Landau level in the designed pattern. Our experimental results extend the concept of quantization beyond rational values and demonstrate that an irrational quantized response can be approached through a finite physical system.

Abstract: Short-wavelength spin waves are central to miniaturizing magnonic devices and to studying exchange-dominated magnetization dynamics. Yet the wave-vector range between roughly 30 and 300 radians per micrometre has remained difficult to access experimentally, posing challenges for both excitation and detection. This Perspective reviews recent advances in emerging measurement techniques and discusses prospects for their future development. Optical and microwave techniques are restricted to the vicinity of the Brillouin-zone centre. Inelastic neutron, electron and resonant hard x-ray scattering cover the full Brillouin zone but lack the energy resolution needed for low-frequency magnons at intermediate wave vectors. This Perspective examines five emerging approaches that are beginning to close this gap: Mie-enhanced Brillouin light scattering, colour-centre magnetometry, magnon momentum microscopy, extreme-ultraviolet transient-grating spectroscopy, and time-resolved scanning transmission x-ray microscopy. For each, we discuss the physical principle, demonstrated capabilities, current limitations and prospects for development. Compared side by side, the techniques give complementary access to magnon frequencies, local stray fields, real-space dynamics and momentum-space distributions, and they differ in their sensitivity to coherent and incoherent excitations. We therefore discuss the main advantages and use cases of each technique and consider the practical requirements for addressing different scientific questions. We highlight opportunities for further development, including higher sensitivity, improved spatial and temporal resolution, and broader frequency and wave-vector coverage. Together, these advances could enable, for example, systematic studies of nonlinear magnon interactions at nanoscale wavelengths and investigations of emerging magnetic materials such as altermagnets.

Abstract: Flat bands amplify interactions into correlated phases, and topological bound states are robust by principle; combining the two in a model that is both exactly analyzable and experimentally accessible is therefore especially desirable. Here we show that a square-lattice model with a slowly modulated Dirac mass texture, $m(x)=m+m_0\cos(2πx/L)$ with $L$ up to hundreds of sites, provides such a setting, without engineered geometries, lattice-scale textures, or long-range hoppings. Exact nesting reduces the problem, independently at each transverse momentum $k_y$, to a $1+1$ dimensional Dirac Hamiltonian with a position-dependent mass: every simple zero of $m(x)$ binds Jackiw-Rebbi zero modes, and the two walls of a period form an effective Su-Schrieffer-Heeger ring. The model interpolates between a chirally pinned flat band at large $L$, a helical miniband whose nodes are enforced by a boundary phase of the ring, and a bias-dimerized regime in which the sign of the bias sets a winding number. A central result is that the miniband bandwidth is not a single scale: a max-min measurement conflates three widths of different origin, the helical height $W_{\rm JR}$ exponential in the period, the zone-edge upturn $W_{\rm edge}\propto L^{-2/3}$ dominating at large $L$, and a bias bump exponential in $L$, so the flat band is robust against bias in the thermodynamic limit. We analyze two lattice realizations, a two-orbital model with modulated inter-orbital hybridization and a single-orbital bond-order texture sharing the nodal physics while leaving the van Hove points gapless, and propose experiments on cold-atom superlattices, Kekule-ordered graphene, STM designer lattices, and microwave arrays.

Abstract: In this paper, we derive the spin polarization and the spin Chern number in momentum and real spaces in terms of the Wilson loop in each spin sector. For these derivations, the spin matrix in the subspace of occupied or unoccupied states is generalized by the unitary rotation to prevent the spectral gap from closing. In the presence of a finite spin gap, the spin eigenvectors recompose the eigenstates of the Hamiltonian in two spin sectors. A dimensional reduction leads to the charge polarization in each spin sector in terms of the Wilson loop along the effective Brillouin zone (BZ) in one dimension (1D). A linear combination of the charge polarizations defines the spin polarization, which naturally quantifies the spin Chern number in momentum space. It is also shown that the spin Chern number is directly derived from the local Wilson loops on a discrete 2D BZ. The theoretical treatment is further generalized for real space in a supercell framework, which can deal with ordered and disordered systems in a unified manner. In this framework, the spin Chern number in real space is formulated in terms of the Wilson loop around the BZ boundary. These formulations are used to numerically evaluate topological phases in the Bernevig-Hughes-Zhang model. Numerical results show an equivalence of the spin Chern number and the ${\mathbb{Z}_2}$ index.

</details>


### [57] [Symmetry Condition and Mechanism for the Anomalous Hall Effect in Odd-Parity Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16247)
*Xun-Jiang Luo,Su-Qi Zhang,Yi-Dong Liu,Yong-Ting Shi,Ding-Fu Shao,Mingliang Tian*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立了奇宇称磁体（OPM）中反常霍尔效应（AHE）的对称性条件与微观机制：AHE仅当磁点群为铁磁型时才被允许，且OMP固有的隐藏塞曼场（HZF）与自旋轨道耦合（SOC）的协同作用可在无外场时产生AHE。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在候选p波磁体Gd$_3$(Ru$_{1-δ}$Rh$_δ$)$_4$Al$_{12}$中观测到巨AHE，但既有理论解释诉诸自发磁化破坏时间反演对称性（$\mathcal{T}$），而磁序本身已破坏$\mathcal{T}$，因此OPM中AHE的微观起源悬而未决。

Method: 从对称性出发，比较OPM序参量的自旋群分类与AHE所遵从的磁群，推导AHE允许/禁止的判据；并构造方格子、蜂窝与kagome晶格模型（含亚晶格分辨SOC的方格子螺旋磁体）来验证机制。

Result: 证明AHE仅在磁点群为铁磁型时允许，而在有效时间反演$\mathcal{T}τ$（$τ$为分数平移）保持时被禁止；OPM普遍存在源于内禀$\mathcal{T}$破缺的HZF，其与SOC耦合无需外场即可产生AHE。方格子螺旋磁体模型精确映射为各向异性Rashba–Zeeman模型，其中Zeeman项即HZF并直接导致AHE。

Abstract: The anomalous Hall effect (AHE) in unconventional magnets has attracted considerable attention. A giant AHE has recently been observed in the candidate $p$-wave magnet Gd$_3$(Ru$_{1-δ}$Rh$_δ$)$_4$Al$_{12}$ [Nature 646, 837(2025)], while its theoretical interpretation invokes a spontaneous magnetization to break time-reversal symmetry ($\mathcal{T}$), although the magnetic order itself already breaks $\mathcal{T}$. Consequently, the microscopic origin of the AHE in odd-parity magnets (OPMs) remains an open question. Here we establish the symmetry condition and microscopic mechanism for the AHE in OPMs. Since the OPM order is classified by the spin group whereas the AHE is governed by the magnetic group, the AHE is allowed only when the magnetic point group is ferromagnetic; conversely, it is forbidden whenever the effective time reversal $\mathcal{T}τ$ (with $τ$ a fractional translation), which is essential to the realization of the OPM, is preserved. Microscopically, OPMs generically host a hidden Zeeman field (HZF) rooted in this intrinsic $\mathcal{T}$ breaking, and its interplay with spin-orbit coupling (SOC) generates the AHE without any external field. We demonstrate this picture with models on the square, honeycomb, and kagome lattices. In particular, a square-lattice helimagnet with sublattice-resolved SOC maps exactly onto an anisotropic Rashba--Zeeman model, whose Zeeman term is nothing but the HZF and directly yields the AHE. Our results establish the symmetry condition for the AHE in OPMs and identify the HZF--SOC interplay as a general route to realize it.

</details>

*Mauricio Gomez Viloria,Florian Herz,Riccardo Messina,Philippe Ben-Abdallah*

We first show that a static mass $m_z$ opens a bulk gap and drives the system into a Chern insulator with Chern number $C^{\rm per}=\pm 2\,{\rm sgn}(m_z)$. In comparison, TFOPL alone only shifts the positions of Dirac points and cannot open a full gap. Nevertheless, it produces a Berry curvature odd in the $x$-direction, yielding a finite Berry curvature dipole (BCD) along $x$ proportional to the sine of the initial phase, while the $y$-component vanishes. The hybrid scenario, by contrast, exhibits a remarkably rich phenomenology. Specifically, its gap-closure criterion becomes cubic and thus admits up to three real roots when the effective parameter along the $x$-direction is negative, thereby producing multiple light-tunable gap-closing points and a series of topological phase transitions. In particular, the BCD exhibits a double-peak structure with strong enhancement near gap-closing manifolds and synchronized sign reversal of the full vector under external tuning. We further analyze the anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC), anomalous Nernst conductivity (ANC), and anomalous thermal Hall conductivity (ATHC). The AHC and ATHC exhibit quantized plateaus that serve as signatures of Chern-insulator phases, while the ANC, being a Fermi-surface probe, provides complementary local information and can identify light-induced gaps even when global topological invariants remain unchanged. Taken together, these results offer useful insights into the topological properties of 2D type-II SDSMs and related systems.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 耗散稀释会降低1/f噪声下限；谐振传感器的最长有用积分时间由其预应力、体积、温度及本征损耗角共同决定。该工作将1/f频率噪声下限归因于张力热噪声，建立了无自由参数的理论关系，并界定了结构阻尼假设的低温适用边界。

Abstract: The frequency noise of a resonant nanomechanical sensor is well understood down to its thermomechanical limit, but the longest useful averaging time is set by a flicker, or $1/f$, frequency-noise floor whose origin has remained unknown. We show that this floor is the thermal noise of the tension. By the fluctuation-dissipation theorem, the tension of a resonator with fixed ends fluctuates by an amount set by the loss angle of its material; for structural damping, these fluctuations produce $1/f$ frequency noise with a flicker coefficient $h_{-1}=E k_{\mathrm{B}} T/(2πVσ_0^2 Q_{\mathrm{el}})$, with $Q_{\mathrm{el}}$ the intrinsic loss under elongation, that contains no free parameter and is diluted by the tensile stress $σ_0$ in the same way as damping is in dissipation dilution. Silicon nitride strings and membranes from 30 to 340~nm thickness follow the predicted volume scaling, the predicted $σ_0^{-2}$ dilution across resonators and within stress-engineered strings, and the predicted thickness dependence, which places noise-active two-level systems in the volume and at the surfaces of the resonators, all at the absolute level set by the independently measured intrinsic loss. The same relation accounts for published data between 78 and 296~K; below 78~K, the loss measured at the resonance no longer represents the loss probed by the noise, marking the limit of the structural-damping assumption. Dissipation dilution therefore lowers the $1/f$ floor, and the longest useful integration time of a resonant sensor is set by its prestress, volume, temperature, and intrinsic loss angle.

Abstract: We reconstruct a geometry of thermodynamic coordinates from transport coefficients within a mesoscopic canonical model. Self-adjoint flux observables determine a compatible evolution generator containing symmetric and antisymmetric transport coefficients. When the Hermitian transport tensor is positive, an exact graded first-order factorization reproduces this generator on its scalar sector. Its commutators define a spectral distance whose metric depends only on the symmetric tensor. Gradients of the antisymmetric tensor can change the evolution while leaving the metric unchanged. The construction allows coordinate-dependent coefficients. In the constant Onsager regime, a Clifford representation gives the same distance and an algebra fixed by the transport matrix. Published transport data for a $\mathrm{Bi_2Te_3}$ film give a principal-distance ratio of approximately $2.12$ in normalized coordinates at $ZT\simeq0.68$. We also examine a separate quadratic entropy-production observable and distinguish its discrete spectrum from the spectrum of the evolution generator. The spinorial representation adds no physical spin degrees of freedom.

Abstract: We derive a distance between thermodynamic states from a positive Hermitian transport tensor within a mesoscopic canonical model. An exact first-order factorization of the evolution generator gives a commutator norm and the metric $G=2g^{-1}$, where $g$ denotes the symmetric transport tensor. Gradients of the antisymmetric tensor can change the evolution, but they leave this distance unchanged. The construction allows coordinate-dependent coefficients. For constant Onsager coefficients, a Clifford representation gives the same metric. In a two-channel thermoelectric system, the ratio of normalized principal distances is $\sqrt{1+ZT}+\sqrt{ZT}$. Transport coupling thus fixes the anisotropy of this metric. The construction requires neither an independent metric nor a physical spin variable.

Abstract: Structured light provides a powerful means of controlling matter while itself exhibiting nontrivial spatial topology. Here, we show that these spatial structures can be harnessed to locally engineer the topological properties of matter. In a paradigmatic example of a topological insulator with Dirac surface states driven by an optical skyrmion beam, we show that structured light spatially tailors the effective Hamiltonian, yielding position-dependent topological gaps and Dirac velocities. The resulting gap modulation can create topological and trivial regions separated by optically defined interfaces, without introducing material boundaries or chemical modifications. We further show that the geometry of these interfaces can be controlled through the spatial structure of the light, in particular its orbital angular momentum. Our results establish structured light as a route to dynamically pattern topological phases and open a broader perspective on the interplay between topology in light and matter, including the possibility of transferring topological characteristics between the two.

Abstract: A recent experiment reported coherent parity oscillations between minimal Kitaev chains, demonstrating a Majorana parity qubit (arXiv:2607.09511). Here we show how the exponential protection in the underlying chains can nevertheless be revealed through unprotected qubit operations. To this end, we develop an analytical theory of quasistatic dephasing for two coupled $N$-site Kitaev chains at the sweet spot. In Rabi experiments, protection can be resolved by adapting the operating point with chain length: depending on the coupling architecture, it appears as an exponentially increasing optimized number of coherent oscillations or as a longer coherence time. Ramsey experiments show an exponentially increasing coherence time, provided the chains can be sufficiently decoupled. Our analytical theory takes into account the non-Gaussian splitting statistics of the Kitaev chains, allowing us to quantitatively predict the scaling with chain length. Moreover, we determine how residual noise and imperfections limit the range of chain lengths over which this scaling can be observed. Our results show how qubit dynamics can be used to probe Majorana protection in experimentally realistic devices.

Abstract: Feynman's ratchet illustrates how work can be extracted from thermal fluctuations in the presence of two heat baths at different temperatures. Abstract: In this paper, we present a comprehensive theoretical study of the electronic states in gapped graphene in the presence of a charge impurity and a magnetic field. We focus on the impurity-induced modification of the Landau levels under the influence of an attractive or repulsive Coulomb potential. Since analytical solutions of the Dirac equation are not available in this case, the study is carried out using the Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin (WKB) approach. Special attention is paid to the screening of the Coulomb potential in the presence of metallic gates situated at some distances from graphene sheet, as is commonly the case in experiments. Using both analytical and numerical methods, we determine the energy spectrum as a function of the magnetic field strength and impurity charge for screened and unscreened Coulomb potentials. Our results reveal a significant dependence of the energy levels for sample-to-gate distances of less than 50 nm.

Abstract: Conventional theoretical descriptions of moiré superlattices, epitomized by the Bistritzer--MacDonald continuum model, are fundamentally constrained by low-energy Dirac cone approximations and first-shell interlayer coupling truncations. While successful for small-angle twisted bilayer graphene near charge neutrality, these frameworks fail to provide universal analytical solutions for strained lattices, high-energy band behaviors, or arbitrary commensurate geometries. Here, we establish a unified, approximation-free analytical formalism for generalized moiré systems based on three pillars: (i) a rigorous two-dimensional-to-one-dimensional supercell cyclic mapping, (ii) exact block-circulant diagonalization of intralayer Hamiltonians, and (iii) quasi-degenerate Löwdin perturbation theory centered on arbitrary energy degeneracy points across the full spectrum. Unlike traditional schemes anchored to the Dirac point, our approach enables systematic perturbative expansions around any band crossing, unifying the characterization of low-energy flat bands with high-energy degeneracies. We demonstrate that this framework perfectly recovers the Bistritzer--MacDonald model in the low-energy limit while extending its reach to heterostrained systems and high-energy regimes. By incorporating high-order interlayer coupling shells, we reveal an intrinsic scale self-similarity in moiré lattices and predict the emergence of higher-order magic angles and tunable flat bands driven by secondary degeneracies. This work eliminates the long-standing lack of universal analytical tools for diverse moiré platforms, providing a full-spectrum theoretical foundation for band engineering and the exploration of exotic correlated and topological states in van der Waals heterostructures.

</details>

Abstract: We present a Python-based program that combines maximally localized Wannier functions-based first-principles electronic structure with the nonequilibrium Green's function formalism to enable efficient simulations of scanning tunneling microscopy (STM). The workflow builds upon standard density functional theory packages to construct compact transport Hamiltonians that preserve the properties of realistic materials. Superconductivity is incorporated via an effective pairing potential within the Nambu-spinor formalism, while coupling to the STM tip and substrate electrodes is treated using the wide-band limit approximation. Within this formulation, transport quantities such as current-voltage characteristics and differential conductance are evaluated in a closed analytical form. This avoids explicit numerical energy integrations and enables rapid spectroscopic calculations over broad parameter spaces. The framework provides a unified and computationally efficient approach for modeling superconducting hybrid nanostructures with spin-orbit coupling, magnetic textures, and spin polarization. Representative applications in spin chains, two-dimensional and molecular magnets on a superconductor demonstrate its robust capabilities for both simple model systems and realistic ab initio molecular structures relevant to modern STM experiments.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索仅由振荡磁场驱动的内禀自旋流机制，拓展自旋量子几何（Zeeman量子度规与Berry曲率）在自旋输运中的应用，并为纯自旋流探测与低能耗自旋电子学提供新途径。

Conclusion: IGSC不仅提供了自旋流探测的可能，还开辟了用于高能效自旋电子学的纯自旋流通道；电流方向的纵向/横向可切换性由Rashba-Dresselhaus相对强度调控。

Abstract: We uncover an intrinsic gyrotropic spin current (IGSC) driven solely by an oscillating magnetic field, arising from the spin version of Zeeman quantum geometry. Using the density matrix formalism, we derive the IGSC in the low-frequency clean limit, in terms of the Zeeman spin quantum metric and the Zeeman spin Berry curvature. The intrinsic quality factor, however, is limited by surface losses at atomic thickness. Abstract: We propose a protocol to prepare and probe dark states in a single-molecule electron spin resonance based on atomic force microscopy (ESR-AFM), thus extending existing techniques developed to address Rabi oscillations. To this end, we simulate in analogy to atomic physics the coherent manipulation of a three-level system in the V-configuration using a multifrequency magnetic field. More specifically, we consider a neutral molecule with a zero-field-split (ZFS) first excited triplet and analyze within a pump-probe scheme the dynamics of the total triplet population. In the triplet subspace, the dark state is a coherent superposition formed by two ZFS states which does not evolve in time despite a continuous resonant driving towards the third one. We characterize the properties of this state comparing it to its fully incoherent counterpart. Within a density-matrix formalism for open systems, we then compute the expected dark state signatures both for ideal scenarios and for the realistic parameterization of ESR-AFM on pentacene.

Abstract: We establish theoretically that transverse spin textures are generically induced by the spin-orbit interaction at the Fermi surface of centrosymmetric ferromagnetic metals. However, a new {\em current-gradient-induced} torque emerges in nanoscale geometries. For a series of Ni-based bilayers, the computed Ni thickness dependence of the current-gradient-induced torque is in quantitative agreement with measurements previously attributed to orbital torques. Abstract: Anyons are particles with quantized fractional statistics. In theory, a single anyon can be stabilized as a localized fractional charge within the bulk of a topologically ordered two-dimensional insulator. However, experiments to date have focused on probing anyons in structures in which many anyonic excitations coexist and interact. Here, we demonstrate the trapping of a single anyon in a device consisting of a graphene-based quantum point contact with an embedded tunable quantum Hall dot. Benchmarking our device at v = 1, we demonstrate backscattering between Fermi liquid edge states mediated by the whole-electron Coulomb blockade of the dot. The cleanliness and control available in our device allow depletion of the dot to the last quasihole, and we observe spin blockade for transitions to the opposite-spin Landau level. At v = 1/3 and 2/3, we observe backscattering resonances with three times the frequency, consistent with resonant transmission of e/3 charges via a single anyon transistor. We demonstrate controlled depletion to the last quasiparticle to form a single anyon quantum dot. Tracing the charge addition spectrum for quasiparticle number Nqp = 1-5, we observe highly irregular addition energies, consistent with DMRG modeling showing a complex orbital shell-filling behavior. At higher Nqp = 10-20, the addition energies show a striking pattern: quasiholes at 1/3 (and quasiparticles at 2/3) show a regular addition spectrum while quasiparticles at 1/3 (and quasiholes at 2/3) do not. We trace the origin of this irregularity to an attractive component of the inter-anyon interaction: by experimentally tuning the confining energy, we observe complete suppression of alternating charge sectors, after which the dot charges in increments of twice the fundamental charge. This observation indicates the emergence of an anyon binding energy as large as the charging energy.

</details>

Abstract: Real-space interference of wave packets is governed by their central momentum separation and momentum width, which set the oscillation wavelength and spatial envelope, respectively. Here, we show that in crystalline systems, quantum metric distribution in an isolated band can similarly give rise to real-space interference through the evanescent tails of a localized bound state. The location and width of a quantum metric hot spot in the momentum space set a complex wavevector as one evanescent plane wave component of a bound state. Consequently, with multiple quantum metric hot spots, the density distribution of a bound state or the total density of two spatially overlapped bound states exhibits an interference pattern, where the oscillation period and long-range decay length are determined by the momentum-space separation of the hot spots and the width of the narrowest hot spots, respectively. This quantum-geometric interference is particularly pronounced in (nearly) flat bands, where competing scales from band dispersion are suppressed. Furthermore, we demonstrate that using a Josephson STM the real-space interference can be experimentally observed through the tip-position dependence of the Josephson critical current. Our results reveal a direct correspondence between momentum-space quantum geometry and real-space interference, showing how bound-state profiles encode the momentum-resolved quantum metric.

</details>

Abstract: Long-baseline optical links for space interferometry and satellite quantum networks rely on heterodyne phase tracking under Doppler motion and gravitational frequency shifts. These relativistic shifts can generate large detector-frame beat-frequency offsets over finite readout windows and thereby constrain the temporal precision of front-end phase readout. We develop a detector-frame framework for this problem using an effective two-mode signal--local-oscillator model in which relativistic motion rescales the local-oscillator frequency. Within this setting we derive closed-form Mandelstam--Tamm and Margolus--Levitin bounds for representative Gaussian resources and connect global state distinguishability with the operational precision of heterodyne phase tracking. In the bright-local-oscillator regime we identify a geometric--operational mismatch: the joint optical state can evolve on arbitrarily short global timescales while the relevant information rate is set by the measured detector record. Combining this result with an open-loop transient-acquisition analysis, we show that white phase noise and relativistic beat-frequency drift that is approximately constant over the acquisition window generate a cube-root scaling benchmark for the optimal pre-lock open-loop integration window and the minimum residual phase error. These results establish detector-frame benchmarks for quantum-limited heterodyne timing in relativistic optical links and clarify how global dynamical bounds enter task-restricted continuous optical measurements.
