Motivation: 量子机器学习（QML）是量子计算的重要应用方向，但何时以及如何让实际学习模型取得量子优势仍不清楚；即便在达到相同预测性能所需资源上获得多项式级改善也有重要意义。作者希望探究QViT是否表现出与经典模型类似的缩放行为，以及把量子比特数纳入数据规模与模型参数之外后该行为如何变化。

Method: 针对量子视觉Transformer（QViT）开展数值图像分类实验，并将验证损失拟合为关于量子比特数、Transformer深度和训练数据集规模的联合平移幂律模型；随后将该拟合结果用经典编码器参数预算来表达，以考察量子比特数与经典编码器参数之间的替代关系。

Result: 数值图像分类实验中的验证损失能够被量子比特数、Transformer深度与训练数据集规模的联合平移幂律模型很好地描述；以经典编码器参数预算表达该拟合后，识别出一个区间：在该区间内增加量子比特数可以在可比预测性能下补偿较少的经典编码器参数。

Conclusion: 该发现与先前关于量子学习模型参数效率的观察一致，并提示了探索QML中可能资源节约的方向。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning (QML) is a promising application of quantum computing, yet when and how practical learning models can achieve quantum advantage remains poorly understood. Even a polynomial improvement in the resources needed to attain a given predictive performance would be significant. In this work, we investigate empirical scaling laws of quantum vision transformers (QViTs), which connect QML to the transformer architecture underlying modern large language models. We ask whether QViTs exhibit scaling behavior analogous to classical models and how this behavior changes when qubit number is included alongside dataset size and model parameters. The validation losses in our numerical image-classification experiments are well described by a joint shifted power-law model in qubit number, transformer depth, and training dataset size. Expressing this fit in terms of the classical encoder parameter budget identifies a regime in which increasing the number of qubits can compensate for fewer classical encoder parameters at comparable predicted performance. This finding is consistent with earlier observations of parameter efficiency in quantum learning models and suggests a direction for exploring possible resource savings in QML.

</details>


### [83] [Parameter-Efficient Multilinear Readout for Quantum Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13974)
*Van Tien Nguyen,Panagiotis Markopoulos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出张量结构的多线性读出（ML-QRC），通过模态投影压缩量子储备池特征到隐藏层的参数量，在保持性能竞争力的同时显著减少参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量子储备池计算（QRC）流程将测量结果展平后训练线性读出，忽略了时间步、量子比特与观测量之间固有的张量结构，且稠密神经读出参数过多。

Method: 设计多线性读出 ML-QRC：以模态（mode-wise）投影对张量特征进行压缩，将特征到隐藏层的参数量从稠密隐藏宽度64的 $64TnF$ 降至 $d(T+n+F)+64d^3$。

Result: 在 $T=50$、$n=6$、$d=8$、$F=n+\binom{n}{2}$ 下实现 $12.1\times$ 特征块压缩；在 Mackey-Glass 预测上与 Ridge 统计上相当，在 TFIM 相分类中取得数值最高的全数据准确率，且参数量比全稠密 MLP 少 $12.0\times$；在 Mackey-Glass 低数据下稳定性优于神经 MLP 读出，但该优势未迁移至 TFIM（50% 数据下 Ridge 最优）。

Conclusion: ML-QRC 应被视为一种在保留 QRC 张量结构的同时压缩稠密 MLP 读出的张量结构化神经读出，而非在测试范围内替代简单线性/Ridge 读出。

Abstract: Quantum Reservoir Computing (QRC) uses fixed quantum dynamics as a high-dimensional feature map. Still, standard pipelines flatten reservoir measurements and train a linear readout, ignoring the natural tensor structure across time steps, qubits, and observables. We propose a multilinear readout for quantum reservoir computing (ML-QRC), a tensor-structured neural readout compressed through mode-wise projections. Relative to a dense hidden-width-64 neural readout, this reduces the feature-to-hidden parameter count from $64TnF$ to $d(T+n+F)+64d^3$, giving a $12.1\times$ feature-block compression at $T=50$, $n=6$, $d=8$, and $F=n+\binom{n}{2}$. Across Mackey-Glass forecasting and TFIM phase classification, ML-QRC remains statistically competitive with Ridge on Mackey-Glass and obtains the numerically highest full-data TFIM accuracy among tested methods, while using $12.0\times$ fewer parameters than the full dense MLP. Its clearest benefit is improved low-data stability relative to neural MLP readouts on Mackey-Glass; this advantage does not transfer to TFIM, where Ridge performs best at 50\% data. Overall, ML-QRC is best viewed as a tensor-structured neural readout that compresses dense MLP readouts while preserving QRC tensor structure, rather than as a replacement for simple linear/Ridge readouts in the tested regime.

</details>


### [84] [On the Fast Electrons-Optical Modes Interaction Problem Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13976)
*Svetlana Churochkina,Dmitry Churochkin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于量子信息方法，用保真度严格量化了自由电子狄拉克旋量近似 Ψ_k ≈ Ψ_{k0} 的误差，发现保真度不仅依赖波矢，还依赖自旋取向与波矢平面取向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有相对论量子光学方法中提出，在 |k−k0| ≪ |k0| 时用 Ψ_{k0} 替代 Ψ_k 引入的误差很小，但该假设缺乏严格论证。本文旨在严格检验这一近似的有效程度。

Method: 采用量子信息中的保真度（fidelity）概念，对非相互作用自由电子狄拉克旋量 Ψ_k 与 Ψ_{k0} 的一致性进行解析计算。

Result: 得到保真度的解析表达式，表明其不仅取决于波矢 k 与 k0，还取决于 [k0, k] 的相互取向以及 s†σs（s 为二分量自旋单位矢量，σ 为泡利矩阵矢量）。

Conclusion: 该结果可视为保真度中的类手征效应，约束了自旋自由度的任意性，并给出了在保真度意义下 k0、k−k0 与 s†σs 的最优几何配置。

Abstract: A recently developed relativistic quantum optics approach for a fast electron beam-optical modes interaction problem (Optica 6, 1524--1534 (2019)) contains suggestion about a smallness of the error induced by replacement $Ψ_{\vec k}\approxΨ_{\vec k_0}$ for noninteracting free electron Dirac spinors at such wave vector $\vec k$ that $\left|\vec k-\vec k_0 \right| \ll\left|\vec k_0\right|$ with $\vec k_0$ as a peaked wave vector of a focused electron beam. Here, based on quantum information approach we strictly investigated a degree of coincidence of spinors $Ψ_{\vec k}$ and $Ψ_{\vec k_0}$ by calculating fidelity for them. The analytical expression is obtained. It is shown that the fidelity inevitably depends on not only wave vectors $\vec k$ and $\vec k_0$ but also on mutual orientation of $[\vec k_0,\vec k]$ and $\hat{\vec{s}}^{\dagger}\vecσ\hat{\vec{s}}$ with $\vec{s}$ is a two-component spin unit vector and $\vecσ$ is a vector of Pauli matrices. This result can be conceived as a chirality-like effect in the fidelity and constraints spin arbitrariness when applying the developed relativistic quantum optics approach to the problems of interest. A geometrical configuration of $\vec k_0$, $\vec k-\vec k_0$, and $\hat{\vec{s}}^†\vecσ\hat{\vec{s}}$ that is optimal in terms of fidelity is established.

</details>


### [85] [Can the Quantum Fisher Effective Dimension Judge a Hybrid Quantum Neural Network?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14070)
*Md Masum Mia,Amith Khandakar,Mohamed Arselene Ayari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文检验量子Fisher信息矩阵有效维度实际含义：该标量刻画电路几何而非模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合量子神经网络中，有效维度常被当作可训练性或精度的指标，但这些解读缺乏受控验证，因此作者系统考察其真正度量对象。

Method: 将有效维度定义为QFIM对角线的参与率（取值介于1与参数数之间），在2个图像数据集、4个冻结骨干（含随机投影对照）、8量子比特上4种电路深度及3个随机种子下评估。

Result: 训练中有效维度远低于参数数；与测试精度相关性为-0.72至+0.87，且半数配置中随种子改变符号。深度增加激活更大比例电路方向（参数数的0.38→0.91），初始化梯度方差保持1e-5~1e-4，8量子比特未出现贫瘠高原。固定电路下4个骨干初始有效维度均为6~9，但CIFAR-10精度从23.14%到86.44%。

Conclusion: 有效维度反映电路几何而非模型质量；作者提出将各诊断对应到其可回答问题的实用协议。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum neural networks (QNNs) pair a classical feature extractor with a variational quantum circuit, and the effective dimension of the circuit's quantum Fisher information matrix (QFIM) is widely used to assess such models. This scalar is often interpreted as an indicator of trainability or accuracy, although these readings are rarely tested under controlled conditions. We therefore investigate what it actually measures. We define the effective dimension as the participation ratio of the QFIM diagonal, which lies between one and the parameter count, and we evaluate it across two image datasets, four frozen backbones including a random-projection control, four circuit depths on eight qubits, and three seeds. During training, the effective dimension remains well below the parameter count, and its correlation with test accuracy ranges from -0.72 to +0.87, changing sign across seeds in half of the configurations. Increasing depth activates a larger share of the circuit directions, from 0.38 to 0.91 of the parameter count, while the initialization gradient variance remains between 1e-5 and 1e-4, so no barren plateau (BP) appears at eight qubits. At a fixed circuit, the four backbones share an initial effective dimension of 6 to 9, even though CIFAR-10 accuracy ranges from 23.14% to 86.44%. These results show that the effective dimension characterizes circuit geometry rather than model quality. We conclude with a practical protocol that assigns each diagnostic to the question it can answer.

</details>


### [86] [Prelocality No-Go Theorems and Exactly Solvable Relational History Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14081)
*Maxim V. Churilov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨置换协变相互作用能否产生稀疏度量几何，证明全支撑 k-体轨道无法具有一致有限程，并构造两个可精确求解的有限关系历史哈密顿量以量化时钟与局域性的资源权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于检验：精确的置换协变性本身是否足以赋予系统稀疏度量几何。若不能，则需明确置换对称与度量几何之间的根本张力，并寻找能精确求解、阐明该局限的模型。

Method: 方法分两部分。其一为解析论证：对置换协变、全支撑的 k-体相互作用轨道，计算关联相互作用范数标度，并利用图的基对角化哈密顿量的基态简并分析，给出近似对称能量的定量版本及固定半径连通性与幺正等距障碍；同时以迹距离不对称性约束选择单一标记图的概率。其二为构造两个可精确求解的有限关系历史哈密顿量：循环时钟加可逆关联更新模型（经规范约化得到带秩一 pin 的循环拉普拉斯量，给出精确能隙公式），以及无序架构模型（每无序对一个时钟量子比特，给出对易四局域项）。

Result: 第一部分的结论性结果：全支撑 k-体轨道的相互作用范数标度为 binom(N-1,k-1)|J_N|‖h‖，故在直径发散的度量中不存在一致有限程；置换不变且在图基下对角的哈密顿量若有基态图 G，则基态简并度至少为 N!/|Aut(G)|。第二部分的精确结果：循环时钟模型的能隙为 Δ_{N,L}=E_*(1-cos θ_L)，其中 tan(Lθ_L/2)=1/sin θ_L，0<θ_L<π/L；渐近展开 Δ_{N,L}/E_*=π²/(2L²)-2π²/L³+O(L⁻⁴)，与顶点数无关。无序架构模型具 2^N 个基态和与尺寸无关的能隙 E_*(1-1/√2)；固定权重控制惩罚可选出 binom(N,q) 个基态且不降低能隙。

Conclusion: 精确有限结果表明时钟资源与局域性之间存在权衡，并揭示了置换协变性无法自行提供稀疏度量几何。但这些结果并未建立任何涌现几何相，因此不能据此推断系统具有 emergent geometric phase。

Abstract: Exact permutation covariance does not itself supply a sparse metric geometry. For permutation-covariant, full-support $k$-body interaction orbits, the incident interaction norm scales as $\binom{N-1}{k-1}|J_N|\|h\|$; such orbits cannot have uniformly finite range in metrics of diverging diameter. A permutation-invariant Hamiltonian diagonal in the graph basis has ground-state multiplicity at least $N!/|\mathrm{Aut}(G)|$ whenever $G$ is a ground graph. We give a quantitative version for approximately symmetric energies, together with fixed-radius connectivity and unitary-isometry obstructions. The trace-distance asymmetry of an arbitrary state also bounds its probability of selecting one labeled graph.
  We then construct two exactly solvable finite relational history Hamiltonians. A cyclic clock with a reversible incidence update has a unique ground state; gauge reduction yields a cycle Laplacian with a rank-one pin and the exact gap $Δ_{N,L}=E_* (1-\cosθ_L)$, where $\tan(Lθ_L/2)=1/\sinθ_L$ and $0<θ_L<π/L$. In particular, $Δ_{N,L}/E_*=π^2/(2L^2)-2π^2/L^3+O(L^{-4})$, independently of the number of vertices. In an alternative order-free architecture, one clock qubit per unordered pair gives commuting four-local terms, $2^N$ ground states and a size-independent gap $E_*(1-1/\sqrt2)$. A fixed-weight control penalty selects $\binom Nq$ ground states without reducing the gap. These exact finite results expose clock and locality resource tradeoffs; they do not establish an emergent geometric phase.

</details>


### [87] [How long is a photon?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14088)
*Sky Nelson-Isaacs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 光子的零固有时间、有限发射/吸收时长之间存在时间描述上的不兼容，该文借助递归傅里叶变换模型加以调和，并提出其可作为克莱因-戈尔登方程的改进形式或薛定谔方程的相对论兼容替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子沿零间隔传播、固有时间为零，而其发射与吸收过程有有限时长并决定光谱内容；三段时间属于同一物理实体却彼此不兼容，是量子力学与相对论衔接的困难所在。

Method: 采用先前提出的量子波函数传播模型，以对偶空间上的递归傅里叶变换来刻画光子的生命周期；模型不把空间视为依赖于时间，并以傅里叶变换为基础（其相位在狭义相对论中为不变量，也是不确定性原理的基础）。

Result: 该模型在概念上化解了零固有时间与有限发射/吸收时长之间的表面矛盾，被定位为促进量子力学与相对论相容的候选框架。

Conclusion: 该模型拟作为克莱因-戈尔登方程的改进版本，或作为薛定谔方程的相对论兼容替代方案。

Abstract: A photon has the strange property that it travels along a null interval with zero proper time, hence its emission and absorption points are, in a sense, a singular event. For their part, the emission and absorption processes have a finite duration which determines the spectral content of the photon. The question in the title points to the conceptual difficulty in `stitching' together these three intervals, which all correspond to the same physical entity but have fundamentally incompatible temporal descriptions. A previously developed model for quantum wavefunction propagation solves this apparent dilemma by formulating the life cycle of a photon in terms of recursive Fourier transforms on dual spaces. Because it doesn't treat space as dependent on time, and it is built on the Fourier transform whose phase is an invariant in special relativity as well as the foundation of the uncertainty principle, the model is a promising candidate framework for making quantum mechanics compatible with relativity. The model proposes to serve as an improved Klein-Gordon equation, or relativistically compatible replacement for Schrödinger's equation.

</details>


### [88] [Hamiltonian-Dependent Compressibility and Physical Preservation in Approximate Quantum Compilation using Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14125)
*Dohyun Kim,Namshik Han*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用张量网络近似量子编译（AQCtensor）研究80量子比特一维各向异性XYZ Heisenberg链的动力学压缩性，发现压缩性能随各向异性耦合与磁场方向系统性变化，并与二阶Trotter误差相关的嵌套对易子度量及目标态MPS键维密切相关；硬件实验中将10次重复演化压缩至5次，保真度0.995474，线路深度降低45.9%，双量子比特门数降低47.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似量子编译（AQC）通过张量网络有望减少量子模拟所需线路资源，但多体动力学的可压缩性由何因素决定，以及压缩后线路在量子硬件上能保留多少物理相关信息，目前仍缺乏清晰认识。

Method: 采用AQCtensor方法，研究对象为初态为Néel态的80量子比特一维各向异性XYZ Heisenberg链；通过改变交换各向异性与外加磁场，考察压缩性能的变化，并将其与二阶Trotter误差相关的嵌套对易子度量及目标态MPS键维进行关联分析；同时在80量子比特量子硬件上开展实验评估，对10次重复的目标演化进行压缩。

Result: 压缩性能随各向异性耦合构型与磁场方向系统性变化，且与嵌套对易子度量和目标态MPS键维呈现方向性趋势；硬件实验中AQCtensor将10次重复演化压缩为5次，目标态保真度保持0.995474，线路总深度降低45.9%，双量子比特门数降低47.6%；压缩线路在磁化强度、最近邻关联和哈密顿量期望值测量上表现相当，过程级诊断与局部约化态层析提供了补充表征。

Conclusion: AQCtensor的可压缩性与哈密顿量对易子结构及目标态复杂度相关联，并在80量子比特量子硬件实验中实现了显著的线路资源缩减，同时保留了物理相关特征。

Abstract: Approximate quantum compilation (AQC) using tensor networks provides a promising route to reducing the circuit resources required for quantum simulation. However, the factors governing the compressibility of many-body dynamics and the extent to which compressed circuits retain physically relevant information on quantum hardware remain poorly understood. We investigate these questions using AQCtensor for an 80-qubit one-dimensional anisotropic XYZ Heisenberg chain initialized in a Néel state. By varying the exchange anisotropy and external magnetic field, we find that compression performance changes systematically with the anisotropic coupling configuration and field orientation. These variations are strongly associated with a nested-commutator measure related to second-order Trotter error, with corresponding directional trends also reflected in the target-state MPS bond dimension. For an 80-qubit quantum-hardware evaluation, AQCtensor compresses a 10-repetition target evolution to 5 repetitions while retaining a target-state fidelity of 0.995474, reducing the total circuit depth by 45.9% and the two-qubit gate count by 47.6%. The compressed circuit exhibits comparable behavior across magnetization, nearest-neighbor correlation, and Hamiltonian expectation measurements, while process-level diagnostics and local reduced-state tomography provide complementary characterization beyond these observables. Together, these results link AQCtensor compressibility to Hamiltonian commutator structure and target-state complexity and demonstrate substantial circuit reduction while retaining physically relevant features in an 80-qubit quantum-hardware experiment.

</details>


### [89] [Compass in the Mirror: Quantum Backpropagation with the Hopf Ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14177)
*Ruge Lin,Ranyiliu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于Hopf拟设的变分量子态完整梯度估计方案，利用共享有限次测量记录实现全局与检查点两类梯度块获取，测量次数仅需对数级增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子态含大量控制角，完整梯度需要对每个角度求导。如何在有限测量次数下高效获取完整梯度向量，避免逐项重复测量，是本文动机。

Method: 采用Hopf拟设：通过嵌套双向幅度分裂（复态另有叶相位）构建任意归一化纯态，每个分裂或相位对应一个可编程角度。借助相位校准的受控反射，逆Hopf框架将归一化幅度方向映射为计算标签，使每个全-X结果贡献于所有幅度导数；复相位块由独立带符号叶记录提供。检查点变体仅反演选定深度以下的后续层，返回该层梯度块。

Result: 对含M个实参数的完整图，在固定加性坐标精度与置信度下，全局方法所需执行计数为O(log log M)，完整检查点调度为O(log M log log M)。全局节省来自记录复用而非输出规模缩减。指定直接角度调度下，全局方法的Hopf CNOT工作量及含输入输出存储相对匹配的标量评估至多具对数级开销。

Conclusion: 同一Hopf几何可前向承载量子态、后向传递目标的完整坐标响应，实现高效共享式梯度估计。

Abstract: A variational quantum state may have many control angles, and its complete gradient contains one derivative for each. The task is to turn access to selected derivatives into a shared finite-shot record for the complete vector. The Hopf ansatz supplies the setting: it constructs any normalized pure state from nested two-way magnitude splits and, for complex states, leaf phases, with one directly programmed angle per split or phase. Earlier Hopf constructions prepare the state, recover coordinates from known amplitudes, and provide indexed derivative access. With a phase-calibrated controlled reflection, the inverse Hopf frame maps each normalized magnitude direction to a computational label, so every all-$X$ outcome contributes to every magnitude derivative. A separate signed leaf record supplies the complex phase block. A checkpoint variant reverses only the suffix below a selected depth and returns that layer's gradient block. For a complete chart with $M$ real-valued parameters, sufficient execution counts are $\mathcal{O}(\log\log M)$ for the global method and $\mathcal{O}(\log M\log\log M)$ for the complete checkpoint schedule at fixed additive coordinate accuracy and confidence. The global saving is record reuse, not a reduction of the $M$-entry output. Under the designated direct-angle schedules, the global method's assigned Hopf CNOT work and input/output-inclusive storage have at most logarithmic overhead relative to a matched scalar evaluation. The same Hopf geometry thus carries a state forward and the objective's complete coordinate response backward.

</details>


### [90] [Lifting an $[n,k,d]$ Binary Linear Code to an $[[n,k,d]]$ Quantum Stabilizer Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14192)
*Thamadathil Aswanth,Navin Kashyap*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究经典二进制线性码 $[n,k,d]$ 在CWS框架下能否提升为参数相同的量子稳定子码 $[[n,k,d]]$，并给出概率下界与Gilbert-Varshamov型存在性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CWS框架可从图与经典码系统构造量子纠错码；当经典码为线性时所得为稳定子码。但经典线性码在CWS构造中保持最小距离 $d$ 的条件尚不清晰，需要明确哪些 $[n,k,d]$ 经典码可提升为 $[[n,k,d]]$ 量子码。

Method: 针对给定 $[n,k,d]$ 二进制线性码 $\mathcal{C}$ 与Erdős-Rényi随机图 $\mathrm{G}(n,\frac12)$ 构造随机CWS码，进行概率分析，推导所得量子码保持经典码最小距离 $d$ 的概率下界。

Result: 建立了随机CWS码保持最小距离 $d$ 的概率下界，并由此得到关于经典码参数 $n,k,d$ 的Gilbert-Varshamov型条件，保证存在某个图使构造出的量子码具有相同的 $n,k,d$。

Conclusion: 经典线性码经由CWS框架提升为同参数量子稳定子码并非普遍成立，但在满足Gilbert-Varshamov型条件时存在这样的图，从而给出 $[[n,k,d]]$ 稳定子码的存在性判据。

Abstract: The codeword stabilized (CWS) framework provides a systematic method for constructing quantum error correcting codes from a simple undirected graph and a classical code. When the underlying classical code is linear, the resulting quantum code is a stabilizer code. In this work, we investigate the conditions under which an arbitrary $[n,k,d]$ classical binary linear code can be lifted to an $[[n,k,d]]$ quantum stabilizer code using the CWS framework. We analyze the random CWS code constructed from a given $[n,k,d]$ binary linear code $\mathcal{C}$ and an Erdős-Rényi random graph $\mathrm{G}(n, \frac12)$. We establish a lower bound on the probability that the resulting quantum code preserves the minimum distance $d$ of the classical code. This probabilistic analysis yields a Gilbert-Varshamov-type condition on the $n,k,d$ parameters of the classical code that guarantees the existence of a graph resulting in a quantum code with the same $n,k,d$ parameters.

</details>


### [91] [Below-threshold execution of fault-tolerant ripple-carry addition on a trapped-ion quantum computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14202)
*Jacob S. Nelson,Andrew J. Landahl,Andrew D. Baczewski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在Quantinuum Helios-1离子阱量子计算机上实现了基于[[8,3,2]]和[[4,2,2]]纠错码的通用容错量子计算架构，并执行了多达5比特的ripple-carry加法器电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证容错量子计算架构在实际硬件上的可行性，通过纠错码与算法校验相结合的方式降低逻辑错误率，并探索其相对于未编码电路的性能提升。

Method: 采用误差检测[[8,3,2]]和[[4,2,2]]码构建通用FTQC架构；编译并执行经典输入的ripple-carry加法器电路（最多5比特）；利用workspace qubit的uncomputing作为算法校验，将有效故障距离从2提升至4；结合突发错误过滤进行错误分析。

Result: 1比特加法器在突发错误过滤后仅观测到1次逻辑失败，估计逻辑失败率约为(6.0±5.9)×10⁻⁵，远低于同一硬件未编码形式的(3.5±1.3)×10⁻³；2比特和3比特加法器也表现出性能提升；4比特和5比特加法器运行在略高于阈值水平。

Conclusion: 该容错架构在小规模加法器上显著优于未编码电路，证明了算法校验与纠错码协同提升有效故障距离的有效性；但随电路规模增大，性能接近阈值，表明需进一步优化以扩展至更大规模计算。

Abstract: We deploy a universal fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) architecture based on error-detecting $[\![8,3,2]\!]$ and $[\![4,2,2]\!]$ codes on Quantinuum's Helios-1 trapped-ion quantum computer. We compile and execute ripple-carry adder (RCA) circuits for classical inputs up to five bits on this architecture. Uncomputing a workspace qubit provides an additional ``algorithmic check'' to detect errors. While available at both the physical and logical levels, this check synergizes with our architecture by increasing the effective fault distance of many circuit locations from two to four. Combining this algorithmic check with the error-detecting codes, we observed only a single logical failure in one-bit addition after burst-error filtering, yielding an estimated logical failure rate of $(6.0 \pm 5.9) \times 10^{-5}$. This is well below the $(3.5 \pm 1.3) \times 10^{-3}$ failure probability observed for one-bit addition on the same hardware in unencoded form. Two- and three-bit adders also showed performance improvements, while the longer four- and five-bit adders executed just above threshold.

</details>


### [92] [Quantum Signal Processing Phase Factorization via Logarithmic Derivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14259)
*Christopher Peng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种改进的量子信号处理(QSP)相位因子稳定分解方法，用对数导数替代Prony方法，复杂度为O(d log²d)，数值误差达10^-12量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QSP需要高效计算与多项式变换相关的相位因子。现有稳定分解方法(arXiv:2202.02671)避免直接求根，但依赖Prony方法从根的加权幂和恢复多项式，流程较复杂。

Method: 利用相关多项式的对数导数替代Prony方法。由于对数导数在计入重数后于每个根处具有相同留数，其傅里叶系数可直接给出单位圆内根的非加权幂和；再用Newton-Girard恒等式在无需计算根的情况下恢复多项式系数。

Result: 算法计算复杂度为O(d log²d)，简化了原方法的分解流程。在哈密顿量模拟、本征态过滤、矩阵求逆及Fermi-Dirac算子等数值实验中，数秒内即可完成相位因子计算，最大误差约为10^-12。

Conclusion: 该改进方法在保持稳定性的同时简化了QSP相位因子的计算流程，并具备良好的复杂度与数值精度。

Abstract: Quantum signal processing (QSP) requires the efficient computation of phase factors associated with a polynomial transformation. One existing stable factorization method avoids direct root finding but relies on Prony's method to recover a polynomial from weighted power sums of its roots (arXiv:2202.02671). We present a modification of this approach that eliminates Prony's method by using the logarithmic derivative of an associated polynomial. Because the logarithmic derivative has the same residue at every root after accounting for multiplicity, its Fourier coefficients directly yield the unweighted power sums of the roots inside the unit disk. The Newton--Girard identities then recover the coefficients of the corresponding polynomial without computing its roots. The resulting algorithm has $O(d\log^2 d)$ computational complexity and simplifies the factorization procedure of the original method. In numerical experiments on Hamiltonian simulation, eigenstate filtering, matrix inversion, and the Fermi--Dirac operator, our implementation computes phase factors in seconds with maximum errors on the order of $10^{-12}$.

</details>


### [93] [The GHZ Game in Black and White](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14411)
*Terry Rudolph,Tony Newton,Dan-Adrian German*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在"迷雾态（misty state）"实数形式框架下研究GHZ博弈，证明只需让每方持有两个球（共六个球）而非一个球，即可用纯实值形式取胜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算通常依赖复数，而迷雾态形式试图仅用实值来描述量子态。作者希望探究：在非局域性证明（如GHZ博弈）中，是否也能摆脱复数、仅用实值迷雾态实现相同效果，从而厘清复数在量子非局域性中的真正作用。

Method: 提出用额外量子比特对复数进行"标记（tag）"的通用方法：在量子计算中,可用一个附加qubit来编码复数相位，从而用实值形式替代复数。针对GHZ博弈，进一步指出不能使用单一的全局标记qubit，因为三方都能访问它，会形成潜在的信息传递通道，破坏非局域性；因此改为引入三个各自只能被本地访问的球（标记），即每方各持两个球。

Result: 证明三方各持两个球（共六个球）时，仅用标准实值迷雾态形式即可赢得GHZ博弈；同时说明单一通用标记qubit方案在非局域性场景下不可行，必须采用本地受限的多重标记。

Conclusion: 复数的"标记"在普通量子计算中可全局共享，但在非局域性证明中必须本地化，否则标记本身会充当通信信道。这揭示了实值迷雾态与复数量子力学在非局域性语境下的关键差别，以及本地性约束对标记方式的严格要求。

Abstract: We show that it is possible for three people to win the GHZ game with just the standard real valued misty state formalism, but each party will need to have two balls instead of one. That is, there will be six balls involved, rather than three. In fact, as a general rule, instead of using complex numbers in a quantum computer you can always add a qubit to "tag" complex numbers. But for proofs of non-locality, like the GHZ game, we cannot have a single universal tagging qubit, because all three parties would need access to it and therefore they could potentially transfer information via it. That's where we need to replace the one ball with three that each only need to be accessed locally.

</details>


### [94] [Exponential Quantum Advantage in Stationary Signal Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14424)
*Sojeong Park,Changhun Oh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明量子纠缠在获取平稳经典信号时间统计特性所需时间上具有指数优势：基于双模压缩探针与含保护闲置模式的局域Bell测量方案可实现多项式获取时间，而任何无闲置模式的局域协议需指数时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知时变信号对精密测量与刻画物理系统涨落至关重要。已有量子传感优势多针对参数估计，而针对平稳信号时间统计特性（如联合位移特征函数）的"获取时间"资源是否存在量子优势尚不明确。

Method: 任务定义为：在固定时长窗口内，利用对单次信号实现的连续测量，估计选定玻色模式上联合位移的特征函数。提出基于双模压缩探针与含保护闲置模式的局域Bell测量的纠缠辅助方案，并在计入读出噪声与信号窗口间关联的情况下界定其获取时间。同时给出针对局域、无闲置模式协议的获取时间下界证明。

Result: 对于严格平稳脉冲族，该方案以对数压缩与多项式光子数，在逆多项式精度下达到多项式获取时间。反之，证明任何局域无闲置模式协议所需的获取时间随每窗口选定时间模式数呈指数增长。该下界适用于顺序制备与任意时间纠缠的探针，允许非高斯输入与测量以及因果经典反馈，仅要求每个输出在其时间格内被破坏性测量。

Conclusion: 结果确立了针对单次连续监测平稳信号的指数级量子学习优势，其中传感成本以总获取时间度量，凸显纠缠与闲置模式保护的关键作用。

Abstract: Sensing time-varying signals is essential for precision measurement and for characterizing fluctuations in physical systems. In this work, we show that quantum entanglement provides an exponential advantage in the acquisition time required to learn the temporal statistics of a stationary classical signal. The task is to estimate the characteristic function of joint displacements on selected bosonic modes within a fixed-duration window, using continuous measurements of one signal realization. We present an entanglement-assisted scheme based on two-mode squeezed probes and local Bell measurements with protected idlers, and bound its acquisition time accounting for readout noise and correlations between signal windows. For a strictly stationary pulse family, it achieves polynomial acquisition time at inverse-polynomial accuracy with logarithmic squeezing and polynomial photon number. On the other hand, we prove that any local idler-free protocols require acquisition time exponential in the number of selected temporal modes per window. This lower bound holds for both sequentially prepared and arbitrarily temporally entangled probes, allowing non-Gaussian inputs and measurements and causal classical feedback, as long as each output is measured destructively within its time bin. These results establish an exponential quantum learning advantage for a single continuously monitored stationary signal, with the sensing cost measured by total acquisition time.

</details>


### [95] [Hyperpolarization-enabled cooperative molecular magnetic amplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14425)
*Jingyan Xu,Raphael Kircher,Min Jiang,Xinhua Peng,Danila Barskiy,Dmitry Budker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实现了一种连续运行的分子磁放大器，利用SABRE超极化技术在零场维持非平衡自旋序，将液态分子中的标量耦合核自旋转变为有源、频率选择性的增益介质，实现了对赫兹频段弱磁场的放大探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在赫兹频段探测弱磁场对生物磁学、地球物理传感及新物理探索至关重要，但受技术系统误差和环境噪声制约，仍是难题。因此需要一种能够在低频段有效放大弱磁信号的新型传感机制。

Method: 利用基于仲氢的SABRE（可逆交换信号放大）超极化，在零场维持非平衡自旋序，使液态分子中的标量耦合核自旋成为有源、频率选择性的增益介质。通过测量其复磁化率（χ）表征本征响应，并在自持J振荡阈值以下将集体产生的磁场反馈给自旋，以延长有效自旋相干并协同增强磁化率。

Result: 复磁化率测量显示本征响应约为 |χ|≈10；在阈值以下反馈可使磁化率协同增强至 |χ|≈80。在接近阈值处，对传感器位置3.37 Hz的弱磁场（20 pT）实现了约四倍的放大。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了超极化分子作为一种可调谐的有源磁性介质，可用于窄带量子传感与精密测量。

Abstract: Detecting weak magnetic fields at hertz frequencies is central to biomagnetism, geophysical sensing, and searches for new physics, yet it remains challenging because of technical systematics and environmental noise. Here, we realize a continuously operating molecular magnetic amplifier. Signal amplification by reversible exchange (SABRE) parahydrogen-based hyperpolarization maintains non-equilibrium spin order at zero field, turning scalar-coupled nuclear spins in liquid state molecules into an active, frequency-selective gain medium. Measurements of its complex magnetic susceptibility ($χ$) reveal an intrinsic response of $ | χ| \approx$ 10. By feeding the collectively generated magnetic field back to the spins below the threshold for self-sustained $J$-oscillation, we extend the effective spin coherence and cooperatively enhance the susceptibility up to $ | χ| \approx$ 80. Near threshold, this produces approximately fourfold amplification of a weak (20 pT) magnetic field at 3.37 Hz at the sensor location. These results establish hyperpolarized molecules as a tunable active magnetic medium for narrowband quantum sensing and precision measurement.

</details>


### [96] [Quadrature-detected zero-to-ultralow-field nuclear magnetic resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14451)
*Anne M. Fabricant,Florin Teleanu,Adam Ortmeier,Piotr Put,Seth Dilday,Alexej Jerschow,Dmitry Budker,Thomas Theis,Danila A. Barskiy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出正交检测零至超低场核磁共振（QD ZULF NMR）方案，利用两个正交原子光泵磁力计分别检测振荡样品磁化分量并合成正交信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单轴检测的ZULF NMR无法区分相对实验室坐标系的正负共振频率，谱峰密度高、重叠严重，且信息有限。

Method: 采用两个正交的原子光泵磁力计作为传感器，独立探测样品振荡磁化的分量，再合成为正交信号，并建立描述QD ZULF NMR信号的理论框架。

Result: 实现了热极化[¹³C]-甲酸和[¹³C]-甲醇（分别代表XA与XA₃自旋体系）的J谱实验；可确定旋磁比的绝对符号，并降低谱峰密度与重叠。

Conclusion: QD ZULF NMR可区分正负共振频率、确定旋磁比绝对符号，较单轴检测提供更丰富的核自旋动力学信息。

Abstract: We present the concept of quadrature-detected zero-to-ultralow-field nuclear magnetic resonance (QD ZULF NMR). Two orthogonal sensors, atomic optically pumped magnetometers, are used to separately detect components of oscillating sample magnetization, which are then combined into a quadrature signal. This approach enables the distinction of positive and negative resonance frequencies with respect to the laboratory frame---such that absolute signs of gyromagnetic ratios can be determined. In addition, QD ZULF NMR reduces peak density and overlap compared to ZULF NMR with single-axis detection, while providing richer information about nuclear-spin dynamics. As an illustration, we present experimental $J$-spectra for thermally polarized [$^{13}$C]-formic acid and [$^{13}$C]-methanol, compounds commonly used in ZULF NMR which represent XA and XA$_3$ spin systems, respectively. A theoretical framework describing QD ZULF NMR signals is provided.

</details>

*Phi Hung Nguyen,Daniel Soh*

TL;DR: 以量子Fisher信息(QFI)为度量优化量子储备池计算机，可在读出成本相当时相对经典储备池取得量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子储备池计算机相对经典储备池是否具有真实的量子优势，并寻找能超越等预算黑箱搜索与经典参数梯度的优化度量手段。

Method: 采用QFI度量优化量子参数，构建受驱Jaynes-Cummings储备池（含1–3个原子），在Mackey-Glass预测任务上与调优的回声状态网络对比；进行网格与贝叶斯搜索至饱和后叠加QFI感知优化阶段；推导关于重训练损失一阶变化率的QFI上界，并将差距分解为五个环节效率。

Result: 在4–30个可训练权重各规模下，该储备池以留出误差25–69%的幅度优于调优回声状态网络，并超过10,000个随机网络中99.5%的个体；搜索饱和后，QFI感知阶段在12–30权重处再降误差29–51%，优于等预算黑箱搜索或仅经典参数梯度，且在30权重处将劣势转为优势。

Conclusion: QFI度量优化可赋予量子储备池计算机可证的量子信息优势；所推导的QFI上界与五环节效率分解可定位限制优化的物理机制。

Abstract: We show that a quantum reservoir computer optimized with its quantum Fisher information (QFI) has a quantum advantage over a classical reservoir of equal readout cost, provided by a quantum-information advantage: optimizing the quantum parameters with the QFI metric. On Mackey-Glass forecasting, a driven Jaynes-Cummings reservoir with one to three atoms beats a tuned echo-state network at every size from 4 to 30 trained weights, by 25-69% in held-out error, and is ahead of 99.5% of 10,000 random networks. After grid and Bayesian searches saturate, the QFI-aware stage lowers the error by 29-51% at 12-30 weights, beyond equal-budget black-box search or classical-parameter gradients alone, and at 30 weights turns a loss into a win. We then derive a fundamental QFI bound on the first-order rate of change of the retrained loss and factorize the gap to this bound into five link efficiencies that identify the physical mechanism limiting optimization.

</details>


### [98] [Harmonic Reduction of Spin-Coupled Grover Walks on Hypercubes via Quantum Bernoulli Noises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14467)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超立方体上的量子行走是高维离散量子动力学的重要模型，而量子伯努利噪声提供了引入环境效应的自然框架。作者希望理解自旋耦合Grover行走在调和子空间上的可约结构，从而获得可解析处理的低维动力学与长期概率行为。

Method: 方法上，作者在量子伯努利噪声框架下考虑超立方体上的自旋耦合Grover行走，识别由中心Hamming层生成的宇称依赖调和子空间；偶数维给出不动点子空间，奇数维则约化为显式的八维动力学。随后借助对称群表示论刻画相应的同型结构并确定约化谱。

Result: 结果表明，偶数维时调和子空间构成不动点子空间，奇数维时行走在调和子空间上约化为八维动力学；通过对称群表示论获得了同型结构和约化谱的显式刻画，并给出长期概率行为。所得状态可只占据超立方体的渐近消失比例，但仍跨越其完整Hamming直径。

Abstract: We consider spin-coupled Grover walks on hypercubes within the framework of quantum Bernoulli noises (QBN). We identify parity-dependent harmonic subspaces generated by the central Hamming layers: in even dimension they give a fixed-point subspace, whereas in odd dimension the walk reduces on the harmonic subspace to an explicit eight-dimensional reduced dynamics. Using symmetric-group representation theory, we characterize the corresponding isotypic structure and determine the reduced spectrum. The harmonic reduction also yields explicit long-time probability behavior and shows that the resulting states may occupy an asymptotically vanishing fraction of the hypercube while still spanning its full Hamming diameter.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCCD架构中，随着QPU规模扩大，90° X-junction占比升高，频繁过junction使输运速度和末态声子激发成为量子算法执行效率的关键；但X-junction电场复杂，波形设计困难，难以兼顾高速与低声子激发。

Method: 提出LHX-T输运波形设计方法，集成Multi-RF低势垒赝势通道设计、多维绝热捷径动力学以及全输运序列上的约束DC电压优化；通过KKT线性系统快速计算平滑多通道波形，贯通波形设计、控制与计算验证。

Result: 单个$^{171}Yb^+$离子以平均14.6 m/s速度在5 μs内完成90°转弯；50次连续往返中末态等效相干声子数始终低于0.001且无明显累积。系统级评估显示，多种电路类型下单次执行周期中位时长降低约30%，输运耗时占比由约70%降至55%。

Conclusion: 该方法为可扩展QCCD QPU提供了高速、低声子激发的离子输运波形设计手段，为量子算法高效执行提供技术基础。

Abstract: Trapped ion quantum computing based on the quantum charge coupled device (QCCD) architecture uses functional zoning and ion transport to enable high fidelity computation and all to all qubit connectivity. As trapped ion quantum processing units (QPU) scale, 90° X-junction turns account for an increasing fraction of total transport time. Frequent junction crossings, therefore, make transport speed and final phonon excitation direct determinants of quantum algorithm execution efficiency. However, complex electric fields in X-junctions complicate waveform design, making it difficult to achieve high speed and low final phonon excitation simultaneously. Here, we develop a waveform design method for low-phonon-excitation high-speed X-junction(LHX-T) transport. It integrates low barrier pseudopotential channel design using multiple radio frequency (Multi-RF), multidimensional shortcut to adiabaticity dynamics, and constrained DC voltage optimization over the entire transport sequence. This framework connects waveform design, control, and computational validation, with smooth multichannel waveforms computed rapidly through a Karush-Kuhn-Tucker(KKT) linear system. The resulting waveform transports a single $^{171}Yb^+$ ion through a 90° turn in $5μs$ at an average speed of 14.6 m/s. Over 50 consecutive round trips, the final equivalent coherent phonon number consistently stays below 0.001, with no noticeable accumulation over time. System level evaluations at the QCCD scale show that the median duration of a single execution cycle is reduced by about $30\%$ across various circuit types. The fraction of execution time spent on transport decreases from approximately $70\%$ to $55\%$. This approach offers a means of designing fast ion transport waveforms with low phonon excitation for scalable QCCD based QPUs and provides a technical basis for efficient execution of quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [100] [Sample-Size Scalability and Representation Sensitivity of Quantum Machine Learning Models for Higgs Boson Event Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14533)
*Shiksha Bhardwaj,Ankhi Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究比较高能物理中量子SVM与变分量子分类器在有限训练样本和受限量子资源下的表现，并考察PCA与自编码器表示的影响，发现QSVM在大样本下优于VQC但仍不及最优经典分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习为高能物理多变量事件分类提供新途径，但在训练数据有限和量子资源受限条件下，其相对优化经典方法的性能尚不明确。

Method: 基于ATLAS Higgs机器学习挑战的模拟事件，对量子支持向量机（QSVM）和变分量子分类器（VQC）进行训练，样本量从250到4000事件；采用PCA和自编码器降维，并以四个优化经典分类器为基准，统一预处理、验证与评估协议；使用四分量PCA评估样本规模可扩展性，并通过四分量与八分量PCA的匹配事件比较考察表示敏感性。

Result: 在较大训练样本量下，QSVM表现优于VQC，但仍低于表现最好的经典分类器。

Conclusion: 在所评估的希格斯玻色子分类场景中，量子分类器尚未超越最优经典方法，其性能受训练样本规模与数据表示方式影响。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning offers new approaches to multivariate event classification in high-energy physics, but its performance relative to optimized classical methods remains uncertain under limited training data and restricted quantum resources. We investigate the sample-size scalability and representation sensitivity of a quantum support vector machine and a variational quantum classifier for Higgs-boson signal--background discrimination using simulated events from the ATLAS Higgs Machine Learning Challenge. The models are trained on balanced subsets ranging from 250 to 4000 events and evaluated on held-out samples preserving the original class distribution. Principal component analysis (PCA) and autoencoder (AE) representations are examined to reduce the input dimensionality and quantum-resource requirements. Four optimized classical classifiers are used as benchmarks under a common preprocessing, validation, and evaluation protocol. We assess training-sample scalability in classical and quantum classifiers using a four-component PCA representation and examine representation sensitivity through a secondary matched-event comparison of four- and eight-component PCA representations. Within the evaluated Higgs-boson classification setting, the QSVM showed stronger performance than the VQC at larger training sample sizes, while remaining below the best-performing classical classifiers.

</details>


### [101] [Entanglement-Capability-Aware Finite-Bond Circuit Cutting for Distributed Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14580)
*Weidong Liu,Qingqing Jiang,Yufu Liu,Ruiqing He,Jiandong Shang,Hengliang Guo,Qiang Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出SACE——一个结合纠缠能力感知筛选的仿真感知线路划分框架，用于分布式量子计算中的线路切割。EP-Cost以参数相关的纠缠能力代理与原生实现代价筛选候选划分，再经有限键精化选出最坏子线路差异最小的候选。实验表明其在QPD开销、重建精度与QFT/QAOA基准上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子计算需将超出单处理器能力的工作负载分解到资源受限组件上，线路切割是实现该分解的关键手段，但其效果高度依赖划分方案的选择。现有划分方法缺乏对纠缠能力与后续重建代价的感知，导致划分质量不稳定，因此需要一个仿真感知、且能兼顾纠缠能力的划分框架。

Method: 提出SACE框架：EP-Cost使用参数依赖的纠缠能力代理与原生实现代价对划分进行筛选；在重建约束下，有限键精化选取使归一化MPS仿真在键维χ与2χ之间最坏子线路差异最小的候选。搜索侧采用遗传算法，并与三种搜索方法对比；同时与EP-Cost-only、Kernighan-Lin和谱方法等基线比较。

Result: 在14个穷举小规模候选空间中，EP-Cost引导的遗传算法选择达到共优原生QPD开销，EP-Cost与QPD开销的线路内Spearman相关中位数为0.9965。相同切割数下，EP-Cost对8比特与9比特QFT分别将QPD开销降低25.20倍与16.89倍，并改善采样重建。12比特近似QFT上，确定性重建在全部六种低键条件下降低Pauli可观测均值绝对误差，在五种条件下降低总变差距离；五切割、7776个QPD项时，SACE在六种条件中的四种上四项重建指标全优。24比特下，SACE在全部30个实例-键维条件上相对三种基线均提升精确局部QFT精度；50比特QFT对比随参考收敛改善仍保持有利。加权QAOA提供互补且对键更敏感的证据。

Conclusion: SACE通过纠缠能力感知筛选与有限键精化，有效提升线路划分的QPD开销效率与重建精度，在QFT与QAOA基准上普遍优于现有基线，且精化收益在三种搜索方法中稳定存在；但更大的候选面板虽能改进优化代理，却不能保证更低的精确局部误差。

Abstract: Distributed quantum computing distributes workloads that exceed a single processor's capacity across resource-constrained components. Circuit cutting enables this decomposition, but its effectiveness depends on partition choice. We present SACE, a Simulation-Aware Circuit Partitioning framework with Entanglement-Capability-Aware Screening. EP-Cost screens partitions using a parameter-dependent entanglement-capability proxy and native implementation cost. Under reconstruction constraints, finite-bond refinement selects the candidate that minimizes the worst-subcircuit discrepancy between normalized MPS simulations at bond dimensions $χ$ and $2χ$. Across 14 exhaustively enumerated small-scale candidate spaces, EP-Cost-guided genetic algorithm selections attain co-optimal native quasiprobability decomposition (QPD) overhead, with a median within-circuit Spearman correlation of 0.9965 between EP-Cost and QPD overhead. At matched cut counts, EP-Cost reduces QPD overhead by $25.20\times$ and $16.89\times$ for 8- and 9-qubit QFT circuits, respectively, and improves sampled reconstruction. On 12-qubit approximate QFT, deterministic reconstruction reduces Pauli-observable mean absolute error in all six low-bond conditions and total-variation distance in five. With five cuts and 7,776 QPD terms, SACE is best on all four reconstruction metrics in four of six conditions. At 24 qubits, SACE improves exact-local QFT accuracy in all 30 instance-bond-dimension conditions against each EP-Cost-only, Kernighan-Lin, and spectral baseline. The 50-qubit QFT comparisons remain favorable as reference convergence improves. Weighted QAOA circuits provide complementary, more bond-sensitive evidence. Refinement benefits persist across three search methods; larger candidate panels improve the optimized proxy without guaranteeing lower exact-local error.

</details>


### [102] [Exploring Dynamical Quantum Phase Transitions via the Dynamical Phase Accumulated at an Equilibrium Critical Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14591)
*Han-Chuan Kou,Peng Li,Zhi-Han Zhang,Zhen-Yu Zheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在平衡临界点处带有有限等待时间的两步线性淬火所诱导的动力学量子相变（DQPT）。等待阶段积累的动力学相位直接反映临界谱结构，可将DQPT从单临界模式（SCM）调控为多临界模式（MCM），并改变率函数的临界指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索两步淬火中等待阶段所积累的动力学相位如何决定并调控DQPT的临界结构，从而建立积累相位与DQPT临界行为之间的直接联系。

Method: 以横场Ising链为主要模型，采用两步线性淬火（含平衡临界点处的有限等待时间）；通过解析求解与数值计算确定SCM与MCM两种区域的边界，并与扩展的SSH模型进行验证。

Result: 等待时间积累的相位使DQPT由单临界模式转变为多临界模式。所得SCM–MCM边界上，等待时间在瞬时淬火极限下趋于常数，而在慢淬火极限下遵循KZ标度。SCM区率函数呈线性奇异性（α=1）；边界处α=1/2，导致一阶导数发散；MCM区内参数平面上还出现α=1/2的离散参数线。

Conclusion: 积累的动力学相位与DQPT临界结构之间存在直接联系，据此可通过调控等待时间与淬火时间来设计不同的临界指数与奇异性行为，扩展SSH模型进一步支持该结论。

Abstract: We investigate dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPTs) generated by a two-step linear quench with a finite waiting time at the equilibrium critical point. The waiting stage leads to the accumulation of a dynamical phase that depends on the waiting time and directly reflects the critical spectral structure. In the transverse Ising chain, this accumulated phase modifies the critical structure of DQPTs from a single-critical-mode (SCM) regime to a multiple-critical-mode (MCM) regime. We analytically and numerically determine the boundary between the two regimes. Along this boundary, the waiting time approaches a constant in the sudden-quench limit, whereas in the slow-quench limit it follows Kibble--Zurek (KZ) scaling with the quench time. In the SCM regime, the rate function exhibits a conventional linear singularity, $r(t)-r(t^{*})\propto |t-t^{*}|^α$, with the critical exponent $α=1$ near the critical time. At the SCM--MCM boundary, this exponent changes to $α=1/2$, leading to a divergent first derivative of the rate function. Additional discrete parameter lines with $α=1/2$ also appear within the MCM regime in the parameter plane spanned by the waiting and quench times. Our findings establish a direct connection between the accumulated dynamical phase and the critical structure of DQPTs, further supported by an extended Su--Schrieffer--Heeger (SSH) model.

</details>


### [103] [Scalability and performance metrics for boson sampling devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14602)
*Dong-Gil Im,Chung-Hyun Lee,Joonsuk Huh,Yoon-Ho Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究玻色采样系统在实际实验条件下的理论可扩展性边界，采用相空间准概率分布的非负性条件分析光子损耗、模式失配和输入态误差的影响，并提出玻色采样分数与玻色采样体积两个器件级指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性光学平台有望通过玻色采样展示量子计算优势，但系统规模受实验约束限制；随着系统扩展，这些约束会将有效问题规模限制在经典可高效模拟的范围内，因此需要理论上的可扩展性边界分析。

Method: 利用相空间准概率分布函数（quasiprobability distribution functions）的非负性条件，分析光子损耗（photon loss）、模式失配（mode mismatch）与输入态误差（input state error）等实际实验效应；并引入两个器件级度量：玻色采样分数（boson sampling score，衡量器件可用容量）与玻色采样体积（boson sampling volume，作为跨平台硬件级基准）。

Result: 给出了玻色采样系统在实际实验条件下的理论可扩展性边界，并通过所提出的两个度量刻画给定器件的可用容量及不同平台在优势相关问题规模区间中的可达性。

Conclusion: 研究结果为理解实际实验条件下玻色采样系统的性能提供了基础性认识，并为玻色采样系统的规模化扩展提供了实用指南。

Abstract: Linear-optical platforms provide a promising route to demonstrate quantum computational advantage, particularly through the boson sampling problem. While a boson sampling system should be large enough to show the quantum advantage, the size of the boson sampling problem is often restricted by experimental constraints. In particular, as a boson sampling system is scaled up, experimental constraints can limit its effective problem size to permit efficient classical simulation. In this paper, we investigate the theoretical scalability bounds for boson sampling systems under practical experimental conditions, including photon loss, mode mismatch, and input state error. We employ non-negativity conditions for phase-space quasiprobability distribution functions to analyze these effects. In addition, we introduce two device-level metrics, the boson sampling score which measures the usable capacity of a given device and the boson sampling volume which provides a common hardware-level benchmark for comparing different platforms and assessing their access to an advantage-relevant problem size regime. Our results provide fundamental insights into the performance of boson sampling systems under realistic experimental conditions and offer practical guidelines for scaling up boson sampling systems.

</details>


### [104] [Reducing Trust Cost in Quantum Application Development Using AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14665)
*Katsuhiro Endo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合生成式AI与形式化验证的量子程序开发方法，通过将实现分解为数学规约、量子电路及对应定理（含证明），把需人工审查的部分与可机械验证的部分分离，以降低"信任成本"，并以随机粒子系统时间演化为例展示该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI能助力量子应用的设计与实现，但随之而来的是验证所生成电路是否真正实现目标计算的负担。作者将这种人工理解、检查与判断的负担称为"信任成本"（trust cost），旨在通过方法论设计来降低它。

Method: 提出一种AI与形式化验证相结合的开发方法学。其核心是将实现划分为三个组成部分：（1）目标数学规约；（2）量子电路；（3）证明二者一致的对应定理及其证明。这种划分使由人类检查的规约与定理陈述，与交由机器机械检查的定理证明相互分离。

Result: 作为该方法的实例开发，作者给出了一个随机粒子系统时间演化的实现及其结果，用以验证所提方法的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将人工审查范围限定于规约与定理陈述、把证明交由机械验证，该AI+形式化验证的开发方法学有望有效降低量子程序开发中的信任成本。

Abstract: Generative AI can be a powerful tool for supporting the design and implementation of quantum applications, whereas the burden of checking whether generated circuits realize the intended computation increases. In this study, we regard this burden of human understanding, checking, and judgment as ``trust cost,'' and propose a development methodology that integrates AI and formal verification to reduce it. In the proposed development methodology, the implementation is divided into three components: a target mathematical specification, a quantum circuit, and a correspondence theorem and its proof that prove the agreement between the two. This division separates the specifications and theorem statements checked by humans from the theorem proofs entrusted to mechanical checking. As a concrete development example of this proposal, we describe an implementation for the time evolution of a stochastic particle system and its results.

</details>


### [105] [Clifford Invariants and Three-Copy Quantum Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14758)
*Xingya Fan,Yongzhi Luan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文显式研究 n 个 qutrit（n≥2）上 Clifford 表示的第三张量幂，给出其分解为 5 个配对不等价不可约成分（重数 1,1,1,1,2），将张量立方的 Clifford twirl 约化为 4 个实参数，并据此推导若干量子信息与设计理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clifford twirl 与高阶张量幂的显式分解在量子信息中具有基础意义，可用于刻画不变量、稳定子保真度、可分离性与设计判据等。此前对 qutrit 多重张量幂缺乏显式处理，本文旨在填补这一空白并给出可计算的结构。

Method: 对 n 个 qutrit 上 Clifford 表示的第三张量幂进行显式分解，得到 5 个配对不等价不可约成分，重数分别为 1,1,1,1,2；由此将张量立方的 Clifford twirl 参数化为 4 个实参数。其中两个为输入的谱矩，另两个为三次不变量，分别表示为 Weyl 特征函数的立方和与离散 Wigner 函数的立方和。在迹一 Hermitian 矩阵上分析次数至多三的不变多项式空间，并研究纯输入情形与稳定子态性质；同时给出有限射影 Clifford 群的特征标公式及两个 qutrit 的完整特征标表。

Result: 在迹一 Hermitian 矩阵上，次数至多三的不变多项式由常数函数与 4 个代数独立函数张成，但这些函数不分离 Clifford 轨道。对纯输入，单个参数即可刻画：恢复了其取值范围及由稳定子态给出的最大值刻画。进一步给出了稳定子保真度的界、对称族中跨三个拷贝的完全可分离性判定、复杂射影 3-设计的判据，并对联合不变的量子布尔函数进行分类；对稳定子检验与未知可观测量检验给出了显式的拷贝数与查询数界。

Conclusion: 第三张量幂的显式分解提供了以少数量刻画 Clifford twirl 的有效框架，将谱矩与三次不变量结合，统一导出稳定子保真度、可分离性、3-设计判据及检验复杂度等结果；所给特征标公式与两 qutrit 特征标表为该框架提供了群论基础，同时表明低次不变量不足以分离 Clifford 轨道。

Abstract: We give an explicit treatment of the third tensor power of the Clifford representation on $n$ qutrits, for $n\geq2$. This representation has five pairwise inequivalent irreducible constituents, with multiplicities $1,1,1,1,2$. The decomposition reduces the Clifford twirl of a tensor cube to four real parameters. Two of them are spectral moments of the input. The other two are cubic invariants expressed as sums of cubes of the Weyl characteristic function and the discrete Wigner function. On trace-one Hermitian matrices, the invariant polynomials of degree at most three are spanned by the constant function and four algebraically independent functions. These functions do not separate Clifford orbits. For pure inputs a single parameter suffices. We recover its range and the characterization of its maximum by stabilizer states. We bound the stabilizer fidelity, determine full separability across the three copies in the symmetric family, and recover the criterion for a complex projective $3$-design. We classify the jointly invariant quantum Boolean functions. For stabilizer testing and for testing unknown observables we give explicit bounds on copies and queries. We also give character formulas for the finite projective Clifford groups and record the complete character table for two qutrits.

</details>


### [106] [Avoiding Defects within Heavy-Hexagon Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14778)
*Hansol Kim,Younghun Kwon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文面向heavy-hexagon（连通度2-3）超导架构提出缺陷规避方案，将super-stabilizer所需规范测量从四个周期压缩到两个周期，并在IBM量子硬件上实验验证缺陷规避使每周期逻辑X/Z错误率降低约10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子处理器存在因制造与校准不完美导致的缺陷量子比特，缺陷会限制相邻比特连通性、破坏稳定子测量、引发频繁检测事件并抬高逻辑错误率。已有方案（stabilizer folding、基于gauge算子的super-stabilizer）主要针对连通度3-4的架构，且缺乏在实际物理硬件缺陷下证明逻辑错误率真正下降的实验演示。

Method: 针对连通度仅2-3的heavy-hexagon架构设计缺陷规避电路。相比此前需要四个测量周期才能完成构成super-stabilizer的所有gauge算子测量，本方案仅用两个周期即完成全部gauge测量。随后在IBM量子硬件上识别物理缺陷并部署相应缺陷规避电路进行实验验证。

Result: 规避缺陷后，逻辑X错误与逻辑Z错误的每周期逻辑错误率均下降约10%。

Conclusion: 即使在heavy-hexagon晶格的受限连通性下，超导硬件中的缺陷也能被成功处理；缺陷规避在真实量子硬件上可显著降低逻辑错误，为容错量子计算的缺陷缓解提供了可行的实验路径。

Abstract: When implementing scalable fault-tolerant quantum computing using superconducting quantum processors, one of the critical challenges to overcome is the presence of defective qubits arising from fabrication and calibration imperfections. A defect on a specific qubit severely restricts the connectivity between adjacent qubits, disrupts stabilizer measurements, induces frequent detection events, and consequently inflates the logical error rate. To mitigate this issue, various strategies have been proposed, such as syndrome extraction circuits based on stabilizer folding and the construction of super-stabilizers via gauge operators. However, these methods have primarily been developed for architectures with relatively high connectivity of 3 to 4. Furthermore, experimental demonstrations showing an actual reduction in logical error rates in the presence of physical hardware defects have been lacking. In this work, we propose a defect-avoidance scheme tailored for the heavy-hexagon architecture, which features a lower connectivity of 2 to 3. While previous approaches required four measurement cycles to measure all gauge operators comprising a super-stabilizer, our proposed scheme completes all gauge measurements within only two cycles. We experimentally validate this approach on IBM quantum hardware by identifying physical defects and implementing the defect-avoidance circuits. As a result, avoiding the defect yields an approximately 10\% reduction in the logical error rate per cycle for both logical $X$ and $Z$ errors. These findings demonstrate that defects in superconducting hardware can be successfully handled even under the constrained connectivity of heavy-hexagon lattices, and that defect avoidance substantially reduces logical errors on real quantum hardware.

</details>


### [107] [Classical Communication Helps Single-Shot Quantum Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14785)
*Omri Farber,Gilad Gour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 无辅助量子压缩中，自由经典通信可在单次（single-shot）场景带来无界节省，即使对i.i.d.源的渐近速率没有改变；本文通过精确刻画最优源保真度建立了这一分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自由经典通信对量子压缩的价值：虽然它不改变i.i.d.源的渐近压缩速率，但作者希望确定它在单次场景中能带来多大的节省，并量化辅助与无辅助压缩代价之间的差距。

Method: 作者确定了通过m维无噪声量子信道、在不泄露给不可访问的参考系统（即保持与参考的关联）的条件下压缩源的最优保真度。无经典辅助时最优保真度为Ky Fan范数‖ρ‖_(m)（源态m个最大特征值之和）；有经典辅助时给出了显式的谱阈值公式及达到该阈值的协议。压缩代价对应于log-rank熵（max-entropy）的两种平滑版本：局域操作下为迹距离平滑，LOCC下为二次平滑。

Result: 在每个固定误差(0,1)下，存在源其无辅助压缩代价任意大，而仅用一个qubit加上经典通信（无需预共享纠缠）即可完成。当m小于源秩时，经典通信严格提升保真度。对具有正熵方差的i.i.d.源，两种代价在一阶、二阶、三阶渐近项上完全相同；对非格点谱，经典通信每块块渐近节省log₂(e)−1比特，辅助与无辅助最小通信维度之比收敛到2/e。

Conclusion: 自由经典通信在单次量子压缩中可带来无界节省，而在i.i.d.源的渐近情形下仅表现为常数阶（常数阶之外的阶数）优势；这一分离由Ky Fan范数与谱阈值公式给出的精确最优保真度刻画，对应两种max-entropy平滑方案。

Abstract: Free classical communication can yield an unbounded single-shot saving in quantum compression, even though it leaves the asymptotic rate of an i.i.d. source unchanged. At every fixed error in $(0,1)$, we exhibit sources whose unassisted compression cost is arbitrarily large, yet a single qubit suffices with classical communication and no preshared entanglement. We establish this separation by determining the exact optimal source fidelity for compression through an $m$-dimensional noiseless quantum channel, with and without free classical communication, while preserving correlations with an inaccessible reference. Without classical assistance, the optimal fidelity is exactly the Ky Fan norm $\|ρ\|_{(m)}$, the sum of the $m$ largest eigenvalues of the source state. With classical assistance, we obtain an explicit spectral threshold formula and a protocol attaining it. Classical communication strictly improves fidelity whenever $m$ is smaller than the source rank. The corresponding compression costs are two smoothed versions of the log-rank entropy, also called max-entropy: trace-distance smoothing under local operations and quadratic smoothing under LOCC. For i.i.d. copies of a fixed source with positive entropy variance, however, the two costs have identical first-, second-, and third-order asymptotic terms. For nonlattice spectra, we determine the constant-order advantage: classical communication saves $\log_2(e)-1$ bits per block asymptotically, and the ratio of assisted to unassisted minimum communication dimensions converges to $2/e$.

</details>


### [108] [Entanglement generation from degeneracy breaking of superradiant Liouvillian steady states by local operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14788)
*Adam Burgess,Erik M. Gauger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在超辐射动力学中引入非相干局域过程，可突破"Dicke阶梯上不产生纠缠"的传统认知，产生高水平的发射体间纠缠，且该纠缠在对称环境噪声下具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超辐射是发射体协同辐射的集体光学效应，尽管其级联过程经历高度纠缠态，但沿Dicke阶梯的动力学中，超辐射并不必然在发射体间产生纠缠。作者旨在探究通常被认为对纠缠和超辐射均有害的非相干局域过程，能否成为产生发射体间纠缠的资源。

Method: 作者发展了基于简并非厄米微扰论的一般理论框架，用于研究这些局域操作引起的超辐射Liouvillian稳态简并破缺，涵盖从少量发射体到热力学极限的情形。

Result: 研究表明，在该regime下纠缠变得普遍存在，并展示其对其他形式对称环境噪声的鲁棒性；在泵浦占主导的regime，布居积累趋向最大纠缠的最小赝自旋态，对应多发射体单重态。

Conclusion: 该工作深入揭示了多体纠缠的本质，以及局域操作与纠缠生成之间引人注目的联系，表明局域非相干过程可成为纠缠产生的有效机制。

Abstract: Superradiance is a collective optical effect where emitters cooperatively radiate with a maximal intensity scaling proportional to the square of the number of emitters. Despite the superradiant cascade transitioning through highly entangled states, it has been shown that, for dynamics along the Dicke ladder, superradiance need not generate entanglement between the emitters. By contrast, we show how introducing incoherent local processes -- normally considered detrimental to both entanglement and superradiance -- can generate high levels of inter-emitter entanglement. We develop a general framework, using degenerate non-Hermitian perturbation theory, for the study of superradiant Liouvillian steady-state degeneracy breaking by these local operations from a few emitters up into the thermodynamic limit. From this, we are able to show that entanglement becomes ubiquitous in this regime and demonstrate its robustness against other forms of symmetric environmental noise. In the regime where pumping dominates, we find that population accumulates towards the maximally entangled minimal pseudo-spin states, corresponding to multi-emitter singlet states. This provides a deeper insight into many-body entanglement and the curious connection between local operations and entanglement generation.

</details>


### [109] [A complete classification of operator controllability for closed three-level quantum systems driven by a single control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14938)
*Sergey Kuznetsov,Alexander Pechen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了两条关于单控制场驱动有限维封闭量子系统算符可控性的李代数结果，并据此对三能级量子系统的算符可控性进行了完整分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单相干控制下有限维封闭量子系统的算符可控性，给出一般性李代数判据，并解决三能级系统可控性缺乏系统分类的问题。

Method: 采用李代数方法：证明当加入受控能级移动时系统可控性保持的充分条件；给出使动力学李代数包含 su(N)（从而系统算符可控）的基元充分条件；并结合已知可控性条件进行分析。

Result: 得到三能级量子系统算符可控性的完整分类，以自由与相互作用哈密顿量的矩阵元表述；分类覆盖能级简并与能级间距简并的各种组合、两条或三条允许跃迁、以及有无受控能级移动的情形，并给出每种情形下算符可控的充要条件。

Conclusion: 所提李代数结果与已知条件相结合，可系统刻画三能级系统的算符可控性，形成完整的充要条件分类。

Abstract: In this work, we study operator controllability of finite-dimensional closed quantum systems driven by a single coherent control. Two general Lie-algebraic results on controllability of such systems are proved. The first gives sufficient conditions under which controllability of the system is preserved when controlled energy-level shifts are added. The second gives a sufficient basis-element condition under which the dynamical Lie algebra contains $\mathfrak{su}(N)$ and the system is therefore operator controllable. These results, together with known controllability conditions, are applied to obtain a complete classification of operator controllability of all three-level quantum systems in terms of their free and interaction Hamiltonian matrix elements. This classification covers all combinations of energy-level and level-spacing degeneracies, two or three allowed transitions, as well as the presence or absence of controlled energy-level shifts, and gives necessary and sufficient conditions for operator controllability in each case.

</details>


### [110] [Partial-coherence weak measurement of polarization qubits with a temporal pointer: theory and experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14954)
*Shane P. Gervais,Lambert Giner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了后选择时间位移的闭式表达式，用指针可见度ν统一描述非相干与相干弱测量极限，并在三种庞加莱球轨迹上实验验证：两条轨迹给出无参数基准（实部为cos²θ与1/2），第三条对非相干敏感并符合单参数部分相干模型，时间位移精度达几皮秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱测量在计量学、量子佯谬探测和量子态直接测量中很有用；偏振量子比特配时间指针的弱测量可表征弱值，但指针仅部分相干的情形尚需一个能衔接非相干与相干极限的统一描述。

Method: 推导后选择时间位移的闭式表达式，通过单一参数ν（指针可见度）在非相干与相干（理想）弱测量极限之间插值；对跨越庞加莱球主轴旋转对应的三条轨迹的多种偏振态进行实验测试，其中两条轨迹为无参数基准，第三条对非相干敏感；从脉冲前沿的平滑导数提取时间位移并进行内部标定，同时用频率质心定性获取虚部正交分量。

Result: 两条轨迹的弱值实部分别遵循cos²θ与1/2，为无参数基准；第三条轨迹的弱值对非相干敏感，可由单参数部分相干模型很好描述，并由此得到降低的指针可见度。在10 ns光脉冲上使用商用元件达到几皮秒精度的时间位移提取；频率质心定性给出虚部正交分量。

Conclusion: 部分相干脉冲系统中的时间弱测量仍然是可访问且定量的，单参数部分相干模型可有效刻画弱值随非相干程度的变化。

Abstract: Weak measurement has proven useful for metrology, for probing quantum paradoxes, and for the direct measurement of quantum states. Weak measurements of polarization qubits with temporal pointers provide a direct route to weak-value characterization, including in regimes where the pointer is only partially coherent. We derive a closed-form expression for the post-selected temporal shift that interpolates between the incoherent and coherent (ideal) weak-measurement limits through a single parameter $ν$, the pointer visibility. The expression is experimentally tested for a range of polarization states spanning three trajectories corresponding to rotations about the principal axes of the Poincaré sphere. Two of the trajectories provide parameter-free benchmarks, with the real part of the weak value following $\cos^2θ$ and $1/2$. In contrast, the weak value along the third trajectory is sensitive to incoherence and is well described by the single-parameter partial-coherence model, from which a reduced pointer visibility is obtained. Time shifts are extracted from the smoothed derivative of the pulse leading edge with internal calibration, reaching few-picosecond precision on 10 ns optical pulses using commercial components. The same apparatus also gives qualitative access to the imaginary quadrature through the frequency centroid, showing that temporal weak measurements remain accessible and quantitative in partially coherent pulsed systems.

</details>


### [111] [Entanglement detection in dynamical quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15021)
*Xingjian Zhang,Jiayi Wu,Jue Xu,Qi Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于稳定子（stabilizer）演化的纠缠见证构造方法，可在较长的多体演化时间内有效探测纠缠的存在与结构，且测量设置数随系统规模增长保持适中，计算可 largely 符号化、粗粒化完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子科学与工程日益关注大规模动力学系统的开发与控制。随着系统规模增大，会出现新现象与量子优势，但多体动力学也更难监测与表征：哈密顿量常缺乏对称性，诱导的多体演化很快难以追踪。因此亟需在长演化时间内有效探测纠缠等关键量子特征的手段。

Method: 受基于稳定子的模拟启发，追踪底层状态广义稳定子（generalized stabilizers）的演化，并据此构造线性纠缠见证（linear entanglement witnesses）。针对一类常见哈密顿量模型设计有效见证，用于探测纠缠的存在与结构；计算可 largely 符号化进行，且与精确态模拟不同，见证设计可采用更粗粒化、数字化的方式进行。

Result: 在最多十二个量子比特的海森堡型（Heisenberg-type）哈密顿量上进行数值实验：随系统规模增大，所需测量设置数可保持在适度规模；纠缠探测在显著超过朴素方法可达时间范围的演化时长内仍然有效。

Conclusion: 稳定子型纠缠见证设计为长时多体演化中的纠缠探测提供了可行途径，并有望推动大规模系统量子控制的进一步探索。

Abstract: Quantum science and engineering increasingly target the exploitation and control of large-scale dynamical systems. As system size grows, novel phenomena and quantum advantages emerge, but the resulting many-body dynamics become correspondingly harder to monitor and characterize. In particular, the underlying Hamiltonian often lacks symmetries, and the induced many-body dynamics quickly become hard to track. Focusing on one of the most important quantum features---entanglement---we demonstrate that, for a broad class of common Hamiltonian models, one can design effective witnesses that detect both the presence and structure of entanglement over relatively long evolution times. Inspired by stabilizer-based simulation, we track the evolution of generalized stabilizers of the underlying state and use them to construct linear entanglement witnesses. Through numerical experiments on Heisenberg-type Hamiltonians with up to twelve qubits, we show that the required number of measurement settings can be kept at a modest scale as the system size increases, and entanglement detection remains effective over evolution times that significantly exceed those accessible to naive approaches. The corresponding calculations can be carried out largely symbolically. Furthermore, unlike accurate state simulation, the stabilizer-based design of entanglement witnesses can be performed in a much more coarse-grained, digitized manner. We hope these results will stimulate further exploration of the quantum control of large systems.

</details>


### [112] [Generative learning of time series with holographic quantum circuit sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15083)
*Bingzhi Zhang,Matthew W. Lee,Tom Finke,Quntao Zhuang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出全息量子电路采样（HQCS），用中电路测量、量子比特复用与独立参数化时间块，在固定量子比特数下生成长时间序列，并证明其损失方差反多项式界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子生成模型在学习长时间序列时面临量子比特需求不断增长以及损失景观集中（指数集中）的问题，限制了可扩展性与可训练性。

Method: HQCS结合中电路测量、量子比特复用和独立参数化的时间块，以固定量子比特数生成轨迹；针对Haar随机HQCS与归一化指数核，理论分析最大均值差异（MMD）损失方差；并将时间参数化与非平稳马尔可夫过程族中重复共享块生成器进行门数对比。

Result: 证明了HQCS在Haar随机情形下MMD损失方差具有反多项式上界，区别于全局Haar随机生成器的指数集中；对一般非平稳马尔可夫过程，精确生成所需门数由相对重复共享块生成器的随序列长度的二次降为线性；仿真成功学习高斯自回归过程与历史VIX轨迹，后者MMD较经典基准低十倍以上。

Conclusion: HQCS为量子时间序列生成提供了固定量子比特需求的框架，并随序列长度增长可证明地避免指数损失集中。

Abstract: Quantum generative models face growing qubit requirements and concentrated loss landscapes when learning long time series. Here we introduce holographic quantum circuit sampling (HQCS), which combines mid-circuit measurements, qubit reuse and independently parameterized temporal blocks to generate trajectories with a fixed number of qubits. For Haar-random HQCS and a normalized exponential kernel, we prove inverse-polynomial bounds on maximum mean discrepancy loss variance, contrasting with exponential concentration in global Haar-random generators. For generic members of a family of nonstationary Markov processes, temporal parameterization reduces exact-generation gate counts from quadratic to linear in sequence length relative to a specified class of repeated shared-block generators. Simulations demonstrate learning of Gaussian autoregressive processes and historical Volatility Index (VIX) trajectories, with the latter achieving over tenfold lower maximum mean discrepancy than the tested classical benchmarks. These results establish a framework for quantum time-series generation with fixed qubit requirements and provable avoidance of exponential loss concentration as sequence length grows.

</details>


### [113] [The Rényi area law conjecture is false below $α=1$ in high dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15199)
*Tomotaka Kuwahara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文否证了高维Rényi面积律猜想：构造出具有均匀能隙、唯一基态的有限程哈密顿量，其基态满足von Neumann面积律却呈Rényi体积律纠缠，证明α=1是该绝热连通类中普适Rényi面积律的尖锐阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面积律表明有能隙量子基态的纠缠随边界而非体积增长，一维对所有Rényi熵成立，Rényi面积律猜想断言高维对每个固定0<α<1亦然。本文旨在检验该猜想在高维是否普遍成立。

Method: 构造固定局域维度、有界相互作用强度、唯一基态且具均匀谱隙的有限程哈密顿量；其基态通过误差空间复杂度机制实现体积律Rényi纠缠（任意小权重的局域可识别分量可编码高纠缠复杂度状态），并通过均匀有隙局域路径与乘积态相连；同时建立近似基态投影算子的最坏情形秩-误差权衡。

Result: 在二维及以上所有空间维度否证Rényi面积律猜想；所构造基态服从von Neumann面积律但呈Rényi体积律。在固定能隙下，截面积A的平面切割处，投影误差ε对应的最优Schmidt秩按exp[Õ((A log ε^{-1})^{2/3})]标度（当log ε^{-1}≳A）。

Conclusion: α=1是该绝热连通类中普适Rényi面积律的尖锐阈值。

Abstract: Area laws express the expectation that entanglement in gapped quantum ground states scales with a boundary rather than a volume. In one dimension this holds for every Rényi entropy, and the Rényi area-law conjecture asserts the same in higher dimensions for each fixed $0<α<1$. Here we disprove this conjecture in every spatial dimension two and higher. We construct finite-range Hamiltonians with fixed local dimension, bounded interaction strength, unique ground states and a uniform spectral gap, whose ground states exhibit volume-law Rényi entanglement while obeying a von Neumann area law and remaining connected to product states through uniformly gapped local paths. The mechanism is error-space complexity: locally identifiable components of arbitrarily small weight can encode states with high entanglement complexity. We also establish the worst-case rank-error trade-off for approximate ground-state projectors, a key tool in one-dimensional area-law proofs. At fixed gap, the optimal Schmidt rank at projection error $ε$ across a planar cut of cross-section $A$ scales as $\exp[\tildeΘ((A\logε^{-1})^{2/3})]$ when $\logε^{-1}\gtrsim A$. Thus $α=1$ is the sharp threshold for a universal Rényi area law within this adiabatically connected class.

</details>


### [114] [Signal-to-noise problems in fault-tolerant quantum computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15210)
*Hyunwoo Oh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子计算通过幺正演化规避符号问题，但测量本身是随机的，估计可观测量所需的采样次数仍会随系统规模、时间间隔或逆温度指数增长，即信号-噪声问题在无符号问题情形下依然存在。本文剖析其起源与结构，并提出量子版 Stein 控制变量（基于对易子 [X,A] 及其离散对角对应物）进行方差缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正一个常见误解：认为量子模拟既然绕开了符号问题，就可作为经典采样的完美替代。实际上量子测量的内在随机性导致估计可观测量时采样成本指数增长，与经典的信号-噪声问题同源。作者意在阐明该噪声在无符号问题情形下仍存在，强调方差缩减才是量子算法在多体物理中实用的关键。

Method: 1) 分析量子计算中信号-噪声问题的起源与结构，并与经典蒙特卡洛进行类比，说明噪声在无符号问题区域依然持续；2) 提出缓解策略：量子推广的 Stein 控制变量构造，从算符对易子 [X,A] 及其离散对角对应物出发构建无偏估计量；3) 通过可精确求解的数值算例验证方法，并量化可实现的方差缩减。

Result: 分析方法揭示了信号-噪声问题在量子计算中的来源与结构，并表明其在无符号问题区域依然存在；所提出的量子 Stein 控制变量方法通过可精确求解的数值示例得到验证，并量化了可实现的方差缩减效果。

Conclusion: 仅避免符号问题并不足以使量子算法在多体物理中具备实际可行性；方差缩减是与（乃至比）符号问题规避同等必要的要求，是量子算法走向实用的关键。

Abstract: Quantum computation offers a promising alternative to classical Monte Carlo methods by naturally avoiding sign problems through unitary evolution. A common misconception follows from this: because quantum simulation sidesteps the sign problem, it is often assumed to be an essentially perfect substitute for classical sampling. This is false---quantum measurements are intrinsically stochastic, and estimating observables often requires a number of samples that grows exponentially with system size, time separation, or inverse temperature, exactly as in the classical signal-to-noise problem. In this work, we analyze the origin and structure of signal-to-noise problems in quantum computation, drawing parallels with classical Monte Carlo, and demonstrate that such noise persists even in sign-problem-free regimes. We discuss strategies to mitigate it, in particular a quantum generalization of Stein control variates construction, in which unbiased estimators are built from operator commutators $[X,A]$ and their discrete diagonal counterparts, and validate the approach with exactly solvable numerical examples that quantify the achievable variance reduction. Our results underscore that variance reduction, not merely sign-problem avoidance, is required to make quantum algorithms practically viable for many-body physics.

</details>


### [115] [Monogamy of quantum steering in exciton--cavity optomechanical system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15233)
*Hamza Harraf,Mohamed Amazioug,Rachid Ahl Laamara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了混合激子-腔光机械系统中量子导引的单配性，建立并验证了高斯导引的CKW型单配不等式，表明系统参数、热噪声与动力学演化可调控三模间双体导引的分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子导引作为介于纠缠与非定域性之间的量子关联，其多体系统中的分配规律（单配性）对量子资源利用具有重要意义；混合激子-光机械平台提供了研究多模高斯量子关联的可控平台，但该体系中导引的单配性尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 采用高斯态形式体系，在激子、光子与机械三种模式间量化导引度；分别考察稳态与动力学区域，建立并验证高斯导引的CKW型单配不等式；分析系统参数、热噪声及时间演化对导引强度与单配关系的调控作用。

Result: 三模间双体导引的分布可由系统参数调控；升温总体上抑制高斯导引强度并改变CKW型单配关系所施加的导引共享程度；在动力学区域，适当时间窗口内导引度可随演化时间增强，说明瞬态动力学可用于增强量子关联。

Conclusion: 混合激子-光机械平台可用于可控量子关联的实现，为多体量子资源的产生与操控提供了可行途径。

Abstract: In this work, we study a theoretical scheme to investigate the monogamy of quantum steering in a hybrid exciton--cavity optomechanical (EOM) system. Within the Gaussian-state formalism, we quantify the steerability among the exciton, photon, and mechanical modes in both the steady-state and dynamical regimes. We establish and verify CKW-type monogamy inequalities for Gaussian steering and demonstrate that the distribution of bipartite steering among the three modes can be controlled through the system parameters. We further examine the influence of thermal noise and temporal evolution on the steering properties and the corresponding monogamy relations. Our results show that increasing the temperature generally suppresses the strength of Gaussian steering and modifies the degree of steering sharing imposed by the CKW-type monogamy relations. In contrast, in the dynamical regime, the steerability can increase with evolution time over suitable temporal windows, indicating that the transient dynamics can be exploited to enhance quantum correlations. These results highlight the potential of hybrid exciton--optomechanical platforms for controllable quantum correlations and provide a promising route toward the generation and manipulation of multipartite quantum resources.

</details>


### [116] [Photonic realization of an electromagnetic cavity field with uniform mirror motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15312)
*A. R. Urzúa,I. Ramos-Prieto,R. Román-Ancheyta,J. Récamier-Angelini,L. Medina-Dozal,F. Soto-Eguibar,H. M. Moya-Cessa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究含匀速直线运动的非稳态电磁腔场有效哈密顿量，证明其动力学方程可幺正对角化，并给出运动速度的双边界；随后以Sp(4,R)辛代数重释玻色耦合项，用线性光学实现强耦合，生成双模压缩态并分析光子数、纠缠与混合度等统计性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C. K. Law 引入的非稳态电磁腔场有效哈密顿量在匀速运动情形下的数学性质尚缺乏系统研究；同时，由幺正对角化导出的速度界限虽数学自洽，却比物理允许范围 |v/c|<1 宽约一个数量级，需要寻找新的理论框架以突破这一限制并刻画强耦合区域的物理效应。

Method: 对含匀速运动的非稳态腔场有效哈密顿量进行详细数学分析，给出其动力学方程的幺正对角化并推导运动速度的双边不等式 |v/c| ≲ 10.1716；进而将玻色耦合项重新解释为辛代数 Sp(4,R) 的生成元，将其对应到可由线性光学元件实现的变换，从而允许耦合参数取非小值，进入强有效相互作用区；在此基础上推导双模压缩态的解析表达式及各模平均光子数。

Result: 得到速度的双边界 |v/c| ≲ 10.1716；实现强耦合条件下的双模压缩态生成并给出各模平均光子数的解析结果；计算了模间关联、Mandel 参数与线性熵等刻画混合度与纠缠的统计量；给出相应的 Wigner 函数以在相空间中可视化系统行为。

Conclusion: 腔场运动、模耦合与量子场非经典关联之间可通过底层辛代数 Sp(4,R) 建立联系；将玻色耦合项视为线性光学可实现变换的重释方法突破了小耦合参数限制，为研究强有效相互作用下的压缩态与纠缠特性提供了可行的解析与相空间框架。

Abstract: We perform a detailed mathematical study of the effective Hamiltonian describing a non-stationary electromagnetic cavity field, introduced by C.~K.~Law, in the presence of uniform linear motion. We find that the corresponding dynamical equations admit a unitary diagonalization and that the normalized velocity of motion satisfies the two-sided bound $\lvert v/c \rvert \lesssim 10.1716$. Although this result is mathematically consistent, the interval obtained is an order of magnitude larger than the physically admissible range $\lvert v/c \rvert < 1$. To address this limitation, we propose a reinterpretation of bosonic coupling terms as generators of the symplectic algebra $\mathrm{Sp}(4,\mathbb{R})$, identifying them with transformations that can be implemented through linear optical elements. Within this photonic approach, the coupling parameters are not restricted to small values, allowing us to explore regimes of strong effective interactions. In this case, we demonstrate the generation of two-mode squeezed states and derive analytical expressions for the average photon number in each mode. Furthermore, we analyze statistical properties that characterize mixedness and entanglement, including intermode correlations, the Mandel parameter, and the linear entropy. The corresponding Wigner function provides a phase-space visualization of the system's behavior, illustrating the connection between cavity motion, mode coupling, and nonclassical field correlations via the underlying symplectic algebra.

</details>


### [117] [Receiver-Constrained Recovery Placement for Encoded-Qutrit Teleportation under Monitored Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15317)
*Yifan Shi,Duan Wang,Quanzhen Ding,He Zhang,Da-Wei Luo,Ting Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究在受监测衰减下编码qutrit隐形传态的恢复放置问题，刻画仅在编码支撑上反转no-jump映射的成功优势，比较两类接收端能力，并分析中间发送端恢复是否有用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在受监测衰减（monitored decay）条件下，编码qutrit隐形传态中恢复操作应放置于发送端还是接收端，以及保真度约束的不同含义如何决定中间发送端恢复的有效性。

Method: 采用概率测量反转理论，构造限制在编码支撑上的映射构建方法（适用于一般有限维支撑）；对相干V型发送端、Λ型接收端及S={0,2}给出显式时间结果。情形一：单一结果无关的全空间接收端滤波器，在固定总时间下以最小成功加权条件保真度为约束最大化成功概率，求解单一预设恢复时间与随机化恢复时间两类优化。情形二：采用Bell结果依赖的非幺正接收端恢复，对每个被接受的全列秩转移分支进行精确反转。

Result: 单一预设恢复时间下最多出现三种时长依赖情形：无发送端反转、最早可行的中间反转、终点反转。若恢复时间可独立于输入随机化且仅要求汇集的成功系综满足保真度，则最优时间分布在ΓT ≥ (ln3)/2时混合两个端点。Bell结果依赖恢复下，每个被接受的全列秩分支可被精确反转；但在同一模型与发送端操作族下，省略发送端反转对任意有限T>0（正衰减率）唯一最大化精确隐形传态成功概率。部分路径相干性可区分相对支撑增益与绝对恢复成功：长时暗态平台仅在完全相干衰减下存续。

Conclusion: 接收端控制能力与保真度约束的含义共同决定中间发送端恢复是否有用；恢复放置的最优策略取决于接收端可用的恢复类型及保真度要求所对应的系综语义。

Abstract: Using established probabilistic measurement-reversal theory, we study recovery placement in encoded-qutrit teleportation under monitored decay. We characterize the success advantage of reversing a no-jump map only on the encoded support and compare two receiver capabilities. The restricted-map construction applies to general finite-dimensional supports; explicit timing results are obtained for a coherent V-type sender, a $Λ$-type receiver, and $S=\{0,2\}$, within a prescribed family of maximal exact sender reversals. With one outcome-independent full-space receiver filter, we maximize success probability at fixed total time subject to a minimum success-weighted conditional fidelity. A single preassigned recovery time yields up to three duration-dependent regimes: no sender reversal, the earliest feasible intermediate reversal, and final-time reversal. If recovery times may be randomized independently of the input and only the pooled successful ensemble must meet the fidelity requirement, the optimum over all such timing distributions mixes the two endpoints for $ΓT \ge (\ln 3)/2$. With Bell-outcome-dependent nonunitary receiver recovery, each accepted full-column-rank transfer branch can instead be reversed exactly. For the same model and sender-operation family, omitting sender reversal uniquely maximizes the exact-teleportation success probability for every finite $T>0$ at positive decay rate. Partial pathway coherence further distinguishes relative support gain from absolute recovery success: the long-time dark-state plateau survives only for fully coherent decay. These results show how receiver control capabilities and the meaning of the fidelity constraint determine whether intermediate sender recovery is useful.

</details>


### [118] [Measuring higher-order bulk topology in a programmable quantum simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16046)
*Pratik Sathe,Niclas Heinsdorf,Majid Kheirkhah,Rahul Deshpande,Fabio Altomare,Andrew J. Berkley,Martin Dehn,Richard Harris,Emile Hoskinson,Stefan Paul,Gabriel Poulin-Lamarre,Jack Raymond,Chris Rich,Mohammad H. Amin,Marcel Franz,Andrew D. King*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用超导模拟-数字量子模拟器（最多728量子比特）实现并探测一维到三维的高阶拓扑绝缘体，通过实空间测量重构传播子，实验提取体拓扑不变量，包括由三层嵌套Wilson环得到的三维八极矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑绝缘体的定义性质编码于量子态的全局结构中，而非局域序参量和自发对称破缺。晶体高阶拓扑绝缘体可在二维及以上维度承载局域角模。虽然这些边界模式可通过局域态密度进行谱学探测，但如何通过嵌套Wilson环等方式实验确定其体拓扑，仍是一项未解决的实验挑战。

Method: 在超导模拟-数字量子模拟器中使用最多728个量子比特，实现由有效紧束缚哈密顿量支配的一维、二维和三维拓扑绝缘体层级体系；通过实空间测量重构复传播子，进行边界与体的互补表征。

Result: 局域态密度揭示出局域化的边界模式；实验重构的能带投影算子给出体拓扑不变量；并最终通过三层嵌套Wilson环提取出三维八极矩。

Conclusion: 该工作为实现、表征和操控可编程三维量子物质中拓扑保护态建立了途径。

Abstract: Topological insulators lie outside the standard Landau paradigm: Instead of being characterized by local order parameters and spontaneous symmetry breaking, their defining properties are encoded in the global structure of their quantum states and manifested as robust boundary states. Crystalline higher-order topological insulators can extend this principle by hosting localized corner modes in two and higher dimensions. Although these boundary modes can be detected spectroscopically through the local density of states, determining the underlying bulk topology, for example through nested Wilson loops, remains an outstanding experimental challenge. Here we use up to 728 qubits in a superconducting analogue-digital quantum simulator to realize and probe a hierarchy of one-, two-, and three-dimensional topological insulators governed by effective tight-binding Hamiltonians. By reconstructing complex propagators from real-space measurements, we obtain complementary boundary and bulk characterizations: local densities of states reveal localized boundary modes, whereas experimentally reconstructed band projectors yield bulk topological invariants, culminating in a three-dimensional octupole moment extracted through third nested Wilson loops. These results establish a route to realizing, characterizing, and manipulating topologically protected states in programmable three-dimensional quantum matter.

</details>


### [119] [Criticality Concentrates Extensive Sensing Information into a Local Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15364)
*Peng Wang,Yu-Xuan Zhang,Hai-Tao Ding,Leong-Chuan Kwek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明临界性可将大量传感信息浓缩到局部轨迹中，并给出稀疏读出下信息保持的理论界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全对全耗散反馈网络通常被认为依赖全局记录才能获得集体传感信息，作者希望探究单条局部二元轨迹能否在临界性下保留与全记录相当的信息量，并澄清临界慢化是否破坏该增益。

Method: 在快速读出极限下，分析全对全耗散反馈网络对共同弱场的Fisher信息率；比较单条二元轨迹与全记录的Fisher信息率随N的标度；通过解析界与稀疏读出定理刻画分数信息损失随读出轨迹数k的衰减。

Result: 单条二元轨迹对共同弱场的Fisher信息率随N线性增长，与全记录相同；临界慢化不消除该增益，因为慢模局部权重随N下降；解析界保证局部记录在全部信道参数范围内保留超过一半全信息；稀疏读出定理给出分数信息损失按k^{-1}衰减，仅需Θ(ε^{-1})条轨迹即可保留1-ε的全信息率；任何增长的读出集即使密度趋于零也渐近恢复全共同场Fisher信息率。

Conclusion: 临界性使局部读出即可高效获取集体传感信息，稀疏甚至消失密度的读出集仍能渐近达到全信息率，为设计局部、稀疏传感读出方案提供理论依据。

Abstract: We show that criticality concentrates extensive sensing information into a local trajectory. In the rapid-readout limit of an all-to-all dissipative feedback network, a single binary trajectory has a Fisher-information rate linear in $N$ for a common weak field, as does the full record. Critical slowing does not remove this gain because the local weight of the slow mode decreases with $N$. An analytic bound guarantees that the local record retains more than half of the full information across the entire channel-parameter range.
