We also identify how parameter drives and observation dynamics determine local access to collective information beyond permutation symmetry. Furthermore, our sparse-readout theorem establishes a $k^{-1}$ decay of fractional information loss: only $Θ(\varepsilon^{-1})$ trajectories are needed to retain a fraction $1-\varepsilon$ of the full information rate. Any growing readout set, even of vanishing density, asymptotically recovers the full common-field Fisher-information rate.

</details>


### [120] [Imaging the topological sign of a Dirac mass with spin-polarized tunneling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17464)
*Payman Kazemikhah,Ruolun Zhang,Byunghoon Kim,Xiangyu Luo,Zachary Ducorsky,Yu-Peng Zhu,Md. Shafayat Hossain,M. Zahid Hasan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出局域测定磁拓扑表面 Dirac 质量符号 $M$ 的方法：利用 $C_3$ 对称轴上自旋分辨隧道谱的隧穿不对称性直接读出占据带 Berry 曲率符号，并以零级朗道能级、相反畴及手性畴壁模作为独立与自洽验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dirac 质量不仅由大小 $|M|$ 决定，其符号还决定 Berry 曲率、Hall 响应与手性边界模传播方向；然而现有磁拓扑表面的局域谱学手段只能获取能隙大小 $|M|$，无法确定 $M$ 的拓扑符号。

Method: 首先厘清自旋分辨隧穿可获取的符号信息及确定绝对 Hall 符号所需参考。针对自旋—轨道纠缠通常掩盖"测量自旋—Dirac 赝自旋"对应关系的困难，利用晶体旋转对称性在带边消解歧义：在 $C_3$ 轴上，轴向针尖耦合到完全自旋极化态，其隧穿不对称性直接揭示占据带 Berry 曲率的符号，且与轨道组分无关。另提出用非极化针尖测量零级朗道能级以独立获取质量符号，并借助相反畴及其手性畴壁模进行自洽检验。

Result: 证明 $C_3$ 轴上的自旋分辨隧穿不对称性可独立于轨道组分直接给出占据带 Berry 曲率（即质量）符号；零级朗道能级提供了无需自旋极化针尖的独立质量符号测量；相反畴与手性畴壁模可作为自洽性校验。

Conclusion: 该工作使磁拓扑材料中的拓扑表征从"仅识别能隙出现位置"推进到"成像拓扑性质发生改变的位置"，为局域探测质量符号及其空间分布提供了可行路径。

Abstract: A Dirac mass is characterized not only by its magnitude but also by its sign, which influences the Berry curvature, Hall response, and the propagation direction of chiral boundary modes. However, local spectroscopy of magnetic topological surfaces has so far accessed only the gap magnitude $|M|$. Here we show how the topological sign of $M$ can be determined locally. We first establish the sign information that spin-resolved tunneling can access and the reference required to obtain an absolute Hall sign. Although spin--orbital entanglement generally obscures the relation between measured spin and Dirac pseudospin, crystalline rotation symmetry alleviates this ambiguity at the band edge. On a $C_3$ axis, an axial tip couples to a fully spin-polarized state whose tunneling asymmetry directly reveals the sign of the occupied-band Berry curvature, independent of orbital composition. We further show that the zeroth Landau level provides an independent mass-sign measurement with an unpolarized tip, while opposite domains and their chiral domain-wall modes provide self-consistency checks. These results pave the way for imaging the locations where the topological character changes within a material, rather than merely identifying where a spectral gap appears.

</details>


### [121] [Genuine Information Backflow Requires Noncommuting Initial System States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15430)
*Abdullah Alsharari,Francesco Buscemi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将"信息回流"判定框架从可任意选取初态推广到受限初态族，证明：只有当可用初态族包含不对易态时，信息复苏才可能标志真正的环境记忆；若所有可用态对易（如经典随机过程），任何复苏均为"外在的"（由惰性自由度噪声关联引起），不能用作为回流证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKLS方程描述的马尔可夫半群动力学中信息不会复苏，而信息复苏通常被解读为环境回流（非马尔可夫性）的标志。但近期工作表明，复苏也可能源于与系统初态制备无关的噪声关联，即由从不与系统相互作用的惰性自由度携带的"外在"复苏（PRX Quantum 6, 020316 (2025)）。然而该框架假设可获取任意初态，这与物理约束或有限实验控制常不相容，因此"哪些可用初态性质是信息复苏认证真实回流的必要条件"尚不明确。

Method: 将既有"外在/内禀复苏"判定框架推广到受限初态族：不再要求对任意初态可及，而是仅基于实验上可用的初始态集合来刻画复苏，并分析该集合的代数性质（对易性/非对易性）与惰性扩展可解释性之间的关系。

Result: 真实回流仅当可用初态族包含非对易态时才可能发生。若所有可用态彼此对易（类似经典随机过程情形），则任一信息复苏都可由适当的惰性扩展解释，因而属于外在复苏，不构成环境记忆的操作性证据。

Conclusion: 可用系统初态的非对易性，是信息复苏能够作为环境记忆操作性标志的必要条件。

Abstract: The Gorini-Kossakowski-Lindblad-Sudarshan (GKLS) equation, a cornerstone of open-system theory, describes Markovian semigroup dynamics in which information about the initial preparation cannot revive. Beyond this regime, information revivals are widely interpreted as backflow from a non-Markovian environment. However, a decrease followed by an increase in information retrievable from the system need not imply that information has returned from the environment [PRX Quantum 6, 020316 (2025)]. It may instead arise from noise correlations unrelated to the system's initial preparation, carried by inert degrees of freedom that never interact with the system and therefore cannot mediate backflow. Such revivals are called extrinsic; genuine backflow is reserved for revivals that cannot be explained this way. The framework developed in that work, however, assumes access to arbitrary initial system states, an assumption often incompatible with physical constraints or limited experimental control. This leaves open which properties of the available preparations are necessary for an information revival to certify genuine backflow. Here we extend the framework to allow the description of restricted families of initial states and show that genuine backflow is possible only if the family contains noncommuting states. If all available states commute, as in a classical stochastic process, every revival can be explained by a suitable inert extension and is therefore extrinsic. Noncommutativity of the available system states is thus necessary for information revivals to provide an operational signature of environmental memory.

</details>


### [122] [Breakdown of thermalization in quantum spin systems with long-range interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15530)
*Filippo Caleca,Fabio Mezzacapo,Tommaso Roscilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 长程铁磁相互作用的自旋系统在有限负能量密度下不能热化到顺磁态，破坏了本征态热化假说，且长时非热态具有长程多体纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为非可积系统在有限能量密度下会发生量子热化，局域性质沿哈密顿量演化弛豫到热力学值。本文旨在探究具备长程铁磁相互作用的自旋系统是否仍遵循这一普适预期，揭示热化失效的机制。

Method: 研究自旋系统：至少一个自旋分量通过长程铁磁相互作用耦合，其强度按幂律衰减 ∼r^{-α}（α<d，d为空间维度），另一个自旋分量与场耦合。分析其热力学性质及本征态特征，考察有限负能量密度下能否热化至顺磁态，并结合本征态典型性与本征态热化假说进行论证。

Result: 当系统具有有限负能量密度时，一般不能热化到顺磁态。热化缺失对无穷多哈密顿量普遍成立，且与可积性无关；其根源在于顺磁相热力学的反常特征无法反映在本征态性质中，导致本征态典型性和本征态热化假说失效。动力学长时出现的非热态表现出长程多体纠缠。

Conclusion: 长程铁磁相互作用可导致非可积系统的热化破坏，这源于热力学与 eigenstate 性质间的失配；非热态的长程多体纠缠将热化破坏转化为一种资源。

Abstract: Quantum thermalization of closed systems -- i.e. the relaxation of local properties to their thermodynamic value along the Hamiltonian evolution -- is generically expected in non-integrable systems at finite energy density. In this work we show that quantum spin systems with at least one spin component coupled through long-range ferromagnetic interactions, and a second spin component coupled to a field, cannot in general thermalize to a paramagnetic state whenever they have a finite, negative energy per spin. The long-range interactions we consider decay as a power law of the distance, $\sim r^{-α}$ with $α< d$, where $d$ is the number of spatial dimensions of the system. The lack of thermalization is generic to an infinite family of Hamiltonians, and it is not associated to any form of integrability. It can instead be understood by the anomalous features of the thermodynamics of the system in the paramagnetic phase, which cannot be reflected in the properties of the eigenstates. This leads to a breakdown of eigenstate typicality and of the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis. Interestingly, the non-thermal states emerging at long times in the dynamics display long-range multipartite entanglement, elevating the breakdown of thermalization to a resource.

</details>


### [123] [Emergent Non-Biorthogonal Born Rule at Dynamical Quantum Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17855)
*Wei-Wei Yang,Henning Schomerus,Jiangping Hu,Zhesen Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文从全局厄米系统与标准Born规则出发，将动力学量子相变（DQPT）从参考态推广到物理子空间，为左右本征态赋予清晰概率解释，并提出由"涌现非双正交Born规则"支配的新现象及例外点DQPT（EP-DQPT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米动力学具有不同的左、右本征态，但其归一化自由度导致缺乏直接的概率解释。作者希望在不放弃厄米量子力学基础的前提下，为非厄米量子力学的概率解释建立可靠依据。

Method: 以标准Born规则下的全局厄米系统为起点，将DQPT由参考态推广至物理子空间；将每个态对自身归一化，用左右态之间的互重叠定义可直接测量的动力学转变率，并分析由该"涌现非双正交Born规则"支配的新现象，识别出临界传播子本征态合并的例外点DQPT（EP-DQPT）。

Result: 当左右态各自归一化后，其互重叠确定了一个可直接获取的动力学转变率；在该新兴非双正交Born规则下出现新现象；并识别出一类EP-DQPT，其特征为临界传播子本征态发生合并。

Conclusion: 该理论将非厄米量子力学的概率解释牢固地建立在厄米量子力学基础之上。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian dynamics possesses distinct left and right eigenstates, but their normalization freedom obscures a direct probabilistic interpretation.
  Starting from a globally Hermitian system governed by the standard Born rule, we generalize dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPTs) from a reference state to a physical subspace.
  This generalization provides a clear probabilistic interpretation of left and right states: when each state is normalized to itself, their mutual overlap determines a directly accessible dynamical transition rate.
  We discuss the new phenomena governed by this emergent non-biorthogonal Born rule and further identify a class of exceptional-point DQPTs (EP-DQPTs), at which the eigenstates of the critical propagator coalesce.
  Our theory places the probabilistic interpretation of non-Hermitian quantum mechanics on the firm footing of Hermitian quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [124] [Riemannian Formulation of Sample-Based Quantum Krylov Solvers for Elliptic Problems: Energy-Norm Guarantees and Duct-Flow Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15586)
*Doyeol "David" Ahn,Byeongyong Park*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文为椭圆偏微分方程产生的正定系统开发了基于采样的量子线性求解方法：采样量子Krylov态并以固定基下的采样集重构解，给出误差界、样本复杂度、残差证书与支撑完备性定理，并结合Riemannian截面、对称性与几何扰动分析，在仿真和六比特离子阱上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于采样的量子线性求解器主要面向一般线性系统，其采样保证与重构方式对椭圆偏微分方程（PDE）离散后产生的正定系统并非专门设计；同时缺乏针对该问题类的严格误差界、可计算证书与几何/对称性结构分析，也缺少对采样效率与量子硬件噪声影响的定量评估。

Method: 1) 证明在任意固定基（只要存在高效变换）下采样保证成立；2) 用采样集上的Galerkin求解替代变分重构；3) 推导期望与高概率能量范数误差界、基于本质集最小采样概率的样本复杂度、可计算残差证书，以及退相位结果分布的支撑完备性定理；4) 针对Riemannian截面上的椭圆问题建立公式：二维共形坐标下刚度矩阵与度量无关、弯曲管道对应同一矩阵类，用Killing对称性给出测量结果的选律，用度量的Lie导数给出几何扰动的一阶界；5) 区分算子近似（可界定影响）与采样器近似（影响覆盖率但不影响目标）；6) 以椭圆、透镜形与弯曲管道中脉动流的隐式步为算例，进行最多12量子比特仿真与六比特离子阱实验。

Result: 仿真（最多12量子比特）：达到1%误差所需Fourier模式数从14增至60，而未知数从64增至4096；在所研究的所有情形中，经典的残差贪心选择均比量子采样更高效，与实测低采样效率一致。六比特离子阱处理器上：实测结果分布符合替换噪声模型，其强度约每两个量子比特门增长0.6%，且奇偶选律按预测速率剔除shots。

Conclusion: 该方法为椭圆PDE正定系统提供了带理论保证的采样式量子线性求解与Galerkin重构框架，涵盖误差界、样本复杂度、残差证书和支撑完备性，并融入Riemannian几何、对称性与扰动分析；但实验与仿真表明，在现有条件下经典残差贪心选择仍优于量子采样，量子硬件的替换噪声与低采样效率是主要限制因素。

Abstract: Sample-based quantum linear solvers measure quantum Krylov states $e^{-iAT}|b\rangle$ and reconstruct the solution of $Ax=b$ classically on the observed basis states. We develop this approach for positive-definite systems that arise from elliptic partial differential equations. We show that the sampling guarantees hold in any fixed basis with an efficient transform. We then replace the variational reconstruction by a Galerkin solve on the sampled set. For this reconstruction we derive expected and high-probability energy-norm error bounds, a sample complexity in terms of the smallest sampling probability on an essential set, a computable residual certificate, and a support-completeness theorem for the dephased outcome law. We formulate the method for elliptic problems on Riemannian cross sections. In two dimensions the stiffness matrix is metric-independent in conformal coordinates, and curved vessels lead to the same matrix class. Killing symmetries of the cross section give selection rules for the measurement outcomes, and the Lie derivative of the metric gives first-order bounds for geometric perturbations. We separate operator approximations, whose effect we bound, from sampler approximations, which affect coverage but not the target. As examples we treat the implicit step of pulsatile flow in elliptic, lens-shaped, and curved ducts. In simulations of up to 12 qubits, the number of Fourier modes required for 1% error grows from 14 to 60 while the number of unknowns grows from 64 to 4096. A classical residual-greedy selection is nevertheless more efficient than quantum sampling in all cases studied, consistent with the low measured sampling efficiency. On a six-qubit trapped-ion processor, the measured outcome laws follow a replacement-noise model whose strength grows by about 0.6% per two-qubit gate, and a parity selection rule rejects shots at the predicted rate.

</details>


### [125] [A Quantum Analogue of the Covering Lemma: Limits of Exact Anonymous Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15601)
*Giuseppe Di Molfetta,Petra Camilli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明量子干涉虽使经典"覆盖引理"失效，但匿名网络中精确计数的不可能性依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典不可能性证明依赖覆盖引理：一个执行可在局部结构相同的更大网络上复现。量子干涉使该论证失效——某网络上可能的测量记录在覆盖网络上可能变得不可能。因此需要回答：量子通信能否在无规模上界时确定匿名网络规模？

Method: 证明量子版覆盖引理，保留"局部可能性"而非"完整执行"：在循环覆盖族中，节点处任何局部测量结果在足够大的对应节点上仍可能，即使在信息绕小网络传播一周、简单局域论证失效之后。进一步推广到通用覆盖树和具有足够长环的有限覆盖。证明结合张量网络方法与图覆盖：跨网络规模的共同表示将归一化转化为对哪些局域结果可出现的约束。

Result: 对节点独立制备的均匀同步协议，该支撑提升原理排除了零错误计数，也排除了在匿名环及其他循环覆盖族上产生有保证的有限规模上界；同时排除在这些族上以概率1有限时间终止的精确领导者选举。允许测量、经典控制与随时间增长的局部记忆，且不假设预定运行时间上界。

Conclusion: 量子干涉击败了执行提升，但未能实现网络规模的无误认证。

Abstract: Can quantum communication determine the size of an anonymous network when no size bound is known? Classical impossibility proofs rely on the covering lemma: an execution can be reproduced on a larger network with the same local structure. Quantum interference invalidates this argument - a measurement record possible on a network can become impossible on a cover. We show that the impossibility of exact counting nevertheless survives. Our main result is a quantum analogue of the covering lemma that preserves local possibilities rather than complete executions. Within a cyclic covering family, every local measurement outcome possible at a node remains possible at a corresponding node in every sufficiently large network. This holds even after information has travelled around the smaller network, beyond the reach of a simple locality argument. We extend the principle to the universal covering tree and finite covers with sufficiently long cycles. The proof combines tensor-network methods with graph coverings: a common representation across network sizes turns normalization into a constraint on which local outcomes can occur. For uniform synchronous protocols with independently prepared nodes, this support-lifting principle rules out zero-error counting and even the production of a guaranteed finite size bound on anonymous rings and the other cyclic covering families considered here. It also rules out exact leader election with termination in finite time with probability one on these families. Measurements, classical control, and local memory growing with time are allowed; no predetermined running-time bound is assumed. Quantum interference thus defeats execution lifting without enabling infallible certification of network size.

</details>


### [126] [Non-Stationary Quantum Systems Associated with Stationary Scattering in One Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15655)
*Ali Mostafazadeh,Alireza Shariati Joni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一维稳态散射的动力学表述：将散射数据提取等效非幺正二能级量子系统的演化算符，并用Bloch球轨迹与量子测量解读反射/透射振幅，无反射、谱奇点与相干完美吸收分别对应极点循环演化与自旋翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳态散射问题通常以静态波动方程求解，作者希望建立其动力学（非幺正量子系统演化）形式，并探讨该等效系统能否物理实现，从而把散射信息与量子测量结果联系起来。

Method: 聚焦一维稳态散射，散射数据为反射与透射振幅；构造等效的非幺正二能级系统，将其态映射到Bloch球S^2上的点，通过演化轨迹与量子测量分析反射振幅，并把特殊散射现象对应到极点循环演化与自旋翻转。

Result: 量子测量能够提供关于反射振幅的信息；无反射构型对应S^2一个或两个极点的循环演化；谱奇点和相干完美吸收对应将一个极点映射到另一个极点的动力学自旋翻转。

Conclusion: 该工作给出散射问题的动力学/量子测量框架，揭示了散射特征量与Bloch球极点动力学（循环演化、自旋翻转）的对应关系，为散射信息的量子读取提供数学方法。

Abstract: Stationary scattering admits a dynamical formulation in which the scattering data are extracted from the evolution operator for an effective non-stationary and non-unitary quantum system. This provides a mathematical method of solving scattering problems. We consider the possibility of realizing such a quantum system and relating the scattering information to outcomes of quantum measurements performed on it. To this end, we examine stationary scattering problems in one dimension where the scattering data consist of reflection and transmission amplitudes and the corresponding effective quantum system is a non-unitary two-level system. The states of this system identify points in a Bloch sphere $S^2$ and their evolution yields trajectories in $S^2$. Quantum measurements turn out to be capable of providing information about the reflection amplitudes. We identify configurations displaying reflectionlessness with situations where one or both of the poles of $S^2$ undergo cyclic evolutions. Spectral singularities and coherent perfect absorption correspond to dynamical spin flips which map one of the poles of $S^2$ to the other.

</details>


### [127] [Separability criteria and lower bounds of entanglement via realignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15664)
*Yuhang Xie,Yanjun Chu,Shao-Ming Fei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于密度矩阵重排与约化密度矩阵矢量化，构造了一族增强的可分性判据，可恢复并优于以往基于重排的判据，并给出更紧的纠缠度量下界及真正多体纠缠判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠的检测与量化是量子信息科学的核心问题，现有基于重排的可分性判据检测能力有限，需要更强、适用范围更广的判据与更紧的纠缠度量下界。

Method: 利用密度矩阵的重排（realignment）以及约化密度矩阵的矢量化（vectorization），构造双体与多体量子系统的一族增强可分性判据。

Result: 新判据将以往基于重排的判据作为特例包含，且能检测出更多纠缠；同时导出更紧的concurrence与凸顶扩展负性（convex-roof extended negativity）下界、真正三分体纠缠的检测判据，以及真正多体纠缠concurrence的下界，并通过具体实例验证其优势。

Conclusion: 所提增强可分性判据与纠缠度量下界在检测与量化纠缠方面优于既有方法，具有更广的适用性和更强的纠缠识别能力。

Abstract: The detection and quantification of quantum entanglement are central problems in quantum information science. Based on the realignment of density matrices and the vectorization of reduced density matrices, we construct a family of enhanced separability criteria for bipartite and multipartite quantum systems. Our criteria recover the previously proposed realignment-based criteria as special cases and detect more entanglement than the earlier ones. Moreover, we derive tighter lower bounds for the concurrence and the convex-roof extended negativity, criteria for detecting the genuine tripartite entanglement, and lower bounds on genuine-multipartite-entanglement concurrence. The advantages of our results are demonstrated with detailed examples.

</details>


### [128] [Molecular Vibronic Complexity as a Quantum Resource for Optimal Control: Nonadiabatic Mixing Creates Protected Coherence Subspaces in Dissipative Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15743)
*Elisa Palacino-González*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文揭示分子电子相干寿命在很大程度上由被激发的相干模式决定，存在被“暗态”保护的、皮秒量级长寿命振动-电子相干子空间；使用GRAPE最优控制可在无先验信息下找到并利用该模式，使800 fs时刻的相干度比高斯脉冲提升两个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为分子中电子相干在数百飞秒内衰减，且归因于环境（浴）作用。作者质疑该时间尺度是否仅由环境决定，提出相干寿命可能取决于“激发的是哪些相干模式”，即分子内部存在浴几乎“看不见”的相干，动机是寻找并利用这类受保护的长寿命、可控相干，作为分子量子信息科学的资源。

Method: 构建含非绝热特征的三态模型（电子基态、光学亮激发态与暗态），在低/高非绝热混合两个区制及不同系统-浴耦合强度下，采用基于梯度的最优控制（GRAPE）结合Redfield动力学，最大化电子基态与光学亮激发态之间的总体振动-电子相干（vibronic coherence）；并用Liouvillian谱分析相干模式的衰变特征，与高斯脉冲进行等通量对比。

Result: Liouvillian谱在两种区制下均显示出与其余模式良好分离的相干模式，具有显著的基态-亮态特征，衰减时间为皮秒量级；该模式通过与暗态混合而受保护——这种混合使光学可及激发态的几何构型接近基态，从而减弱其与浴的耦合。GRAPE无需先验信息即可找到该模式，并使光激发波包保持在Franck-Condon区域附近。等通量条件下，在弱耦合时800 fs处相干度比高斯脉冲提高两个数量级以上，在强耦合时提升更多；Liouvillian对长寿命相干浴依赖性的预测误差在1%以内；调谐至该模式频率的高斯脉冲能达到最优脉冲效果的10%以内。

Conclusion: 研究超越二能级与自旋系统，提出利用分子的振动-电子结构构建受保护的相干子空间，识别出暗态保护机制并给出基于分子参数的设计准则，为将振动-电子分子系统作为长寿命、可控相干的资源用于分子量子信息科学奠定基础。

Abstract: In molecules, electronic coherence typically decays within a few hundred femtoseconds, a timescale usually attributed to the environment. We show that it is set to a large extent by which coherences are excited: the molecule can host coherences that the bath barely sees, and optimal control can find and exploit them. Using gradient-based optimal control (GRAPE) with Redfield dynamics, we maximize the total vibronic coherence between the electronic ground and an optically bright electronic excited state in a three-state model with nonadiabatic features, in two regimes of low and high nonadiabatic mixing and for different system-bath coupling strengths. The Liouvillian spectrum reveals, in both regimes, a well-separated coherence mode with substantial ground-bright character that decays on a picosecond timescale. This mode is protected by mixing with the dark state, which brings the geometry of the optically accessible excited state close to that of the ground state and thus weakens its coupling to the bath. Without prior information, GRAPE finds this mode and keeps the photoexcited wave packet near the Franck-Condon region. At equal fluence, it increases the coherence at 800 fs by more than two orders of magnitude over a Gaussian pulse at the weakest coupling, and by many more at stronger coupling. The Liouvillian predicts the bath dependence of the long-lived coherence to within 1\%, and a Gaussian pulse tuned to the mode frequency comes within 10\% of the best pulse found. Beyond two-level and spin systems, our study puts forward the vibronic structure of molecules as a route to protected coherence subspaces, identifying a dark-state protection mechanism and a design criterion based on molecular parameters. These results lay the groundwork for our goal of using vibronic molecular systems as a resource for long-lived, controllable coherences in molecular quantum information science.

</details>


### [129] [Two-axis twisting by two-photon exchange: rate, depth, and the self-Kerr constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15774)
*Qing Yu,Ying Li,Cuilu Zhai,Shiqing Tang,Wangjun Lu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究双模玻色子对交换在真实非线性介质中与双轴扭曲等效性的保持程度，给出控制有效克尔强度与交换强度之比的独立判据（速率与深度），并揭示短时数据提取速率的陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双模间的成对量子相干交换在数值因子意义下等价于能实现海森堡标度压缩的双轴扭曲生成元。作者希望确定这种等效性在真实非线性介质中能保留多少，因为真实介质中交换项总伴随局域克尔非线性。

Method: 在固定粒子数子空间内，将哈密顿量化为仅依赖有效克尔强度与交换强度之比的单一无量纲参数；解析推导压缩生成速率（闭合形式）与可达到压缩深度两个判据，并用闭合形式高斯动力学进行分析。

Result: 速率判据呈闭合形式，两强度幅值相等时恰好为零，并随失谐量以平方根形式恢复；深度判据仅取决于平均场鞍点的各向异性，只有各向同性鞍点能达到完整的双轴扭曲值，且与速率无关（相同各向异性但速率相差较大的参数组给出的深度逐位一致）。从短时数据提取速率会高估超过两倍，除非鞍点为各向同性。各向同性三次非线性使速率消失于特定比值，需失谐才能实现对交换的双轴扭曲，适度失配即可恢复几乎全部最优深度。

Conclusion: 该约束此前未显现的原因在于：两个量子时自克尔对两个耦合态施加相等位移，故两个扭曲哈密顿量严格不可区分，简并直到四个量子才被解除。

Abstract: The coherent exchange of pairs of quanta between two bosonic modes is equivalent, up to a numerical factor, to the generator of two-axis twisting, which attains Heisenberg scaling of the squeezing; we determine how much of that equivalence survives in a realistic nonlinear medium. In any such medium the exchange term is accompanied by a local Kerr nonlinearity, and within a sector of fixed particle number the Hamiltonian depends on a single dimensionless ratio of the effective Kerr strength to the exchange strength. We derive two criteria governing this ratio and show that they are independent. The first is the rate at which squeezing is generated: the exponential rate follows in closed form, vanishes exactly when the two strengths are equal in magnitude, and recovers as the square root of the detuning from that point. The second is the depth to which squeezing can be driven: at fixed particle number the attainable depth is a function of the anisotropy of the mean-field saddle alone, reaches the full two-axis-twisting value only for the isotropic saddle, and is independent of the rate, parameter sets sharing the same anisotropy but differing in rate by a sizeable factor giving depths that agree to every printed digit. The closed-form Gaussian dynamics reveals a pitfall: extracting rates from short-time data overestimates them by more than a factor of two unless the saddle is isotropic. An isotropic cubic nonlinearity imposes the value of the ratio where the rate vanishes, so realizing two-axis twisting by pair exchange requires detuning it; a moderate mismatch already recovers almost all of the optimal depth. We also explain why the constraint has not surfaced before: at two quanta the self-Kerr shifts the two coupled states equally, so the two twisting Hamiltonians are rigorously indistinguishable, the degeneracy being lifted only from four quanta.

</details>


### [130] [Multipolar Nanophotonics with Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16062)
*Alex Krasnok*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出用量子比特表示纳米颗粒的多极光响应，并通过量子电路计算散射方向性与Purcell效应，在理想模拟器及IBM、IonQ真机上验证，结果与经典理论吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米天线阵列、超表面等纳米光子系统的仿真成本随规模和精度要求急剧上升，需要更高效的响应计算方法。

Method: 将颗粒的多极响应映射为量子比特，构建量子电路计算散射与Purcell效应；电路可编码已知多极，或由颗粒表面的Maxwell方程推断未知多极；并建立了多极数目与精度对量子比特及测量需求的关系。

Result: 在理想量子模拟器及IBM、IonQ真机上对照经典理论：半径78 nm硅纳米颗粒在679 nm照射下，IonQ给出前向指向性2.984，接近理想偶极值3；距金纳米颗粒10 nm、发射波长510 nm的发射体，IBM测得总衰减率为真空值的20.48倍，理论为20.47，并做了量子态读取误差修正。

Conclusion: 结果表明量子辅助方法可支撑纳米光子系统中定向辐射与发射体衰减的设计研究。

Abstract: Small particles respond to light through electric and magnetic patterns called multipoles. Their interference determines the direction of scattered light and affects the decay of nearby emitters. Larger nanoantenna arrays, metasurfaces, and other nanophotonic systems demand increasingly costly simulations to meet strict performance and reliability requirements. We show how to represent these responses with quantum bits (qubits) and use quantum circuits to calculate scattering and the Purcell effect, the change in an emitter's decay rate caused by its surroundings. The circuits can encode known multipoles or estimate unknown ones from Maxwell's equations at the particle surface. We test the method against classical theory using an ideal quantum simulator and real IBM and IonQ quantum computers. For a silicon nanoparticle of radius 78 nm illuminated at 679 nm, IonQ gives a forward directivity of 2.984, close to the ideal dipolar value 3, quantifying the concentration of scattered light in the forward direction. For an emitter 10 nm from a gold nanoparticle and emitting at 510 nm, IBM measurements yield a calculated total decay rate 20.48 times its vacuum value versus 20.47 from theory, with correction for errors in reading the qubit states. We also establish how the number of multipoles and the required accuracy set the demands on qubits and measurements. These results motivate quantum-assisted design studies of directional radiation and emitter decay in nanophotonic systems.

</details>


### [131] [Violation of Bell-type Inequality in the Hanbury Brown-Twiss (HBT) Toy-Model: Quantum Correlations beyond Local-Realism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15783)
*Sarbani Chatterjee,Arvind*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个简化的4模式玩具模型，用于捕捉原始8模式HBT干涉仪的核心物理要素。通过定制Bell型不等式检验其关联函数，发现该模型违反不等式，表明其关联具有非局域性，无法用经典隐变量描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始HBT效应表明强度关联可揭示光的量子特性，但8模式HBT干涉仪结构解析复杂度高。作者希望找到一个既能保留核心物理要素、又能大幅降低分析复杂度的简化模型，并检验其是否能展现真正的非经典行为。

Method: 基于原始8模式HBT干涉仪的结构，发展出一个4模式玩具模型；针对该模型特定的测量配置，构建并应用相应的Bell型不等式，以检验其关联函数是否具有非经典性。

Result: 该玩具模型所产生的关联违反了所构建的Bell型不等式，说明其中存在与任何经典隐变量描述不相容的非局域特征。

Conclusion: 结果表明，简化模型在揭示光学系统中非经典性的基本方面具有重要价值，玩具模型可有效保留HBT干涉的核心量子特征并用于非局域性研究。

Abstract: The Hanbury Brown-Twiss (HBT) effect marked a significant milestone in quantum optics by illustrating that intensity correlations can reveal inherently quantum features of light. Motivated by the structure of the original 8-mode HBT interferometer, we develop a simplified 4-mode toy-model that captures its essential physical ingredients while significantly reducing analytical complexity. Our toy-model provides an intuitive and well-structured framework for examining the desired correlation functions. We investigate whether our toy-model can exhibit genuinely non-classical behaviour by formulating and applying a Bell-type inequality tailored to its specific measurement configuration. Our analysis shows that the correlations generated by the toy-model violate the inequality, indicating the presence of non-local features that are incompatible with any classical hidden-variable description. The results highlight the power of simplified models in uncovering fundamental aspects of non-classicality in optical systems.

</details>


### [132] [Metasurface quantum graphs for polarization-independent certified randomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16286)
*Xing Lin,Wenwen Liu,Wange Song,Sixin Chen,Shuang Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于超表面的量子图（MQG）框架，将多通道纳米光子响应映射为图顶点，用加权图度直接估计条件最小熵，实现与偏振无关的认证随机性；实验制备4/6通道介质超表面实现高维POVM图，在预示单光子和泊松光下成功认证随机性，为集成量子技术提供可扩展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子随机数发生器受限于笨重光学系统、器件缺陷以及态对准约束，难以实现紧凑、稳健且与偏振无关的认证随机性，因此需要一种可将复杂量子测量集成到单层无源器件中的新平台。

Method: 建立定量量子图框架，将多通道纳米光子响应映射到图顶点，从而赋予超表面测量操作性的信息论意义，把加权图度与条件最小熵估计直接关联；据此设计并制备4通道和6通道介质超表面，实现高维正算子值测度（POVM）图，并在预示单光子与泊松光条件下进行随机性认证。

Result: 图认证测量在无需复杂光学系统的条件下，即使在基于纠缠的攻击下仍能产生稳健的私有随机性；实验上4/6通道介质超表面成功实现高维POVM图并完成随机性认证。

Conclusion: 通过将复杂量子测量集成到单个无源层中，超表面量子图为集成量子技术提供了一条可扩展的技术路线。

Abstract: Quantum random number generators are crucial for information security, yet existing implementations are hindered by bulky optics, device imperfections, and state alignment constraints. Here, using a compact metasurface platform, we achieve polarization-independent certified randomness by establishing a quantitative quantum graph framework. By mapping multi-channel nanophotonic responses onto graph vertices, we assign metasurface measurements an operational information-theoretic meaning, directly connecting weighted graph degrees to conditional min-entropy estimation. The resulting graph-certified measurements yield robust private randomness even under entanglement-based attacks without complex optics. Experimentally, we fabricate 4- and 6-channel dielectric metasurfaces to implement high-dimensional positive operator-valued measure graphs, successfully certifying randomness with heralded single photons and Poissonian light. By integrating complex quantum measurements into a single passive layer, our metasurface quantum graphs provide a scalable route for integrated quantum technologies.

</details>


### [133] [Quantum ordinal optimization under a fixed comparison budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15791)
*Hongyu Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将序优化问题形式化为在固定oracle预算下最大化对齐概率，研究了相干成对比较的精确量子情形，给出了经典淘汰赛最优性、量子最大度上界及单次量子比较的精确最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序优化在评估昂贵且预算有限时选择"足够好"的设计，而对齐概率衡量所选集合包含至少规定数量真正优秀设计的概率。现有研究缺乏在该概率目标下经典与量子比较预算效力的系统刻画。

Method: 将量子序优化形式化为固定oracle预算下最大化对齐概率的问题，以预算按比较查询计数的、以一个选定设计为参照的精确相干成对比较为首个实例；对每个经典比较预算小于设计数的情况，证明均匀采样设计的淘汰赛达到最优，样本无放回且比比较次数多一个设计；分析均匀随机顺序下所选设计秩的多项式概率律，并推导最大量子度与经典最大度的关系，进而求出一比较量子最优解。

Result: 对每个小于设计数的经典比较预算，均匀采样淘汰赛达到最优；样本无放回且含比较次数加一个设计；均匀随机顺序下所选设计秩服从多项式概率律。相同比较预算下最大量子度至多为经典最大度的两倍，且当设计数超过比较数两倍时该因子可达，故量子算法能实现经典同预算无法实现的秩律。确定了一比较下对任意设计数和任意足够好集合的精确量子最优。选择n个设计中最佳时，一量子比较的最优成功概率大n时约为3.30/n，而一经典比较为2/n；当设计数超过九时，一量子比较的对齐概率高于两次经典比较。

Conclusion: 量子相干成对比较在固定预算下可超越经典算法：最大量子度可达经典上界两倍，单次量子比较在大n时成功概率约3.30/n（经典为2/n），并在超过九个设计时优于两次经典比较，表明量子资源能实现经典同预算不可达的秩概率律。

Abstract: Ordinal optimization selects designs that are good enough rather than optimal when evaluation is expensive and the budget is small. The alignment probability measures the chance that the selected set contains at least a prescribed number of truly good designs. We formulate quantum ordinal optimization as a class of problems that maximize this probability under a fixed oracle budget. We study exact, coherent pairwise comparisons with one selected design as a first instance, with the budget counted in comparison queries. For every classical comparison budget smaller than the number of designs, we prove that a knockout among uniformly sampled designs attains the optimum. The sample is drawn without replacement and contains one more design than the number of comparisons. For a uniformly random order, the selected design's rank has a polynomial probability law. At the same comparison budget, the maximum quantum degree is at most twice the classical maximum, a factor attained whenever there are more than twice as many designs as comparisons, so a quantum algorithm can realize rank laws that no classical algorithm with the same budget can. We determine the exact quantum optimum with one comparison for every number of designs and every good-enough set. For selecting the best of n designs, the optimal success probability with one quantum comparison is approximately 3.30/n for large n, compared with 2/n for one classical comparison. When the best design is sought, one quantum comparison gives a higher alignment probability than two classical comparisons whenever there are more than nine designs.

</details>


### [134] [Direct polarisation-state reconstruction with a temporal pointer: accessible states, resolution and optimal coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16871)
*Shane P. Gervais,Lambert Giner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对高斯时间指针与任意后选择态，解析推导了弱值直接重构量子态所需的三项关键参数：耦合强度、角分辨率及可用态集合，并给出不可重构态构成的球冠范围与最优耦合条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱测量常用于放大微弱信号、探讨基础问题以及无需层析地表征量子态。在直接态重构中，态矢量的每个振幅正比于一个复弱值，可由后选择脉冲的平均到达时间与平均频率读出。但此类实验需预先确定耦合强度、重构所需角分辨率及方法适用态集合，而这些通常由可用仪器决定而非理论推导，缺乏系统性依据。

Method: 作者对高斯时间指针与任意后选择态进行解析推导，确定上述三个量。通过分析不可重构态在布洛赫球上的几何分布、区分两态所需的最小步长，以及一阶耦合反演中系统误差与统计误差的相互制约关系展开研究。

Result: 不可重构态构成以与后选择态正交的态为中心的球冠，其测地线半角由三个独立阈值中的最大值决定，且不含可调参数。两态可区分当且仅当其差异大于由耦合、入射光子数及弱值沿路径灵敏度共同决定的最小步长。一阶反演下系统误差与统计误差相反，最优耦合随入射光子数的六次方根之倒数变化；有限耦合下的精确反演可消除系统误差，剩余限制在于双质心读出，当振幅低于耦合的四分之一时，两态虽具相同质心对但剖面不同。

Conclusion: 该研究为弱测量直接态重构实验提供了定量设计准则，使实验可在装置搭建前完成参数设定与可行性评估。

Abstract: Weak measurements are used to amplify small signals, to address foundational questions, and to characterise quantum states without tomography. In direct state reconstruction each amplitude of the state vector is proportional to a single complex weak value, which for a temporal pointer is read from the mean arrival time and the mean frequency of the postselected pulse. Such an experiment requires three quantities to be fixed, the coupling, the angular resolution demanded of the reconstruction, and the set of states over which the method remains usable, and these are commonly set by the available apparatus rather than derived. We derive all three for a Gaussian temporal pointer and an arbitrary postselection state. The states that cannot be reconstructed form a spherical cap centred on the state orthogonal to the postselection, whose geodesic half-angle follows from the largest of three independent thresholds and involves no adjustable parameter. Two states are distinguishable only if they differ by more than a minimal step fixed by the coupling, by the number of incident photons and by the sensitivity of the weak value along the path followed. For an inversion at first order in the coupling the systematic and statistical errors are opposed and fix an optimal coupling that varies as the inverse sixth root of the number of incident photons, whereas an exact inversion at finite coupling removes the systematic error, the remaining bound being one on the two-centroid readout rather than on the inversion, since below an amplitude equal to a quarter of the coupling two states give the same pair of centroids while their profiles differ. These results allow a direct reconstruction experiment to be dimensioned before the apparatus is assembled.

</details>


### [135] [End-to-End Fidelity and Rate Analysis for Zero-Added-Loss Multiplexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15879)
*Jeffrey H. Shapiro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对基于相位匹配光谱岛的零附加损耗复用（ZALM）方案进行端到端保真度与速率分析，纳入多对SPDC发射、部分BSM损耗、信道传输损耗、模式转换器损耗与模失配，以及量子存储器加载损耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ZALM可避免其他复用方案的开关损耗；当Sagnac源中的SPDC采用具有相位匹配光谱岛的χ(2)晶体时，可规避传统晶体SPDC经DWDM滤波通道化时出现的预报效率与输出态保真度之间的权衡。然而，此前尚缺乏对该"岛屿式"ZALM在完整链路下的保真度与速率评估。

Method: 建立端到端分析模型，涵盖ZALM量子发射机（QTX）中Sagnac偏振纠缠源的全部阶多对SPDC发射与部分Bell态测量（BSM）损耗；QTX到Alice/Bob量子接收机（QRX）的传播损耗；QRX模式转换器的损耗与模失配；以及Alice/Bob发射型量子存储器的部分BSM加载损耗。

Result: 给出了岛屿式ZALM在包含上述各环节损耗与非理想因素条件下的端到端纠缠保真度与成对分发速率。

Conclusion: 该分析为岛屿式ZALM的实际性能提供了定量评估框架，明确了各类损耗与多对发射对最终保真度和速率的影响，为其在量子网络中的应用评估奠定基础。

Abstract: Zero-added-loss multiplexing (ZALM)~[Phys. Rev. Appl. {\bf 19}, 054029 (2023)] is a midpoint-source scheme for entanglement distribution. Its quantum transmitter (QTX) employs two Sagnac sources of polarization-entangled pairs plus partial Bell-state measurements (BSMs) on their idler outputs to herald entanglement in their signal outputs, thus avoiding the switching losses suffered by other multiplexing approaches. When its Sagnac sources' spontaneous parametric downconverters (SPDCs) utilize $χ^{(2)}$ crystals with phase-matched spectral islands, ZALM avoids the tradeoff between heralding efficiency and output-state fidelity incurred when conventional-crystal SPDCs' outputs are channelized by dense wavelength-division multiplexing filters. This paper reports an end-to-end fidelity and rate analysis for islands-based ZALM that includes: SPDC multipair emissions of all orders and partial-BSM losses in the ZALM QTX; loss in propagation from the ZALM QTX to Alice and Bob's quantum receivers (QRXs); loss and mode mismatch in the mode converters employed at Alice and Bob's QRXs; and loss in the partial-BSM loading of Alice and Bob's emissive quantum memories.

</details>


### [136] [Local-dimension-invariant low-density parity-check stabilizer codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15893)
*Md. Shahinul Islam,Aaryan Reza,Lane G. Gunderman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究LDI（局部维度不变）扩展方法在将量子比特稳定子码转换为qudit稳定子码时的距离保持问题，针对原本需要极大素数局部维度的缺陷，通过对qLDPC码的分析成功降低了该截断值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qudit系统（多于两个基态）中量子信息保护仍是难题，而LDI扩展是将大量qubit稳定子码转为qudit稳定子码的工具，但其距离保持条件通常要求新选择的局部维度为大于某个大截断值的素数，这一缺陷限制了方法的实用性。

Method: 分析大截断值带来的影响，并聚焦于量子低密度奇偶校验（qLDPC）码，通过针对性分析来降低截断值。

Result: 在某些情况下可将截断值降至初始局部维度；对于任意CSS型量子比特衍生码，可降至关于d的单指数级别，且不依赖于逻辑量子比特数k。

Conclusion: 该方法减小了LDI形式在其距离保持条件中的限制性缺陷，使LDI扩展更实用。

Abstract: The protection of quantum information in systems leveraging more than two basis states (qudits) is an ongoing challenge. A tool for transforming the plethora of qubit stabilizer codes into qudit stabilizer codes is the local-dimension-invariant (LDI) extension to the stabilizer formalism. A drawback of the LDI method is that generally in order to ensure the distance of the resultant code is at least preserved the newly selected local-dimension will need to be any prime bigger than a large cutoff value. In this work we consider the repercussions of this large cutoff value, then by focusing on quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes we are able to bring down this cutoff value. Notably, we are able to reduce it in some cases to the initial local-dimension and for arbitrary CSS qubit-derived codes to singly exponential in $d$ with no dependence on the number of logicals $k$. This reduces the size of the caveat in LDI forms in their distance preservation condition.

</details>


### [137] [Ghost unitaries for loss-resilient photonic resource-state generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17300)
*Kevin J. Randles,Manuel H. Muñoz-Arias,Kevin Thompson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出"幽灵幺正"（ghost-unitary）方案：利用多模压缩真空的转置恒等式，将资源生成干涉仪作用于预告（heralding）模式而非资源模式，把干涉损耗导致的直接振幅阻尼转化为需要高阶对发射与光子损耗共同发生的假阳性预告事件，从而在低压缩区以参数化抑制提高条件资源态保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠少光子态（Bell态、GHZ态）是离散变量光子量子信息处理的核心资源，但通常由双光子源预告单光子后在线性光学电路中干涉而概率性产生，干涉损耗等不完美会直接降低输出资源态质量。作者希望利用双光子源本身的纠缠结构进行噪声工程，而非仅将其视为单光子来源，以在相同硬件条件下获得更高质量的资源态。

Method: 基于多模压缩真空态的转置恒等式，引入"幽灵幺正"实现：将目标资源生成干涉仪施加于预告模式而非交付的资源模式。针对干涉损耗这一主导不完美因素，分析该重定位如何把输出的直接振幅阻尼转变为假阳性预告事件（需同时发生高阶对发射与光子损耗），并在低压缩区给出参数化抑制。将该机制用于单量子比特态、双光子NOON态、Bell态、n量子比特GHZ态族，以及单量子比特隐形传态和基于type-I融合的资源生长，划分幽灵幺正实现优于直接实现的有限参数区间。同时证明n-GHZ族直接实现的输出为恒等阻尼：等价于理想态后接均匀振幅阻尼。

Result: 在若干具体任务中界定了幽灵幺正实现优于直接实现的有限参数区间；假阳性预告事件在低压缩区被参数化抑制，从而给出更高保真度的条件资源态。证明n-GHZ族直接实现的输出具有恒等阻尼结构（理想态叠加均匀振幅阻尼），将已知的n=2,3情形推广至一般n。

Conclusion: 纠缠双光子源的纠缠结构可被直接用于噪声工程，幽灵幺正实现通过把干涉损耗转化为受抑制的假阳性预告事件，在干涉损耗主导时提升资源态质量，尤其适用于主动线性光学变换损耗较大的频率梳（frequency-bin）光子学平台；n-GHZ族的恒等阻尼结果具有独立的结构性意义。

Abstract: Entangled few-photon states, such as Bell and Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) states, are central resources for discrete-variable photonic quantum information processing. In many architectures, these states are generated probabilistically by first heralding single photons from biphoton sources and then interfering them in linear-optical circuits. In this work, we show that the entanglement structure of said biphoton sources can be directly exploited for noise-engineering to deliver higher quality resource states. Using the transpose identity of multi-mode squeezed vacuum states, we introduce "ghost-unitary" implementations in which the target resource-generation interferometer is applied to the heralding modes rather than to the delivered resource modes. When the dominant imperfection is interferometric loss, this relocation converts direct amplitude damping of the output into false-positive heralding events that require both higher-pair emission and photon loss. In the low-squeezing regime, these events are parametrically suppressed, yielding higher-fidelity conditional resource states. We analyze this mechanism for single-qubit states, two-photon NOON states, Bell states, and a family of $n$-qubit GHZ states, as well as for single-qubit teleportation and type-I-fusion-based resource growth. Across these examples, we delineate finite parameter regimes in which ghost-unitary implementations outperform direct implementations, with particular relevance to frequency-bin photonics where active linear-optical transformations can be relatively lossy. We also prove that, in the setting considered here, the direct implementation of the $n$-GHZ family delivers identically damped outputs: equivalent to the ideal state followed by uniform amplitude damping. This extends the known $n=2,3$ cases and provides a structural result of independent interest for photonic quantum information processing.

</details>


### [138] [Quantum limit cycles from phase coupling: A framework for NISQ thermodynamic devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15905)
*Nana Siddhartha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将Kuramoto模型与Van der Pol振子量子化并与量子比特耦合，证明在典型量子热力学环境下仍存在稳健极限环，可实现量子同步，并观察到纠缠态复苏、Zeno锁定与热流干涉等新效应，提出基于量子比特与非线性相位振子同步的器件设想，理论框架为"Spectral Mechanics"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子退相干使得许多微型化器件与协议无法像经典情形那样无需用户显式干预即可运行；经典驱动信号不足以描述完全构建于量子域中的真正量子器件。作者希望寻找一种能在微型化过程中保持量子相干性的机制（同步），以支撑新型量子器件的实现。

Method: 对Kuramoto模型和Van der Pol振子进行量子化处理，并将其与一个或多个量子比特（qubit）耦合，置于典型量子热力学设定下进行分析；采用"Spectral Mechanics"作为理论基础框架。

Result: 即使在典型量子热力学环境下也表现出稳健的极限环；观察到新效应，包括纠缠态复苏（Entanglement State Revivals）、Zeno锁定（Zeno locking）以及热流干涉（thermal current interference）。

Conclusion: 同步（不同于热化）能够在微型化条件下维持而不牺牲量子相干性；提出可利用单个量子比特（或任意数量量子比特集合）与非线性相位振子的同步来实现的器件设想；所用Spectral Mechanics框架为传统量子力学基础提供了新视角，并可扩展至实验环境中全新形式的相互作用与模型。

Abstract: Quantum decoherence does not permit the operation of (new) miniaturised devices or protocols that can otherwise run unassisted in classical settings, without explicit user agent intervention in the quantum realm. Classical driving signals are inadequate to model a truly quantum device built entirely in the quantum realm. Synchronisation endures miniaturisation without sacrificing quantum coherence, unlike thermalisation. We quantise the Kuramoto model and the Van der Pol oscillator and couple it with qubit(s) to show a robust limit cycle even in typical quantum thermodynamical settings. Novel effects such as Entanglement State Revivals, Zeno locking and thermal current interference are observed. We propose toy model devices that can be implemented harnessing the Synchronisation of a qubit (or an arbitrary collection of qubits) with a nonlinear phase oscillator. The framework theory used here, Spectral Mechanics, is a fresh look at the foundations of conventional quantum mechanics and is extensible to completely new forms of interactions and models in experimental settings.

</details>


### [139] [Boson Sampling Using Fock States from On-Demand Single Photon Source Arrays: Quantum Complexity & Impact of Dephasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17773)
*Swarnabha Chattaraj,Anupam Madhukar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个将退相干物理来源与多光子关联测量结果直接联系的框架，用于评估基于按需单光子源的芯片级玻色采样电路中N光子纠缠态的生成概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以按需单光子（离散变量Fock态）为资源态的玻色采样在复杂度扩展上优于由非线性光子学产生的连续变量态模拟的单光子态。规则有序的mesa顶单量子点（MTSQD）阵列的进展使芯片级单光子玻色采样成为可能，但单光子退相位与衰减时间尺度如何影响N光子纠缠态最终概率分布尚不明确，这是实现规模扩展的关键问题。

Method: 提出一个将退相干物理来源与多光子关联测量结果直接关联的框架：显式引入N光子态的对称化，同时考虑单个波包的形状与退相位效应。基于该框架，用4个相同单光子波包（模拟文献[2]中MTSQD单光子源发射的光子）初始化的光学电路，对生成4光子NOON态的概率进行预测性估计。

Result: 定量给出了生成各种4光子纠缠态的概率如何依赖于双光子干涉可见度，而后者由纯退相位时间尺度相对于辐射衰减时间尺度的比值决定。

Conclusion: 研究结果为测量基于按需单光子源的光学电路中生成的4光子及更高数目纠缠光子态提供了定量指导，并建立了温度等物理过程与量子光学电路输出态之间的联系。

Abstract: Boson sampling with array of on-demand single photons (discreet variable Fock states) as resource states provides distinct advantages in complexity scaling compared to single photon resource states mimicked by the states (continuous variable) generated in nonlinear photonics. Recent progress in realization of regular ordered array of mesa top single quantum dots (MTSQDs) enabling on-demand generation of many photon resource states with high single photon purity and indistinguishability [1,2] has opened the possibility of implementing chip-scale Boson sampling with single photons. An important question towards scale up of such circuits is how the dephasing and decay timescale of individual on-demand single photons affect the final probability distribution of the N-photon entangled state. In this work we report a framework that provides direct link between the physical sources of decoherence to the outcome of multiphoton correlation measurements. We explicitly incorporate the symmetrization of the N-photon states while accounting for the shape of the individual wave packets and dephasing. Using this framework, we show predictive estimates of the probability of generation of a 4-photon NOON state using an optical circuit initialized with 4 identical single photon wave packets, mimicking the emitted photons from MTSQD single photon sources reported in [2]. Specifically, we show how the probability of generating various 4-photon entangled states depend on the two-photon interference visibility which is determined by the pure dephasing timescale in relation to the radiative decay timescale. The results provide quantitative guidelines to measure 4 photon and higher number of entangled photon states generated in an optical circuit with on demand single photon sources and provide a link between physical processes such as temperature and the output state of the quantum optical circuits.

</details>


### [140] [Quantum simultaneous decoding via pretty-good measurements of tilted states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15955)
*Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了量子多址信道中同时解码的自包含证明，结合Sen的倾斜子空间方法与Cheng对pretty-good测量(PGM)的分析，得到适用于有限状态系综（含不等先验）的确定性packing定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多址信道中，接收方需依据全部消息区分量子输出态，而不同解码错误的检验可能不对易，传统方法难以直接处理。作者希望建立统一框架，用一个联合测量在独立码本下达到可达速率，并避免对每个应用重复构造。

Method: 方法上结合Sen的tilted-subspace方法与Cheng对pretty-good measurement (PGM)的分析。引入靠近公共参考态的辅助标签，分离不同错误对应的几何方向；再用Gram-matrix界使分别选定的二元检验能够认证单一测量的性能；最后移除标签，得到确定性packing定理。先处理双发送方情形（含示例与图示），再推广到任意固定发送方数。另单独推导有限误差保证，给出两个特定分析步骤的紧界。

Result: 得到一个适用于有限状态系综（包括不等先验）的确定性packing定理，并给出同一解码器的更紧有限误差保证（针对两个特定步骤的紧界，而非全局最优解码声明）。应用方面恢复了无辅助与纠缠辅助经典通信、带量子边信息的分布式经典压缩、以及经分束器或无源多端口器件的相干态通信的已知可达速率。

Conclusion: 统一的证明揭示了同时解码的几何机制及其定量后果，使各应用无需重复构造即可获得相应可达速率，为量子多址信道的解码分析提供了通用工具。

Abstract: A multiple-access channel transmits messages from several senders to one receiver. For quantum outputs, the receiver must distinguish states depending on all messages, while tests for different decoding errors may not commute. In this paper, we give a self-contained account of simultaneous decoding, in which one joint measurement recovers the messages at the established achievable rates using independent codebooks. The proof combines Sen's tilted-subspace method with Cheng's analysis of the pretty-good measurement, a standard measurement for distinguishing quantum states. Auxiliary labels close to a common reference state separate the geometric directions associated with different errors. A Gram-matrix bound then allows separately chosen binary tests to certify the performance of one measurement. The labels are removed from the completed decoder, leading to a deterministic packing theorem that applies to finite state ensembles, including unequal priors. We develop the two-sender case first, with worked examples and figures, before treating any fixed number of senders. Sharper finite-error guarantees for the same decoder are developed separately, with sharp bounds for two specified steps of the analysis rather than a claim of globally optimal decoding. Applications recover known achievable rates for unassisted and entanglement-assisted classical communication, distributed classical compression with quantum side information, and coherent-state communication through a beamsplitter or passive multiport device. The common proof makes the geometric mechanism and its quantitative consequences available without repeating the construction for each application.

</details>


### [141] [Scalable Exact Non-Pauli Noise Simulation for Fault-Tolerant Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15975)
*Yuewen Hou,Gokul Subramanian Ravi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QuasiStim 是一个可扩展的精确 QEC 仿真框架，通过 QuasiFold 编译前端与 DeltaFrame 采样后端，在避免 Pauli Twirling 近似的前提下高效模拟相干控制误差与热弛豫等真实噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子设计需要仿真包含数百至数千物理比特、大电路深度的含噪 QEC 电路。Stim 等高吞吐稳定子模拟器是该场景的事实标准工具，但通常仅支持 Clifford 门与随机 Pauli 噪声，因此相干控制误差和热弛豫等更真实的器件噪声模型常被 Pauli Twirling 近似替代。该替换虽带来速度优势，却会引入精度损失。

Method: 提出 QuasiStim，包含编译器前端 QuasiFold 与采样后端 DeltaFrame。QuasiFold 通过合并量子电路中等价的非 Pauli 位点来降低采样开销；DeltaFrame 通过复用预计算的预测参考来采样所得的稳定子轨迹，而非对每个 shot 重新仿真完整 tableau。

Result: QuasiFold 可在双变量自行车（BB）码上将 shot 数减少 100 倍以上。在相干 R_Z 噪声下，随着 d 从 5 增至 35，DeltaFrame 在旋转表面码存储实验上比逐 shot tableau 模拟器快 166–1331 倍。QuasiStim 还使 PTA 偏差可测：该偏差不可忽略，并随噪声强度、解码器和电路调度而变化。

Conclusion: QuasiStim 实现了对真实噪声的可扩展精确 QEC 仿真，在不牺牲精度的情况下显著降低采样开销与仿真时间，并首次使 PTA 近似偏差可量化。论文发表后将在 https://github.com/isaachyw/QuasiStim 开源。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum design relies on simulating noisy quantum error correction (QEC) circuits with hundreds to thousands of physical qubits and large circuit depths. High-throughput stabilizer simulators, such as Stim, are the de facto tools for this regime. However, these simulators typically support only Clifford gates and stochastic Pauli noise. As a result, more realistic device noise models-such as coherent control errors and thermal relaxation-are often approximated using Pauli Twirling. This replacement offers speed but can introduce inaccuracies.
  To address this, we propose QuasiStim for scalable exact QEC simulation of realistic noise, with QuasiFold as a compiler frontend and DeltaFrame as a sampling backend. QuasiFold lowers sampling overhead by merging equivalent non-Pauli sites in the quantum circuit. DeltaFrame samples the resulting stabilizer trajectories by reusing precomputed predicted references instead of resimulating the full tableau for each shot.
  QuasiFold can reduce the number of shots by over $100\times$ on bivariate-bicycle (BB) codes. Compared to a per-shot tableau simulator, DeltaFrame is $166\times$--$1331\times$ faster on rotated surface-code memory under coherent $R_Z$ noise as $d$ grows from 5 to 35. QuasiStim also makes the PTA bias measurable: the gap is non-negligible and varies with noise strength, decoder, and circuit schedule.
  QuasiStim will be open-sourced upon publication at https://github.com/isaachyw/QuasiStim

</details>


### [142] [Adaptive Entanglement Witnessing for Two-Qubit Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16018)
*Lev Gruber,Ria Haapala,Elio Thadhani,Paco Navarro,Eritas Yang,Benjamin Hartley,Rebecca Verghese,Oscar Scholin,Isabel Godoy,Stuart Kerr,Brayden Mendoza,Alec Roberson,Theresa W. Lynn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于$\{W^3\}$的附加纠缠见证集合$\{W^5\}$，并设计自适应两步测量协议，用五个基的测量即可检测双量子比特纠缠，计算识别率达72%，并用偏振纠缠光子对实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知双量子比特系统中判定纠缠通常需要完整量子层析，代价高。Riccardi等(2020)提出的$\{W^3\}$见证仅需三个基的局域测量，但覆盖范围有限，因此需要扩展见证集并提升检测效率。

Method: 构建在$\{W^3\}$基础上增加两个测量基的见证集$\{W^5\}$；设计自适应两步协议，先利用$\{W^3\}$的测量结果选择需要评估的具体$W^5$见证；通过数值计算评估协议性能，并使用偏振纠缠光子对进行实验验证。

Result: 自适应两步协议能够识别按Roik等(2021)方法生成的72%纠缠态，且仅需九个全层析基中的五个基；两类见证集的行为在偏振纠缠光子对实验中得到验证。

Conclusion: 所提$\{W^5\}$见证集与自适应两步协议在降低测量开销的同时有效提升双量子比特纠缠检测能力，兼具计算与实验可行性。

Abstract: Entanglement witnesses are a key instrument in determining the entanglement of unknown bipartite qubit systems without requiring a full tomography. Riccardi et al. (2020) proposed a set of two-qubit witnesses, denoted $\{W^3\}$, which require a set of local measurements in three bases. In this work we propose an additional set of witnesses, denoted $\{W^5\}$, that build on $\{W^3\}$ and require measurements in two additional bases. We present an adaptive two-step method that uses the measurement results obtained for $\{W^3\}$ to select which $W^5$ to evaluate. We computationally demonstrate that our adaptive two-step protocol can identify 72% of entangled states generated according to the methods of Roik et al. (2021) while requiring measurements in only five of the nine bases needed for full quantum tomography. Additionally, we experimentally verify the behavior of both sets of witnesses using polarization-entangled photon pairs.

</details>


### [143] [Sequential optimization for orbital-optimized variational quantum eigensolvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16032)
*Hiromichi Hayashi,Takeshi Sato*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 SOO-UCCD，将无梯度的序贯最小优化扩展到轨道旋转参数，通过九点能量剖面确定轨道旋转角，实现无梯度、无超参数的轨道优化，在 LiH 上达到 UCCSD 级别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQE 框架下的轨道优化可提升活性空间质量并准确捕捉电子关联，且适配浅层量子线路；但原始 OO-UCC 方法依赖轨道梯度与 Hessian 的 Newton–Laphson 过程，需要梯度/Hessian 计算，代价高且实现复杂。

Method: 提出序贯轨道优化酉耦合簇双激发（SOO-UCCD），把无梯度的序贯最小优化（SMO）推广到轨道旋转参数。依据轨道旋转生成元代数性质所隐含的频率结构，对每个轨道旋转角由九点能量剖面确定；能量景观通过经典变换哈密顿量积分并与 VQE 能量评估中已测量的约化密度矩阵收缩得到，无需计算轨道梯度或 Hessian。

Result: 对 STO-3G 基组下的 LiH 进行态矢量模拟：SOO-UCCD 在键拉伸曲线上重现 UCCSD 级别精度，宏观迭代收敛性与基于 L-BFGS 的轨道优化相当。

Conclusion: SOO-UCCD 继承了 OO-UCC 框架的核心优势，提供无梯度且无超参数的轨道优化流程，可在不评估梯度或 Hessian 的前提下达到与常规优化相当的精度与收敛表现。

Abstract: Orbital optimization within the variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) framework is useful for improving active-space quality and accurately capturing electron correlation with shallow quantum circuits. The orbital-optimized unitary coupled cluster (OO-UCC) method [W. Mizukami et al., Phys. Rev. Res. 2, 033421 (2020)], in its original formulation, employs the orbital gradient and Hessian in a Newton--Laphson procedure. In this study, we propose sequential orbital-optimized unitary coupled cluster doubles (SOO-UCCD), extending the gradient-free sequential minimal optimization approach [K. M. Nakanishi et al., Phys. Rev. Res. 2, 043158 (2020)] to orbital rotation parameters. Based on the frequency structure implied by the algebraic properties of the orbital rotation generators, our method determines each orbital rotation angle from a nine-point energy profile. The energy landscape is evaluated by classically transforming the Hamiltonian integrals and contracting them with the reduced density matrices already measured for the VQE energy evaluation. This allows for orbital optimization without evaluating the orbital gradient or Hessian. By building on the core benefits of the OO-UCC framework, our method provides a gradient-free and hyperparameter-free orbital-optimization procedure. State-vector simulations for LiH in the STO-3G basis show that SOO-UCCD reproduces UCCSD-level accuracy along the bond-stretching curve and exhibits macro-iteration convergence comparable to that of L-BFGS-based orbital optimization.

</details>


### [144] [Permanent Thermodynamic Mpemba Inversion from Quantum-Jump Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16064)
*Jincheng Lu,Chen Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子跳跃反馈协议可永久逆转两个指定初态的非平衡自由能排序，同时保持各自的吉布斯平衡，实现永久热力学量子姆潘巴效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究中普遍关注量子姆潘巴效应（初态在弛豫中排序反转）是否可被主动控制且持续存在；本文旨在构造一种反馈协议，使自由能排序反转成为永久性、而非瞬态的现象。

Method: 利用受监测通道之间的集体补偿：反馈修正项在平衡时相互抵消，但在弛豫过程中保持活跃，从而在保持自由能单调性的同时重塑弛豫模式。通过消除初始较高自由能态的完整慢模扇区贡献、保留比较态的该扇区，使两者获得不同的渐近衰减指数，进而令自由能比在长时极限下趋于零。以热三能级系统（qutrit）通过相干干涉实现该机制。

Result: 证明了两态的渐近衰减指数不同，自由能比在长时下消失，从而保证受控动力学下永久的热力学量子姆潘巴反转；热qutrit实验方案展示了相干干涉机制，并给出谱选择与反馈重调下精确慢扇区消除的不同探测阈值。

Conclusion: 该工作表明量子跳跃反馈可在保持平衡性质不变的前提下永久重构弛豫动力学，为主动调控量子姆潘巴效应提供了可行方案，并可在三能级系统中实现与探测。

Abstract: We show that a common quantum-jump feedback protocol can permanently reverse the nonequilibrium free-energy ordering of two prescribed initial states while preserving their Gibbs equilibrium. The construction exploits collective compensation between monitored channels: their feedback corrections cancel at equilibrium but remain active during relaxation. This balance preserves free-energy monotonicity while allowing the relaxation modes to be reshaped. We prove that eliminating the complete slow-sector contribution of the initially higher-free-energy state, while retaining it for the comparison state, produces distinct asymptotic decay exponents. Their free-energy ratio then vanishes at long times, guaranteeing a permanent thermodynamic quantum Mpemba inversion under the same controlled dynamics. A thermal qutrit realizes the mechanism through coherence interference and exhibits distinct detection thresholds for spectral selection and exact slow-sector cancellation under feedback retuning.

</details>


### [145] [Noise-resistant off-resonance π-rotation realized in an NMR system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16098)
*Xilong Wang,Peng Du,Tianzi Wang,Haoxue Qiao,Wenxian Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出双脉冲与十八脉冲序列，在失谐条件下实现单自旋π旋转，可同时抑制退相干与驱动场噪声，并在NMR体系中验证了高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子比特（自旋）的精确操控需要抑制自旋-环境耦合引起的噪声以及驱动场涨落。传统π旋转多依赖共振或近共振条件，而失谐条件下的鲁棒控制方法仍待发展。

Method: 提出双脉冲序列和十八脉冲序列，在失谐（而非共振或近共振）条件下实现单自旋π旋转。以极化初始自旋态的NMR自旋体系为实验平台，对两种序列进行原理性验证。

Result: 失谐π旋转保真度：双脉冲序列达0.9992±0.0008，十八脉冲序列达0.9997±0.0006。十八脉冲序列的噪声容忍范围约为驱动场强度的20%，比双脉冲序列宽约三倍。

Conclusion: 十八脉冲序列在同时抑制退相干与驱动场噪声方面显著优于双脉冲序列。该方案除适用于NMR领域外，还为通过抑制共存的横向与纵向噪声实现量子比特精确操控提供了实用替代途径。

Abstract: Precise manipulation of qubits (spins) requires strong suppression of noise arising from couplings between qubits and their environment, as well as from driving field fluctuations. We propose a double-pulse sequence and an eighteen-pulse sequence to realize a single spin pi-rotation under off-resonance conditions (instead of conventional on-resonance or near-resonance conditions). It is proved that the latter sequence performs much better than the former in simultaneously suppressing both dephasing and driving field noise. As a proof of principle, we experimentally demonstrate, by implementing both sequences in an NMR spin system with a polarized initial spin state, that the off-resonance pi-rotation fidelity reaches as high as 0.9992 +/- 0.0008 for the double-pulse sequence and 0.9997 +/- 0.0006 for the eighteen-pulse sequence. In addition, the noise-tolerance range of the eighteen-pulse sequence is about 20 percent of the driving field strength, approximately three times wider than that of the double-pulse sequence. Besides its potential application in the NMR community, our proposal provides an alternative practical way to accurately control qubits by suppressing the coexisting transversal and longitudinal noises.

</details>


### [146] [QIAP: A Quantum-Inspired Authentication Protocol with BB84-Based Channel Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16124)
*Owais Ishtiaq Siddiqui,Nouhaila Innan,Hira Zaheer,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出QIAP：一种结合经典承诺-挑战响应认证与BB84信道监测的有状态混合框架，实现身份验证与信道扰动评估的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子感知通信场景要求认证机制既能验证用户合法性，又能判断信道是否受到过度扰动；现有方案通常仅侧重单一目标，缺乏轻量化的混合设计。

Method: 采用双分支验证结构（受测量基选择启发）：直线基分支中证明方开启基于哈希的承诺，验证方校验其与注册轮令牌的一致性；对角基分支中证明方返回绑定验证方nonce、轮标识与提交承诺的HMAC标签，且不泄露轮专属子密钥。认证成功后由BB84模块估计QBER，达到或超过阈值即中止会话。

Result: 在设定的哈希、HMAC、新鲜性、安全注册、受保护存储与高熵密钥假设下，QIAP对错误密钥与重放攻击具备计算抗性，并保护长期主密钥不被直接恢复；仿真中每分支1,000次错误密钥尝试均无成功认证；基线与完整截获-重发条件下平均QBER分别约为0.02与0.25；经典认证阶段约耗时0.6 ms，单轮认证消息约113字节。

Conclusion: QIAP在既定威胁模型下可作为轻量级混合框架，将基于密钥的身份验证与基于QBER的信道扰动评估相结合。

Abstract: Authentication in quantum-aware communication settings requires mechanisms that verify user legitimacy while also assessing whether the communication channel exhibits excessive disturbance. This paper presents QIAP, a stateful hybrid framework that combines classical commitment-based challenge-response authentication with BB84-based quantum-channel monitoring. QIAP uses a dual-branch verification structure inspired by rectilinear and diagonal basis selection. In the rectilinear branch, the prover opens a hash-based commitment and the verifier checks consistency with the registered round token. In the diagonal branch, the prover returns an HMAC tag bound to the verifier nonce, round identifier, and submitted commitment without disclosing the round-specific sub-secret. Under the stated hash, HMAC, freshness, secure-registration, protected-storage, and high-entropy-secret assumptions, QIAP provides computational resistance to the modeled wrong-secret and replay attempts and protects the long-term master secret from direct recovery. After successful authentication, the BB84 module estimates the quantum bit error rate (QBER) and aborts the session when the measured value reaches or exceeds the configured threshold. In the reported simulation, no successful authentication was observed among 1,000 tested wrong-secret attempts per branch. The baseline and full intercept-resend conditions produced mean QBER values of approximately 0.02 and 0.25, respectively. The classical authentication stage required approximately 0.6 ms, and the implemented authentication messages required approximately 113 bytes per round. These results indicate that QIAP can provide a lightweight hybrid framework for combining secret-based identity verification with QBER-based channel-disturbance assessment under the stated threat model.

</details>


### [147] [Perfect discrimination of an orthogonal $2\otimes2$ basis may require collective separable measurements on two copies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16138)
*Somshubhro Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究两量子比特空间中三纠缠态加一积态的完备正交基，证明单拷贝下LOCC与可分离测量均无法完美区分，双拷贝下自适应可分离协议仍失败，但集体可分离测量成功；双拷贝LOCC情形仍开放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察一种由三个纠缠态和一个积态构成的正交基，该基在单拷贝条件下即使允许可分离测量也无法完美区分，因此需要研究增加拷贝数能否提升区分能力，并厘清LOCC与可分离测量在多方/多拷贝场景下的区分能力差异。

Method: 以C^2⊗C^2中的三纠缠态加一积态的完备正交基为研究对象，分别在单拷贝与双拷贝条件下进行分析：证明任意双拷贝自适应可分离协议（逐次测量拷贝）无法完美区分该基；进而构造并证明双拷贝集体可分离测量可完美区分该基。

Result: 单拷贝下该正交基无法被LOCC甚至可分离测量完美区分；双拷贝下不存在自适应可分离协议能完美区分；但双拷贝集体可分离测量可以完美区分该基。

Conclusion: 增加拷贝数并结合集体测量可超越单拷贝及自适应可分离协议的限制，实现该正交基的完美区分；而双拷贝条件下LOCC能否实现完美区分仍为未决问题。

Abstract: We consider an orthogonal basis of $\mathbb C^2\otimes\mathbb C^2$ with three entangled states and one product state. This basis is not perfectly distinguishable by LOCC or even by separable measurements when a single copy of the unknown state is available. We show that with two copies no adaptive separable protocol, which measures the copies one at a time, distinguishes the basis perfectly. We then show that a collective separable measurement on the two copies does. Whether two copies suffice for LOCC remains open.

</details>


### [148] [Device-to-protocol simulation of spectrally-resolved quantum photonic circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16150)
*Seongjin Jeon,Youngbin Kim,Joaquin Matres,Dong-Yeop Na,Young-Ik Sohn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: PhoQuS：统一量子光子电路仿真框架，融合频域源模型、电磁紧凑模型与空间相关工艺变异，在保留光谱细节的同时将读出成本降低，实现60.4倍光子计数加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子光子电路性能预测需兼顾源与器件的物理保真度和光子计数计算的可负担性，二者往往难以同时满足，因此需要一套既保留频率分辨细节又能降低读出复杂度的仿真流程。

Method: 提出PhoQuS框架，集成频率分辨源模型、电磁紧凑模型与空间相关工艺变异。在全分辨率高斯传播后采用协方差-Gram约简降低读出成本并保留主导光谱关联。用于连续变量态制备（GKP电路）加速光子计数，并以频率分辨建模指导级联压缩与时间走离补偿。

Result: GKP电路光子计数加速60.4倍；级联压缩结合时间走离补偿在无损仿真中对单模电路参考的保真度达0.99、条件纯度0.98；三光子GHZ电路在虚拟晶圆上的仿真表明元件鲁棒性本身不能预测电路保真度。

Conclusion: 电路保真度取决于源光谱、电路架构与控制的联合作用，PhoQuS通过统一工作流将器件物理与协议级性能相连接，兼顾光谱细节与低读出成本。

Abstract: Predicting quantum-photonic circuit performance requires retaining source and device physics without making photon-counting calculations prohibitively expensive. Here we present PhoQuS, a framework that combines frequency-resolved source models, electromagnetic compact models and spatially correlated fabrication variation to simulate quantum photonic circuits. Covariance-Gram reduction after full-resolution Gaussian propagation lowers readout cost while retaining dominant spectral correlations. For continuous-variable state preparation, spectral reduction accelerates photon counting by 60.4-fold in an approximate Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill circuit. Frequency-resolved modeling guides the use of cascaded squeezing with temporal walk-off compensation, yielding 0.99 fidelity to the single-mode circuit reference and 0.98 conditional purity in lossless simulations. For discrete-variable state preparation, simulations of three-photon Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger circuits across a virtual wafer show that component robustness alone does not predict circuit fidelity, which depends jointly on source spectra, circuit architecture and control. PhoQuS connects device physics to protocol-level performance through a unified workflow that retains spectral detail while reducing readout cost.

</details>


### [149] [Entanglement and parametric resonance in coupled oscillator systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16389)
*Roxane Thériault,George Zahariade*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究两个二次耦合谐振子系统（分别处于基态与相干态），用Feynman-Vernon影响泛函微扰计算约化纯度，揭示参数共振区中量子纠缠的失控增长与微扰论失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该玩具模型可描述量子场单模在谐振经典背景中的演化，此类系统在特定背景振幅与耦合强度下会出现参数共振；作者希望定量澄清参数共振与量子纠缠之间的相互作用，并检验文献中关于半经典近似有效性的论断。

Method: 构建两二次耦合谐振子模型，一个初态为基态、另一个为相干态；采用Feynman-Vernon影响泛函方法，对其中一个振子求迹得到混合态，并微扰计算其纯度以刻画纠缠。

Result: 在参数不稳定区域，两振子间量子纠缠呈现失控（runaway）行为，同时微扰论有效性迅速崩溃；这为文献中的相关说法提供了定量确认。

Conclusion: 结果与耦合量子-经典系统中半经典近似有效性问题密切相关，表明在参数共振条件下纠缠增长与微扰失效相互交织。

Abstract: We study a system of two quadratically-coupled harmonic oscillators, prepared respectively in the ground state and in a coherent state. This toy model can describe for instance the dynamics of one mode of a quantum field evolving in a harmonically-oscillating classical background. In such situations, it is well-known that, depending on the background oscillation amplitude and coupling strength, the system may exhibit parametric resonance. Using the Feynman-Vernon influence functional, we analyze the interplay between parametric resonance and quantum entanglement by perturbatively computing the purity of the mixed state resulting from tracing over one of the oscillators. We find a runaway behavior of the quantum entanglement between the two oscillators, as well as a rapid breakdown of the validity of perturbation theory, in regions of parametric instability. This provides a quantitative confirmation of various statements in the literature and is highly relevant to questions pertaining to the validity of the semiclassical approximation in coupled quantum-classical systems.

</details>


### [150] [QBFF: Deep Quantum Bottleneck for Automatic Speaker Verification Anti-Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16390)
*Anton Firc,Nouhaila Innan,Vojtěch Staněk,Kamil Malinka,Martin Perešini,Zbyněk Lička,Walid El Maouaki,Alberto Marchisio,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出QBFF，将压缩语音表示通过8量子比特变分量子电路(VQC)作为瓶颈层，用于深度伪造语音检测；实验表明量子瓶颈在多数匹配对比中提供更优的置信度校准，并首次在真实IBM量子处理器上运行反欺骗流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习旨在找到小量子电路能提升任务表现且可在真实硬件上执行的应用。深度伪造语音检测在此领域尚少被探索，且该任务中可靠的置信度分数与判别性能同等重要，因为部署决策基于固定阈值，过度自信的错误代价高昂。

Method: 提出QBFF，将压缩的语音表示送入一个小型8量子比特变分量子电路(VQC)，作为即插即用的瓶颈层，并以匹配的经典瓶颈作为对照。随后在真实IBM量子处理器上运行同一电路。

Result: 在多数匹配对比中，包括分布外评估，量子电路给出校准更好的置信度，但下游部署成本收益依模型而定。硬件执行验证了可行性，子集EER与C_llr更低，但平均actDCF高于匹配的经典参照。

Conclusion: 硬件可执行的量子瓶颈能提升分数可靠性，其增益随评估设置而变化；这是首个在QPU上运行的反欺骗流水线，证明了可行性。

Abstract: A central goal of quantum machine learning is to identify applications in which small quantum circuits can improve task performance while remaining executable on real hardware. We investigate deepfake (synthetic) speech detection, a largely unexplored application in this area, where reliable confidence scores are as important as discrimination performance because deployment decisions are made at fixed thresholds and overconfident errors can be costly. We introduce QBFF, which routes a compressed speech representation through a small 8-qubit variational circuit (VQC) used as a drop-in bottleneck, with a matched classical bottleneck as the control. The quantum circuit yields better-calibrated confidence in most matched comparisons, including out-of-distribution evaluation, though the downstream deployment-cost benefit is model-dependent. We then run the same circuit on a real IBM quantum processor. To our knowledge, this is the first anti-spoofing pipeline to run on a QPU; hardware execution demonstrates feasibility, with lower subset EER and $C_{llr}$ but higher mean actDCF than the matched classical reference. Together, these results show that hardware-executable quantum bottlenecks can improve score reliability, with gains that vary by evaluation setting.

</details>


### [151] [Magic-Free Certification of Logical Error Rates in Universal Fault-Tolerant Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16401)
*Gebrat Porta-San Miguel,Shahram Panahiyan,Dieter Jaksch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"无魔法认证"(magic-free certification)协议，可在不消耗魔法态的情况下，对通用容错量子电路的逻辑错误率是否低于预设阈值进行可扩展的认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件进入容错阶段后，算法开始在编码量子比特上执行，但如何可扩展地认证其运行质量仍是未解问题。魔法态是使容错计算超出高效经典模拟能力、且成本最高的资源，因此直接认证代价高昂。

Method: 协议与纠错码及解码器无关，与目标电路在同一设备上交错运行；通过陷阱电路(trap circuits)实现——将目标电路中每个魔法态替换为稳定子态，其输出具有确定性，且对随机泡利噪声而言与目标电路不可区分、失败率相同。在给定误差容限p_L*下，以选定置信水平判定设备执行目标电路时的逻辑错误率是否低于p_L*。

Result: 样本复杂度随逻辑量子比特数与非Clifford门数线性增长，且与逻辑错误率本身解耦；在Steane码模拟中对魔法态注入线路和量子傅里叶变换进行了示例验证。

Conclusion: 该协议实现了不消耗魔法态、码与解码器无关、可随电路规模扩展的容错电路认证，为容错量子计算的性能验证提供了可行途径。

Abstract: As quantum hardware enters the fault-tolerant regime, algorithms are beginning to be executed on encoded qubits, yet scalable certification of how well they run remains an open problem. Here we introduce magic-free certification, a certification protocol for universal fault-tolerant circuits that consumes no magic, the resource that generally places them beyond efficient classical simulation and is by far the most expensive element of a fault-tolerant computation. The protocol is code- and decoder-agnostic, runs interleaved with the target circuit on the same device, and has a sample complexity that grows linearly with the number of logical qubits and non-Clifford gates while remaining decoupled from the logical error rate itself. Given a predefined error tolerance $p_L^*$, the protocol determines at a chosen confidence level whether the device executes the target circuit at a logical error rate below $p_L^*$. The method relies on trap circuits, copies of the target with every magic state replaced by a stabilizer state, whose outcomes are deterministic and which, being indistinguishable from the target to stochastic Pauli noise, fail at the same rate. We illustrate the protocol in Steane-code simulations of a magic state injection gadget and a quantum Fourier transform.

</details>


### [152] [Quantum state texture of maximal-resource Fourier states under Hawking radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16481)
*Shengyan Ma,Yiding Wang,Xiaofen Huang,Tinggui Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究双模狄拉克场中三个最大资源态在霍金辐射下的量子态纹理（QST）行为，揭示“正交保护”现象及QST在模式间的再分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QST是近期提出的刻画密度矩阵在给定计算基下非均匀性的量子资源，其傅里叶态为最大资源态。作者希望探究这些最大资源态在黑洞霍金辐射背景下的退化规律及资源再分配机制。

Method: 在双模狄拉克场中，采用基于保真度的QST度量，分别考察三种情形：仅模B辐射、仅模A辐射、双模同时辐射；并从资源理论角度给出正交保护的一般性解释。

Result: 单模辐射下，若未受辐射影响的模与单模无纹理态正交，则整体输出态的QST保持最大且与霍金温度无关（正交保护）；双模同时辐射时正交保护消失，三个傅里叶态的QST均随霍金温度升高而下降；且双模辐射下f_3的QST高于仅模B辐射情形。

Conclusion: 霍金辐射会导致QST退化，但QST可在模式间再分配，一个模式在辐射中获得的部分QST可补偿另一模式的损失；正交保护现象不依赖于QST度量的具体形式，为黑洞背景下量子资源再分配提供了新视角。

Abstract: Quantum state texture (QST) is a recently proposed quantum resource that characterizes the nonuniformity of a density matrix in a given computational basis. In the resource theory of QST, the Fourier states f_k are maximally resourceful states, attaining the maximum QST measure. We study the QST behavior of three maximally resourceful states in a two-mode Dirac field under Hawking radiation from a Schwarzschild black hole, using a fidelity-based measure. We consider three cases: only mode B undergoes radiation, only mode A undergoes radiation, and both modes undergo radiation simultaneously. Our results show that for single-mode radiation, if the mode unaffected by radiation is orthogonal to the single-mode textureless state, the QST of the overall output state remains maximal and is independent of the Hawking temperature; we call this phenomenon orthogonality protection. When both modes undergo radiation simultaneously, orthogonality protection vanishes, and the QST of all three Fourier states decreases with increasing Hawking temperature. Notably, the QST measure of f_3 under two-mode radiation is higher than that under only mode B radiation, indicating that QST can be redistributed among modes: QST gained by one mode under radiation can partially compensate for QST lost by the other. We further provide a general resource-theoretic explanation of orthogonality protection, showing that this phenomenon is independent of the specific form of the QST measure. These results reveal how QST degrades under Hawking radiation and provide a new perspective on the redistribution of quantum resources in black hole backgrounds.

</details>


### [153] [Bell's go theorem: regaining local causality with a new notion of realism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16493)
*Lorenzo Catani,Nesta van der Schaaf,Rui Soares Barbosa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过将本体论模型中的"实在元素"由点推广为无点拓扑空间（locale）的子locale——即"记录"（records），并以超出经典Kolmogorov概率的、在不相容语境间失效的模性定律重构局部因果性，使Bell定理由no-go转为go定理，并显式构造出再现最优CHSH违背等量子策略的局部因果模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Bell定理在本体论模型框架下将实在形式化为可测空间的点，局部因果模型预设一个支撑全局赋值的共同原因。在此实在观下，任何对量子预言的实在论解释都必须是"阴谋式"的：超光速、超决定论或逆因果等机制都需精细调节以永不显现于操作层面。作者试图重新定义实在，以摆脱这种阴谋性要求。

Method: 提出新的本体论框架：实在元素不再是点，而是无点拓扑空间（locale）的广义化对象——子locale中的"记录"，作为关系性部分。两个不相容语境共享的记录确立测量在其间的同一性，但这类成对同一性不构成全局赋值；共同原因成为记录之网。据此重述局部因果性，并允许经典概率的容斥律（模性）在不相容语境间失效，从而推广Kolmogorov概率论。

Result: 在记录框架下，Bell no-go定理变为go定理：重述后的局部因果性与量子统计不再冲突。给出再现若干量子策略的显式模型，包括最优CHSH违背；更一般地，此类局部因果模型可达到直至代数上界的任意Bell-CHSH值。

Conclusion: 对量子预言的实在论解释不必是阴谋式的：这些模型所设定的每一种因果影响都反映在操作预言中，从而消解了Bell定理对实在论与局部因果性的强制性张力。

Abstract: Bell's theorem is popularly stated as the inconsistency of quantum theory with local realism. Realism is here formalized by the ontological models framework, where elements of reality are points of a measurable space and probabilities obey the Kolmogorov axioms. In the Bell scenario, locally causal ontological models posit a common cause supporting a global valuation, a joint assignment of outcomes to all measurements at once, across incompatible contexts. Within this notion of realism, Bell's theorem makes any realist explanation of the quantum predictions conspiratorial: any mechanism, whether superluminal, superdeterministic, or retrocausal, must be finely tuned so as never to manifest operationally. We redefine realism through a new ontological framework that generalizes the standard one. The elements of reality, which we call records, are no longer points but relational parts of a point-free generalization of a topological space (sublocales of a locale). A record shared by two incompatible contexts establishes the sameness of a measurement across them, without such pairwise identities composing into a global valuation, and the common cause is then a web of such records. Within this framework, Bell's no-go theorem becomes a go theorem: local causality, restated for records, is no longer at odds with the quantum statistics. This is possible because modularity, the inclusion-exclusion law of classical probability, fails across incompatible contexts, so that the probability theory generalizes Kolmogorov's. We exhibit explicit models reproducing several quantum strategies, including the optimal CHSH violation. More broadly, locally causal models of this kind attain every Bell-CHSH value up to the algebraic maximum. A realist explanation of the quantum predictions need not be conspiratorial; every causal influence posited by these models is reflected in the operational predictions.

</details>


### [154] [Towards the Geometry of Quantum Optical Measurements: Kraus Trajectories in the Instrumental Weyl-Heisenberg Group Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16512)
*Tzula B. Propp,Chris S. Jackson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子单模测量中Kraus算子所属的十二维李群流形 IWH¹² 应用于广义'dyne测量，借助 Instrument Manifold Program 与类 Harish-Chandra 分解，构造了 slomodyne（介于扩散零差与外差之间）及带自适应压缩与位移的'dyne测量两类新测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自古希腊以来，相似性（similar triangles）使人类能推断远超直接感官的世界；在量子测量中，相似性同样重要，但体现为旋转、正则变换等虚拟对称的无穷小生成元之间的相似。单模量子光学测量的Kraus算子多落在 IWH¹² 内，且该群足以包含所有扩散型'dyne测量，因此需要借此发展更一般的'dyne测量类别。

Method: 采用 Instrument Manifold Program (IMP) 方法，对 IWH¹² 作类 Harish-Chandra 分解，从右不变基到相应坐标基计算 vielbein 变换，从而可普适、自主地在 IWH¹² 上导航，并随时间按测量记录定位任意选择性过程的 Kraus 算子。

Result: 发展了更一般的两类'dyne测量：一类在扩散零差与扩散外差之间插值（“slomodyne”）；另一类在'dyne测量上加入自适应压缩与位移。同时给出了计算 vielbein 变换、在 IWH¹² 上导航并定位时变 Kraus 算子的通用框架。

Conclusion: IWH¹² 结合 IMP 与类 Harish-Chandra 分解，为单模量子测量提供了统一的几何化处理框架，可统一描述并推广各类扩散型'dyne测量及自适应测量方案。

Abstract: Since ancient Greek times, geometry has been a method by which humans can make inferences, by the use of similar triangles, about the world far beyond their immediate senses. Similarity plays an equally important role in quantum measurement, except that instead of triangles, the similarity is between the infinitesimal generators of rotation, canonical transformation, or some other virtual symmetry. For many quantum optical measurements of a single mode, these generators produce Kraus operators that are contained within a twelve-dimensional Lie group: the Instrumental Weyl-Heisenberg group manifold ($\mathrm{IWH}^{\textbf{12}}$). In particular, $\mathrm{IWH}^{\textbf{12}}$ is general enough to include all diffusively generated 'dyne measurements. In this paper, $\mathrm{IWH}^{\textbf{12}}$ will be used to develop two more general classes of 'dyne measurement: one that interpolates between diffusive homodyne and diffusive heterodyne (``slomodyne'') and one that adds to 'dyne measurements adaptive squeezing and displacement. Our analysis uses an approach known as the Instrument Manifold Program (IMP). In particular, a Harish-Chandra-like decomposition of $\mathrm{IWH}^{\textbf{12}}$ is used to calculate the vielbein transformation from a right-invariant basis to the corresponding coordinate basis, allowing us to universally and autonomously navigate $\mathrm{IWH}^{\textbf{12}}$ and locate the Kraus-operator of whatever selective process is being considered as it evolves in time according to the measurement record.

</details>


### [155] [Emergent quantum algebras in dissipative time crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16609)
*Joseph Tindall*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为集体自旋开放系统建立了强动力学对称性代数描述的渐近版本，在单自旋于磁场中经线性跳跃算符耗散的情形下，给出S→∞时不衰减振荡模式出现的精确条件及其闭式对称算符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强动力学对称性已在有限维、满秩稳态的Lindblad系统中被证明是非衰减振荡模式存在的充要条件，但该代数框架是否以及如何推广到集体自旋这类大自旋渐近极限尚不清楚，需要建立其渐近对应物。

Method: 考虑磁场中的单个自旋S，受线性跳跃算符耗散作用；通过分析Liouvillian谱在S→∞极限下的行为，推导非衰减振荡模式涌现时对磁场与耗散耦合的精确条件；构造作用在稳态上的闭式"对称"算符以生成这些模式；并引入一个涌现守恒量作为第二算符来补全代数。

Result: 在S→∞时识别出非衰减振荡模式出现的精确参数条件；给出可作用于稳态以构造这些模式的简单闭式对称算符，所得模式为Liouvillian的本征矢，具有虚本征值，误差为O(1/S)；第二算符（涌现守恒量）补全代数，其慢弛豫决定量子噪声如何选择稳态；该构造可直接推广到共享同一库的相互作用自旋对。

Conclusion: 该工作将强动力学对称性代数推广到集体自旋的渐近极限，为一大类耗散自旋系统中的持续振荡提供解析描述，并可用于分析共享库下自旋对淬火过程中的纠缠动力学。

Abstract: Strong dynamical symmetries provide an algebraic description of persistent oscillations in open quantum systems governed by the Lindblad equation. For finite-dimensional systems with a full-rank stationary state, such symmetries are necessary and sufficient for non-decaying oscillatory modes to appear in the Liouvillian's spectrum.
