These modes can be directly constructed by acting on stationary states with the identified symmetry operators. Here we establish an asymptotic counterpart of this algebra for collective spins. Taking a single spin $S$ in a magnetic field, subject to dissipation through linear jump operators, we identify the exact conditions on the field and dissipative couplings under which non-decaying oscillatory modes emerge as $S \rightarrow \infty$. We then write down a simple, closed form for the corresponding `symmetry' operator which can be applied to the steady state to construct these modes, which are eigenvectors of the Liouvillian, with imaginary eigenvalues, up to $O(1/S)$ corrections. A second operator, an emergent conserved quantity, completes the algebra, and its slow relaxation determines how quantum noise selects the stationary state. The construction can be directly extended to a pair of interacting spins sharing a common reservoir, with the resulting quantum algebra making the entanglement during a quench analytically accessible.

</details>


### [156] [Closed-form construction of exceptional points in a two-parametric PT-symmetric large-matrix model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16613)
*Miloslav Znojil*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在离散一维N点晶格上，由双参数PT对称势V(A,B)生成的稳定束缚态，其Kato例外点坐标A^(EP)、B^(EP)可由一个初等四次多项式的根精确给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究PT对称离散晶格模型中的稳定束缚态，定位其稳定性丧失（相变）发生的临界参数点，即Kato例外点。

Method: 构造一个双参数PT对称势V(A,B)作用于离散一维N点晶格，将Kato例外点坐标A^(EP)与B^(EP)定义为某个初等四次多项式方程的根，从而将例外点求解转化为多项式求根问题。

Result: 对任意N，稳定束缚态的Kato例外点坐标A=A^(EP)、B=B^(EP)均由同一类初等四次多项式的根精确确定。

Conclusion: PT对称离散晶格体系的稳定性相变点可由初等四次多项式精确刻画，为非厄米晶格模型中例外点的解析求解提供了简洁途径。

Abstract: Stable bound states living on a discrete one-dimensional N-point lattice are assumed generated by a certain two-parametric PT symmetric potential $V=V(A,B)$. At any $N$, the coordinates $A=A^{(EP)}$ and $B=B^{(EP)}$ of the Kato's exceptional point (marking the phase-transition instant of the loss of stability) are defined, exactly, via roots of a certain elementary quartic polynomial.

</details>


### [157] [Bosonic Quantum Conditional Mutual Information and Bose-Einstein Condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16626)
*Anupam Ghosh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文定义连续变量（玻色）系统的玻色非对称量子条件互信息 $I_B(A;B|C)$，证明BEC导致对数发散，并将其确立为BEC的量子信息序参量，同时给出临界点附近的幂律标度及若干应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为连续变量玻色系统提供严格定义的信息论量，并建立量子信息量与BEC相变之间的定量联系，从而用信息论刻画凝聚与临界行为。

Method: 在无穷维Fock空间上通过量子仪器（quantum instruments）定义 $I_B(A;B|C)$；给出五步完整证明，结合测量反作用（measurement back-action）、非对角长程序（ODLRO）与Jensen不等式推导不等式 $I_B(A;B|C) \geq \alpha\log_2(N_0^C+1)$；在凝聚相中由长波极限下的Bogoliubov声速 $c_s$ 给出有效因果传播速度。

Result: 证明BEC诱导 $I_B(A;B|C)$ 的对数发散，且临界点附近呈幂律标度；在凝聚相中有效因果传播速度由 $c_s$ 设定，并在超流–Mott绝缘体转变处连续消失；约化玻色QCMI可作为类似Binder累积量的有限尺度标度探针。

Conclusion: 玻色QCMI是BEC的量子信息序参量，兼具相变诊断与有限尺度标度功能，并可用于复合玻色子、BEC–BCS交叉及量子气体显微镜测量方案等场景。

Abstract: This article aims to introduce the bosonic asymmetric quantum conditional mutual information (QCMI), $I_{B}(A; B|C)$, as a rigorously defined information-theoretic quantity for continuous-variable (bosonic) systems. After defining $I_{B}(A; B|C)$ through quantum instruments on infinite-dimensional Fock spaces, we prove that Bose-Einstein condensation (BEC) induces a logarithmic divergence $ I_{B}(A; B|C) \geq α\log_2 (N_{0}^C+1)$, which further establishes $I_{B}(A; B|C)$ as a quantum-information order parameter for BEC with power-law scaling near the critical point. We provide the complete five-step proof of this result, connecting measurement back-action, off-diagonal long-range order, and Jensen's inequality. In the condensed phase, the effective causal propagation velocity is set by the Bogoliubov sound speed $c_s$ in the long-wavelength limit, and vanishes continuously at the superfluid--Mott-insulator transition. Applications to composite bosons, the BEC--BCS crossover, and a quantum-gas-microscope measurement protocol are presented. Finally, we demonstrate how the reduced bosonic QCMI serves as a finite-size scaling probe analogous to the Binder cumulant.

</details>


### [158] [Reverse quantum aging: Restoring rhythmicity from quantum aging state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16638)
*Bulti Paul,Biswabibek Bandyopadhyay,Tanmoy Banerjee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究量子振子网络中"老化转变"的逆过程：通过引入受控非相干单玻色子泵浦通道，可使已失稳的网络恢复节律动力学，且所需泵浦强度远弱于完全补偿耦合耗散损失所需的强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究报道了量子老化转变现象，即全局耦合的量子自维持振子网络中，当非活跃节点比例超过临界值时振荡动力学急剧退化。然而，如何从老化态中恢复节律动力学（逆向过程）尚不明确，本文旨在探索这一恢复机制。

Method: 针对全局耦合的活跃—非活跃量子Stuart-Landau振子网络，采用Lindblad形式的自洽量子主方程进行建模，引入受控非相干单玻色子泵浦通道，系统研究泵浦强度对老化转变的抑制与消除作用。

Result: 随着泵浦强度增加，老化转变被逐步抑制并最终完全消除，即使所有振子均为非活跃态也能恢复节律性。值得注意的是，无需完全补偿耦合引起的耗散损失，显著更弱的泵浦强度即可消除老化转变。恢复机制源于非活跃振子Fock态布居向高占据态的重分布。

Conclusion: 受控非相干泵浦是恢复量子振子网络动力学鲁棒性的有效机制，为量子网络中的节律恢复提供了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum aging transition has recently been reported, whereby the oscillatory dynamics of a network of globally coupled quantum self-sustained oscillators is degraded sharply when the fraction of inactive nodes exceeds a critical value. Here we explore the reverse process: the revival of rhythmic dynamics from an aged state in a quantum oscillator network by introducing a controlled incoherent single-boson pumping channel. Using a self-consistent quantum master equation in the Lindblad form for globally coupled active--inactive quantum Stuart--Landau oscillators, we show that increasing the pumping strength progressively suppresses the aging transition and eventually eliminates it, restoring rhythmicity even when all oscillators are inactive. Remarkably, complete compensation of the coupling-induced dissipative loss is not required; a substantially weaker pumping strength is sufficient to eliminate the aging transition. We show that the revival is governed by a redistribution of the Fock-state population of the inactive oscillators toward higher-occupation states. Our results establish controlled incoherent pumping as an effective mechanism for restoring dynamical robustness in quantum oscillator networks.

</details>


### [159] [Euler's thirty-six officers need not all be entangled: real AME(4,6), families and classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16673)
*Palash Pandya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了四体六维系统(AME(4,6))的实数绝对最大纠缠态，其在60个基态上具有非零系数，取值于{±1,√2}/√72，并给出两个闭式族及分类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AME(4,6)是Euler三十六军官问题的量子解，此前所有已知解均为复数，且曾被猜想不存在36阶实正交2-酉矩阵，因此需要构造实数解并研究其结构。

Method: 基于4阶局部对称性的-1本征空间，在该支撑上AME条件化为线性条件；构造10参数族（72基态支撑）与11参数族（60基态支撑）两个闭式解族，并用区间算术验证局域酉与置换下的等价类分离。

Result: 得到60个非零系数的实AME(4,6)态；两族在两个端点处相交并各自损失12个系数，60项支撑上形成11参数族；两族共含4096个实AME态（模对角局域酉），其中1024个为第一族的极限；在局域酉与交换各party下分为恰好20个等价类。

Conclusion: 实数AME(4,6)态确实存在，其结构由线性AME条件与局部对称性决定，并给出了完整的参数族与等价类分类。

Abstract: Absolutely maximally entangled states of four six-level systems, AME$(4,6)$, are the quantum solutions of Euler's problem of the thirty-six officers. Until recently all reported solutions were complex, and it was conjectured that no real orthogonal 2-unitary matrix of order $36$ exists. We give real AME$(4,6)$ states with $60$ non-zero coefficients in $\{\pm1,\sqrt2\}/\sqrt{72}$. Of their thirty-six officers, $12$ are classical, as some must be whenever fewer than $72$ coefficients are non-zero. They lie in the $-1$ eigenspace of an order-$4$ local symmetry, and on their support the AME conditions become linear. We derive two closed-form families of AME$(4,6)$ states that meet at one of these states: a $10$-parameter family that is supported on $72$ basis states, and an $11$-parameter family on $60$ basis states. At each of its two ends the first family loses $12$ coefficients, and the AME states on the remaining $60$ basis states form an $11$-parameter family: the second family at one end, and an analogous family at the other. These two $60$-term families contain $4096$ real AME states up to diagonal local unitaries, $1024$ of which are limits of the first family. They fall into exactly $20$ classes under local unitaries and permutations of the parties, and we certify their separation with interval arithmetic.

</details>


### [160] [Fisher-Information Certification from Bell Statistics: CHSH Bounds, Information Loss, and Finite-Data Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16702)
*Juan Sumaya-Martínez,Omar Olmos-López,Jorge Mulia Rodríguez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将贝尔检验数据与量子度量学相联系，利用基于矩的Fisher信息不等式，从贝尔得分直接导出经典Fisher信息下界，无需完整态层析，并揭示贝尔度量权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝尔检验数据通常用于验证非局域关联，量子度量学关注测量统计对未知参数的敏感性，二者此前相互独立。作者希望将两者在实验可及的贝尔统计层面统一，从而仅凭标准关联测量即可提取度量学保证。

Method: 采用标准基于矩的Fisher信息不等式，从贝尔得分及其参数依赖性直接推导经典Fisher信息下界，不需完整态层析；对CHSH测量建立完整结果记录、各关联子集合与压缩贝尔参数三者之间的信息层级；在偏振相位传感中分析该层级；并推导基于符合计数的有限样本下置信界，分析偏置边际、偶然符合、可见度损失与多对发射的影响。

Result: 在CHSH测量中建立了信息层级，量化了数据压缩的度量学代价；在偏振相位传感中发现贝尔—度量权衡：最大CHSH违背点未必最大化、甚至可能抑制贝尔得分所携带的Fisher信息；同时给出了有限样本下的下置信界及多种实验非理想因素（偏置边际、偶然符合、可见度损失、多对发射）的分析。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了仅使用标准关联测量即可从贝尔型实验中提取度量学保证的实用途径，将贝尔检验与量子度量学在实验可及层面统一起来。

Abstract: Bell-test data are typically used to certify nonlocal correlations, while quantum metrology is concerned with the sensitivity of measurement statistics to unknown parameters. Here we connect these tasks at the level of experimentally accessible Bell statistics. Using standard moment-based Fisher-information inequalities, we derive lower bounds on the classical Fisher information directly from Bell scores and their parameter dependence, without requiring full state tomography. For CHSH measurements we establish a hierarchy between the information contained in the full outcome record, the set of individual correlators, and the compressed Bell parameter, thereby quantifying the metrological cost of data compression. In polarization-phase sensing, this hierarchy reveals a Bell-metrology tradeoff: the point of maximal CHSH violation need not maximize, and can even suppress, the Fisher information carried by the Bell score itself. We further derive finite-sample lower-confidence bounds from coincidence counts and analyze the effects of biased marginals, accidental coincidences, visibility loss, and multipair emission. The framework provides a practical route for extracting metrological guarantees from Bell-type experiments using only standard correlation measurements.

</details>


### [161] [Realistic simulation of universal multiport interferometers with losses within a Gaussian formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16719)
*Sebastiano Corli,Dario Chemoli,Samuele Altilia,Edoardo Suerra,Dario Tamascelli,Simone Cialdi,Enrico Prati*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种高斯模型，通过对非理想干涉仪的实验表征复现含损耗动力学，给出均值向量与协方差矩阵，适用于空间或时间编码的连续变量架构，并量化了多端口干涉仪中损耗偏离均匀分布的漂移，为NISQ光学平台的现实量子算法模拟提供蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子技术是展示量子优势的主要候选平台，但光子损耗是实现容错光量子计算机的主要障碍。对损耗的准确刻画对在含噪后端上进行量子算法的经典模拟至关重要。若仅采用噪声regime下Fock后端模拟，或先验假设光学损耗均匀分布，则可能对特定硬件上算法结果产生错误预测，因此需要更精确的损耗建模方法。

Method: 提出一个高斯模型，通过对非理想干涉仪的实验表征来复现含损耗动力学，输出相应的均值向量和协方差矩阵。该模型可适配任意连续变量架构（空间编码或时间bin编码），并适用于高斯玻色采样或基于general-dyne的算法。此外，通过数值模拟量化光学损耗在多端口干涉仪中偏离均匀分布的程度。

Result: 所提高斯模型能够通过实验表征复现损耗动力学，并可泛化到多种连续变量架构与算法类型；数值模拟展示了损耗均匀性假设的偏差（漂移）程度。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来针对特定器件与架构的表征提供了蓝图，适用于潜在的错误纠正与缓解技术，并为NISQ光学平台上量子算法的现实模拟铺平了道路。

Abstract: Photonic technologies are among the leading platform contenders for demonstrating quantum advantage. Nevertheless, photon losses represent the major hurdle for the realization of a fault-tolerant optical quantum computer, and the need for an accurate characterization of this phenomenon is crucial for classical simulations of quantum algorithms on noisy backends. The noisy regime would require the simulation of the algorithm on a Fock backend or rather assuming the a priori hypothesis on the uniformity of the optical losses, which may lead to wrong predictions about the outcome from the algorithm on a cerain hardware. We propose a Gaussian model that can reproduce a lossy dynamics through the experimental characterization of imperfect interferometers, yielding the corresponding mean vector and the covariance matrix. Such model can be adapted to any continuous-variable architecture (spatial or time-bin encoded) and for both the Gaussian boson sampling or general-dyne based algorithms. Eventually, we quantify the drift of optical losses from being uniform throughout the multiport interferometer by a numerical simulation. This work proposes a blueprint for future characterizations, tailor-suited for specific devices and architectures, as well as for potential error correction and mitigation techniques, and it paves the way for realistic simulations of quantum algorithms on NISQ optical platforms.

</details>


### [162] [The Quantum Character Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16827)
*Kagwe A. Muchane*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论离散特征变换——限定在群代数中心 $Z(\mathbb{C}[G])$ 上的群傅里叶变换，在其中类函数构成交换卷积代数、不可约特征构成其谱基；并给出类卷积的量子算法及其在对称适配多体动力学、分子与光谱对称分析、量子行走与隐子群问题中的应用与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bastidas 等（2025）强调利用群对称性降低计算开销、统一凝聚态物理与从头算量子化学模拟方法。本文延续这一动机，聚焦群代数中心结构，寻求对任意有限群可高效实现的量子特征变换与类卷积算法，以推广对称性工具的应用范围。

Method: (1) 定义离散特征变换为限定在群代数中心 $Z(\mathbb{C}[G])$ 上的群傅里叶变换；(2) 指出归一化特征表可通过限制算子获得，从而对任何具有高效量子傅里叶变换的有限群给出高效量子特征变换；(3) 给出类卷积的两种互补量子算法：基于直接类和（class-sum）块编码的方法，以及在特征基上的谱实现方法。

Result: 建立了离散特征变换的形式化框架：在 $Z(\mathbb{C}[G])$ 中类函数形成交换卷积代数，不可约特征为其谱基；证明归一化特征表可由限制算子得到，故高效量子特征变换可由高效量子傅里叶变换导出；给出类卷积的直接类和块编码与特征基谱实现两类互补量子算法。

Conclusion: 该工作将离散特征变换作为统一工具，应用于对称适配多体动力学、分子与光谱对称分析、量子行走，并刻画了隐子群问题中所用的中心信息及其在图同构问题上的局限性。

Abstract: Bastidas et al. (2025) highlighted the importance of exploiting group symmetries to reduce computational overhead and unify simulation methods across both condensed matter physics and ab initio quantum chemistry. I discuss the discrete character transform, which is the group Fourier transform restricted to $Z(\mathbb{C}[G])$, the center of the group algebra, where class functions form a commutative convolution algebra and irreducible characters are its spectral basis. The normalized character table of a non-abelian group can be obtained through a restriction operator, which means an efficient quantum character transform for any finite group with an efficient quantum Fourier transform. I give complementary quantum algorithms for class convolution based on direct class-sum block encoding and spectral implementation in the character basis, explaining their applications to symmetry-adapted many-body dynamics, molecular and spectroscopic symmetry analysis, and quantum walks, as well as characterizing the central information used in hidden-subgroup problems, including its limitations for graph isomorphism.

</details>


### [163] [Geodesic steering for rapid dissipative entanglement distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16854)
*Keshav Venkataraman,Pablo Martinez-Azcona,Marko Ljubotina,Adrian Parra-Rodriguez,Peter Rabl*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种最优控制策略，通过设计控制脉冲使开放量子系统沿瞬时稳态轨迹演化并最小化非绝热跃迁泄漏，从而加速耗散制备复杂强纠缠多体态，且控制参数可解析求得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耗散型储备工程可制备纠缠态，但淬火后裸弛豫动力学制备时间长，难以兼容高保真量子信息处理；需寻求加速方案。

Method: 类比对孤立量子系统的最优操控，构造沿瞬时稳态轨迹的控制脉冲序列，抑制非绝热跃迁导致的泄漏；在诸多情形下可解析导出控制参数，无需对完整多体主方程做昂贵数值模拟；应用于耦合公共光子波导或分布式压缩库的量子比特系统，制备双体与多体纠缠态。

Result: 在两种场景下，该最优控制策略显著优于淬火后的裸弛豫动力学，将近最大纠缠态的制备时间缩短数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该加速使耗散纠缠分发协议可与高保真量子信息处理应用兼容，并更广泛地展示了泄漏最小化对加速储备工程方案的潜力。

Abstract: We describe an optimal-control strategy designed to accelerate the dissipative preparation of complex, highly entangled quantum many-body states. Analogous to optimal steering of isolated quantum systems, our scheme identifies control pulses that guide the evolution of open quantum systems along trajectories of instantaneous steady states while minimizing leakage resulting from non-adiabatic transitions. In many cases of interest, these control parameters can be derived analytically and without the need for computationally expensive numerical simulations of the full many-body master equation. We apply this technique for the preparation of bi- and multipartite entangled states in qubit systems coupled to a common photonic waveguide or a distributed squeezed reservoir. In both scenarios, our optimal-control strategy significantly outperforms the bare relaxation dynamics after a quench, reducing preparation times of near-maximally entangled states by orders of magnitude. This drastic speedup makes dissipative entanglement distribution protocols compatible with high-fidelity quantum information processing applications and illustrates more broadly the potential of leakage minimization for accelerating reservoir-engineering schemes.

</details>


### [164] [Geomagic Square Representation of a Real AME$(4, 6)$ State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16882)
*Wojciech Bruzda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个实数 AME(4,6) 态，并给出其几何化的“geomagic square”图示表示，可由图像完整重建张量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望为局部维度为 6 的四体绝对最大纠缠（AME）态寻找一种图像化表示，从而以直观视觉结构刻画其复杂张量。

Method: 从带符号 Pauli 矩阵表出发，独立构造实数 AME(4,6) 态；其 two-unitary 矩阵含 60 个非零元，取值为 1 或 ±1/√2；再通过局部等价变换得到对应的 genuine geomagic square 表示。

Result: 所得 geomagic square 中，每行、每列及主对角线均铺满一个六边形，全部 36 个拼块两两不全等；颜色、形状和阴影共同保留完整张量信息。

Conclusion: 该图示不仅满足几何魔方性质，还能从图像重建原 AME(4,6) 张量，表明四体纠缠态可被紧凑且无损地可视化。

Abstract: Seeking a pictorial representation of four-party absolutely maximally entangled (AME) states of local size six, we independently construct a real AME$(4,6)$ state from a table of signed Pauli matrices. Its two-unitary matrix has $60$ nonzero entries, equal to $1$ or $\pm 1/\sqrt{2}$. A locally equivalent representative yields a genuine geomagic square, where every row, column and main diagonal tiles a hexagon and all $36$ pieces are pairwise non-congruent. Colors, shapes and hatching retain the full tensor, allowing its reconstruction from the picture.

</details>


### [165] [Thermal aliases in quantum noise: sampling clocks and pulse widths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16889)
*Zi-Xuan Wang,Xiu-Hao Deng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究在耦合谱未知的平稳高斯振子环境中，集体量子噪声测量能否区分不同模式温度与同一共同温度，给出精确热混叠的带宽阈值及可排除共同温度拟合的采样设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无法实现模式分辨访问的条件下，需要判断哪种新观测能使热模型检验具有信息量，从而区分各模式温度与单一共同温度。

Method: 针对平稳高斯振子环境，推导整数时间采样格点上精确热混叠的锐利带宽阈值；利用量子响应中的温度序依赖；采用分数时钟采样设计（固定足够整数时间关联后增加一次分数时间涨落观测）与校准双脉冲宽度方案，并通过二值读出协议结合随机交织匹配参考、集体方差与相干性检验来处理振子损耗和可预测读出漂移。

Result: 阈值依赖温度序；在第一混叠周期内，固定足够的整数时间关联后，一次额外分数时间涨落观测即可排除所有共同温度拟合；双脉冲宽度方案要求两个宽度探测同一谱并保持各自测量方差尺度；指定双模式示例中连续频率证书覆盖所有共同温度及共享脉冲斜坡的不确定性；分数时钟与双宽度设计分别需约3.8×10^7和1.6×10^8个结果，以满足至多5%第一类错误与至少95%检验功效。

Conclusion: 该结果识别出在缺乏模式分辨访问时使热模型检验具有信息量的新观测方式；所给理论与数值获取保证假设制备、谱支撑和控制规格均已独立验证，验证成本另计。

Abstract: Can collective quantum-noise measurements distinguish different mode temperatures from one common temperature when the coupling spectrum is unknown? For stationary Gaussian oscillator environments, we derive a sharp bandwidth threshold for an exact thermal alias on the integer-time sampling lattice. The threshold depends on temperature order through the quantum response. For frequencies within the first alias period, fixing sufficient integer-time correlations makes one additional fractional-time fluctuation observation enough to exclude every common-temperature fit. A calibrated second pulse width provides another route through the same collective port: both widths must probe one spectrum and retain their measured variance scales. For a specified two-mode example, continuous-frequency certificates cover all common temperatures and uncertainty in a shared pulse ramp. A binary-readout protocol combines randomly interleaved matched references with collective-variance and coherence checks to handle Gaussian oscillator loss and predictable readout drift. The selected fractional-clock and two-width designs require approximately $3.8\times10^7$ and $1.6\times10^8$ outcomes for at most 5\% type-I error and at least 95\% power on the stated alternative class. These theoretical and numerical acquisition guarantees assume independently validated preparation, spectral support and control specifications; validation costs are additional. The result identifies which new observation makes thermal-model testing informative when mode-resolved access is unavailable.

</details>


### [166] [Photon-stimulated decay of $χ$-matched ancillas causes unheralded bit flips in bosonic codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17017)
*Aleksandr Gulkanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 玻色码用transmon辅助比特读取错误症状时，码中的光子会以辅助比特衰减率的一阶效应打开原本被宇称禁戒的 $f\to g$ 衰变，产生无预警的比特翻转，限制噪声偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色码通常假设辅助比特的 $g$、$f$ 能级宇称相同且色散位移匹配，因而 $f\to e$ 可被预警且不破坏码，而 $f\to g$ 被宇称禁戒。作者质疑这一保护机制在真实条件下是否会因码模子的光子而失效。

Method: 通过分析辅助比特的衰减与电荷噪声，研究码模子修饰（dressing）导致的声子/光子受激拉曼衰变通道 $|f,n\rangle\to|g,n+1\rangle$，并将结果与二阶双衰变 $f\to e\to g$ 比较；在 Ocelot 重复猫码的微观模型中评估该通道对门错误的影响。

Result: 该衰变率为 $f\to e$ 速率的 $(n+1)(χ_{ge}/2g_{01})^2$ 倍（平滑噪声谱）；当匹配由失谐设定时，每门每光子代价约 $\approxΓ_{fe}/α$，与耦合强度无关。该通道无预警且以约 1/2 概率翻转猫态，并随辅助比特改善而超过二阶双衰变。在 Ocelot 重复猫码中，门比特翻转本底随猫尺寸线性增长，从四光子到十光子增大 1.2–1.7 倍，将噪声偏置限制在 $\sim 10^3$。

Conclusion: 码模高激发数会以一阶效应破坏辅助比特的宇称保护，形成无预警比特翻转通道并限制噪声偏置；现有已发表门数据止于四光子，尚无法验证这一增长趋势，需更高光子数实验检验。

Abstract: Bosonic codes often read out their syndrome with a transmon ancilla whose computational levels $g$ and $f$ have equal parity and matched dispersive shifts: a decay $f\to e$ is then heralded and leaves the code intact, while the direct $f\to g$ is forbidden by parity. We show that the photons of the code open this decay at first order in the ancilla's decay rate. Dressing by the code mode lets the charge noise that limits the ancilla's $T_1$ drive a photon-stimulated Raman decay $|f,n\rangle\to|g,n+1\rangle$, at $(n+1)(χ_{ge}/2g_{01})^2$ times the $f\to e$ rate for a smooth noise spectrum. When the matching is set by the detuning, the decay costs $\approxΓ_{fe}/α$ per gate and photon, whatever the coupling strength; a deliberate mismatch or matching by a drive lowers this cost, and a qubit ancilla has no such channel. The opened decay is unheralded and flips a cat with probability $\approx 1/2$; being first order, it overtakes the second-order double decay $f\to e\to g$ as the ancilla improves. In a microscopic model of the Ocelot repetition cat code, it makes the bit-flip floor of the gates grow linearly with the cat size, by a factor 1.2-1.7 from four to ten photons, which would limit the noise bias to $\sim 10^3$. The published gate data end at four photons and do not yet test this growth.

</details>


### [167] [Entanglement Sudden Death in Gapped Ground States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17038)
*Robert Ferydouni,Thomas Jackson,Samuel O. Scalet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究量子纠缠的"空间突然死亡"现象，定义纠缠长度并证明：任意维度有能隙基态的纠缠长度在有限子系统上有限；一维情形下，正规矩阵乘积态具有与A、C无关的有限纠缠长度，且具此性质的纯态必有键维与A、C无关的矩阵乘积态表示，故纠缠长度可区分一维矩阵乘积态与其他纯态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究关注纠缠随时间的"突然死亡"，而纠缠随空间距离的消失（空间突然死亡）及其与张量网络表示的关系尚不清晰。作者希望用纠缠长度这一量刻画基态关联的空间结构，并厘清其与矩阵乘积态这一一维高效表示之间的本质联系。

Method: 在三分系统ABC上取密度矩阵ρ_ABC，令B在空间上分隔A与C，将纠缠长度ℓ(A,C)定义为对B取偏迹后约化密度矩阵ρ_AC变为可分离时的距离。对任意维有能隙基态分析有限子系统上的纠缠长度；在一维情形下结合矩阵乘积态表示与纠缠长度的界进行证明。

Result: 证明了任意维度有能隙基态的纠缠长度在有限子系统上有限；在一维中，正规矩阵乘积态的纠缠长度有限且与A、C的选择无关；反之，具有这种一致有限纠缠长度的纯态可写成键维不依赖于A、C的矩阵乘积态。

Conclusion: 纠缠长度是一维系统中区分矩阵乘积态与所有其他纯态的一个判据，揭示了纠缠的空间衰减性质与张量网络可表示性之间的等价关系，为理解有能隙基态的低复杂度结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: Spatial sudden death of entanglement refers to the disappearance of quantum correlations between subsystems after a certain distance called the entanglement length. Specifically, given a density matrix $ρ_{ABC}$ on a tripartite system $ABC$ with $B$ spatially separating $A$ and $C$, the entanglement length is the distance $\ell(A,C)$ after which the reduced density matrix $ρ_{AC}$, given by tracing out the $B$ subsystem, is separable. We show in the case of gapped ground states in any dimension that the entanglement length is finite on finite subsystems. Restricting to one-dimensional systems, we prove that normal matrix product states have a finite entanglement length independent of $A$ and $C$, and that a finite pure state with such uniform entanglement length has a matrix product state representation with bond dimension independent of $A$ and $C$. Consequently, in one dimension, entanglement length distinguishes matrix product states from all other pure states.

</details>


### [168] [Enhanced Quantum Metrology via Saddle-Point Scrambling in Phase Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17047)
*Lei Shao,Hai-Jun Xing,Libin Fu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子混纺（scrambling）集成方案，利用双模BEC相空间中的非线性动力学轨迹与几何性质优化初态选择与参数编码，在非临界参数区实现Heisenberg标度的测量精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性效应可提升量子计量精度，但既有临界性增强协议要求参数逼近临界点，适用参数范围狭窄，亟需扩展至更宽参数区间的增强机制。

Method: 以双模玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚为典型模型，利用相空间中非线性动力学轨迹及几何特性设计量子混纺集成方案；选取位于鞍点和分界线（separatrix）上的无关联自旋相干态作为初态并进行参数编码；给出长时演化下量子Fisher信息的解析表达式。

Result: 在特定参数范围内，采用鞍点与分界线处的自旋相干态可使命中精度达到Heisenberg标度；长时演化的量子Fisher信息具有解析形式；增强机制由能谱对参数变化的敏感性所支配。

Conclusion: 相比需逼近临界点的临界性增强协议，该方案在更宽参数范围内具有适用性优势，并可推广至含单轴与双轴反向扭曲相互作用及混沌动力学的模型，为非线性动力学提升量子参数估计精度提供了新视角。

Abstract: Nonlinear effects are widely utilized in quantum metrology to enhance measurement precision by leveraging complex dynamical behaviors. Here, we propose a quantum scrambling-integrated scheme that optimizes the selection of initial states and parameter encoding by exploiting the nonlinear dynamical trajectories and their geometric properties in phase space. We consider a two-mode Bose-Einstein condensate as a typical example. Within a certain parameter range, using uncorrelated spin coherent states located at saddle points and separatrices enables the measurement precision to reach the Heisenberg scaling. An analytical expression for the quantum Fisher information under long-time evolution is provided, and our analysis further reveals that the enhancement mechanism is governed by the sensitivity of the energy spectrum to parameter variations. Compared to the criticality-enhanced protocol that requires parameters to approach the critical point, our scheme offers a distinct advantage in its applicability over a broader range of parameters. Moreover, this scheme can also be applied to models that include one- and two-axis countertwisting interactions and chaotic dynamics. This Letter offers a new perspective for advancing the application of nonlinear dynamics to enhance the parameter estimation precision in quantum systems.

</details>


### [169] [Optical Detection of Coherent Nuclear Spin Control in a Molecular System at Room Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17050)
*Eoin O. Irving,Ziqiu Huang,Max Attwood,Sam L. Bayliss,Sarah K. Mann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 首次在室温下实现分子体系核磁共振的光学探测与相干核自旋操控，展示了6,13-二氮杂并五苯光激发三重态中¹⁴N核自旋的光学检测拉比振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子自旋体系是新兴量子传感平台，具有可调量子比特特性和分子尺度空间分辨能力。耦合核自旋因寿命更长，是提升传感性能的潜在资源，但需在室温下实现核自旋的初始化、相干控制与读出。

Method: 利用有机生色团6,13-二氮杂并五苯的光激发三重态，通过光学方法探测¹⁴N核磁共振信号，并实现核自旋的相干操控；借助电子自旋与¹⁴N核之间强超精细耦合提升核拉比频率。

Result: 实现了室温下分子体系核磁共振的光学探测及相干核自旋操控，观测到¹⁴N核自旋的光学检测拉比振荡，并因强超精细耦合获得快速的核自旋相干旋转。

Conclusion: 该工作为将核自旋用作记忆单元、增强分子基量子传感性能奠定了基础。

Abstract: Molecular systems with optically addressable electron spins are emerging as versatile quantum sensing platforms, offering tunable qubit properties and molecular-scale spatial control. Owing to their longer lifetimes, coupled nuclear spins provide an attractive resource for enhancing sensing performance. For molecular spin systems, the ability to initialise, coherently control and readout nuclear spins at room temperature is crucial for realising such benefits. Here, we present the first optical detection of nuclear magnetic resonance and coherent nuclear spin control with a molecular system at room temperature. Specifically, we demonstrate optically detected Rabi oscillations of $^{14}$N nuclear spins in the photoexcited triplet state of the organic chromophore 6,13-diazapentacene. The strong hyperfine coupling between the electronic spin and $^{14}$N nuclei enhances the nuclear Rabi frequency, enabling fast coherent nuclear-spin rotations. This work lays the foundation for leveraging nuclear spins as memories for enhanced molecular-based quantum sensing.

</details>


### [170] [Architecting Instruction Execution for Scalable Fault-Tolerant Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17058)
*Dmitry Filippov,Justin Hogaboam,Mathias Soeken,Raghu Gatta,Prakash Murali,Matthias Troyer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出ARIES指令集层级分类，并构建端到端评测工具流，系统评估不同量子指令集架构（QISA）抽象层在低温固定连接量子平台上的权衡，发现Level 4 QISA（局域逻辑操作）在热预算与硬件复杂度之间取得最佳架构平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展容错量子计算需要在紧耦合的经典-量子控制栈上连续编排数百万次操作，其中关键挑战是确定最优的硬件-软件边界，即量子指令集架构（QISA）。对于超导和拓扑马约拉纳等低温、固定连接量子平台，现有QISA要么层级过低，在规模化时违反低温热预算；要么层级过高，给控制引擎带来无法实现的复杂度。

Method: 引入ARIES指令集层级（ARIES instruction set hierarchy），将QISA划分为不同抽象层级；构建端到端评测工具流，包含周期精确的经典驱动器与硬件引擎模拟器；在超导与拓扑量子平台上选取八个量子应用，测量指令带宽、收发器功耗耗散与测量处理开销。

Result: 物理层抽象超出稀释制冷机热极限达数个数量级；规范层（canonical-level）抽象显著低估硬件路由拥塞与执行运行时；Level 4 QISA（局域逻辑操作）提供最优的架构平衡。

Conclusion: 随着量子硬件平台在未来数年向容错量子计算扩展，本文发现为其经典控制栈与指令接口设计提供了具体的架构指导。

Abstract: Scaling fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) requires orchestrating millions of operations continuously across a tightly coupled classical-quantum control stack. A critical challenge in this orchestration is determining the optimal hardware-software boundary, i.e., the Quantum Instruction Set Architecture (QISA). We focus on cryogenic, fixed-connectivity qubit platforms, such as superconducting and topological Majorana qubits. For these, existing QISA choices either operate at too low a level, violating cryogenic thermal budgets at scale, or at too high a level, imposing unrealizable complexity on the control engine. To reason about these tradeoffs, we introduce the ARIES instruction set hierarchy, which categorizes QISAs into distinct abstraction levels. To systematically evaluate these levels, we develop an end-to-end evaluation toolflow featuring a cycle-accurate classical driver and hardware engine simulator. Using eight quantum applications across superconducting and topological qubit platforms, we measure instruction bandwidth, transceiver power dissipation, and measurement processing overheads. We demonstrate that while physical-level abstractions exceed the thermal limits of dilution refrigerators by orders of magnitude, canonical-level abstractions significantly underestimate hardware routing congestion and execution runtimes. Our Level 4 QISA (local logical operations) offers an optimal architectural balance. As quantum hardware platforms scale toward FTQC in the coming years, our findings provide concrete architectural guidance for the design of their classical control stacks and instruction interfaces.

</details>


### [171] [Cyclotomic Structure and Exact Recurrences in Floquet Many-Body Quantum Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17137)
*Amit Anand,Dinesh Valluri,Shohini Ghose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为周期驱动量子系统的精确Floquet回归建立了充要的算术判据，并给出存在回归时的最小回归周期；以kicked Ising链为例，在Clifford与非Clifford区均实现精确回归，并揭示非Clifford区magic的周期性复活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期驱动量子系统可呈现混沌等复杂动力学，但在特殊参数下演化可精确回归。近期若干量子协议利用精确Floquet回归展示真实量子优势，因此需要从Floquet幺正元的算术结构出发，给出精确回归的系统判据及其周期刻画。

Method: 通过研究Floquet幺正元的算术结构（数域/代数数性质），建立精确回归的充要算术判据，并确定最小回归周期；仅需已知哈密顿量参数关联的数域。以kicked Ising链作为具体模型进行验证，分别考察Clifford与非Clifford两种参数区制。

Result: 建立了精确回归的充要算术判据，并给出最小回归周期；由于回归是幺正元的全局性质，故对任意初态均成立，应用时只需知道哈密顿量参数对应的数域。在kicked Ising链中，于Clifford与非Clifford区制均找到精确回归；在回归的非Clifford区制，magic在中间时刻被产生，并因全Floquet幺正元的回归而发生精确周期性复活。

Conclusion: 该工作将数论、量子多体动力学与量子信息相连接，表明精确回归不局限于Clifford电路，并为magic产生与量子硬件基准测试开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Periodically driven quantum systems exhibit a wide range of complex dynamics, including chaos. Yet, at special parameter values, their evolution can recur exactly. Several quantum protocols have recently been proposed that exploit exact Floquet recurrences to demonstrate genuine quantum advantage. Such recurrences can be studied through the arithmetic structure of the Floquet unitary. In this work, we establish a necessary and sufficient arithmetic criterion for exact recurrence and determine the minimal recurrence period when one exists. Because recurrence is a global property of the unitary, it holds for every initial state. Applying our framework requires only knowledge of the number field associated with the Hamiltonian parameters. We demonstrate the method using the kicked Ising chain, establishing exact recurrences in both Clifford and non-Clifford regimes. We further show that, in the recurrent non-Clifford regime, magic--a resource for quantum computation--is generated at intermediate times and undergoes exact periodic revivals enforced by the recurrence of the full Floquet unitary. By connecting number theory, quantum many-body dynamics, and quantum information, our work shows that exact recurrence extends beyond Clifford circuits and opens new avenues for magic generation and quantum hardware benchmarking.

</details>


### [172] [On the Computational Complexity of Blind Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17145)
*Rex Fleur,Tzula B. Propp,Sebastian Feld*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建并使用了通用砖墙转译器 MasonQ，证明量子计算效率优势与隐私优势可以共存，给出 UBQC 砖墙图深度上界并分析盲量子机器学习算法的资源估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究核心问题是：量子在计算效率上的优势能否与量子在隐私上的优势共存。作者试图连接两个看似不同的范式：基于测量的通用盲量子计算（UBQC）与量子机器学习（QML）算法（如 HHL、量子推荐系统）。

Method: 开发并基于 MasonQ——一个通用砖墙转译器，将任意量子电路映射为计算砖墙图态。以最优 Qiskit 电路为输入，给出 MasonQ 在砖墙图宽度与深度渐近缩放意义下（至多对数修正）的最优性证明，并据此推导 UBQC 砖墙图深度上界及多个盲量子机器学习算法的深度界。

Result: 证明 UBQC 砖墙图深度上界为 D = O(ωn)，其中 ω 为电路宽度，n 为计算门复杂度。据此得到量子傅里叶变换、HHL 算法、推荐系统、量子 transformer 等代表性 B-QML 算法的深度界，并给出近期演示的资源估计：最小 B-QML 实现仅需 12 量子比特量子服务器。

Conclusion: 量子计算效率优势与隐私优势可以共存。MasonQ 是首个功能完整且最优的通用“电路到砖墙图”转译器，能够从第一性原理实现完整砖墙转译流程，并支持近期实验与渐近情形下盲实现的系统性成本分析。

Abstract: Can quantum advantages in computational efficiency coexist with quantum advantages in privacy? In this paper, we construct and use MasonQ, a universal brickwork transpiler, to answer this question with a resounding yes. We approach this question by bridging two seemingly disparate paradigms: universal blind quantum computation (UBQC), based on measurement-based quantum computation, and quantum machine learning (QML) algorithms such as the Harrow-Hassidim-Lloyd (HHL) algorithm and quantum recommendation systems.
  We provide new upper bounds on the depth of UBQC brickwork graphs, establishing that $D = \mathcal{O}(ωn)$, where $ω$ is the circuit width and $n$ its computational gate complexity. This is enabled by our newly developed transpiler MasonQ, which maps arbitrary quantum circuits to computational brickwork graph states. Given an optimal Qiskit circuit as input, we prove optimality (up to logarithmic corrections) of the MasonQ transpiler in terms of asymptotic scaling of the total brickwork graph width and depth after decomposition into bricks, enabling systematic cost analyses of blind implementations in near-term experiments and asymptotic regimes.
  Leveraging MasonQ, we derive depth bounds for several representative blind quantum machine learning (B-QML) algorithms, including the quantum Fourier transform, the HHL algorithm, recommendation systems, and quantum transformers. Lastly, we analyze concrete resource estimates for near-term demonstrations of blind quantum machine learning. We propose a minimal B-QML implementation requiring only a $12$ qubit quantum server.
  To the best of our knowledge, MasonQ is the first functional and optimal universal circuit-to-brickwork-graph transpiler to implement the full brickwork transpilation process from first principles.

</details>


### [173] [Programmable Floquet Response for Quantum Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17156)
*Dionisio Cendoya,Kang Xu,Lisandro Buljubasich,Carlos A. Meriles*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于周期驱动二能级系统中可编程Floquet锁定的新型量子传感范式，将信息编码于驱动下的布居弛豫动力学而非相位积累，实现直流与频率选择性交流传感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子传感依赖相位积累，其灵敏度本质上受限于传感系统的相干时间，构成根本性瓶颈。

Method: 利用周期驱动二能级系统产生有效Floquet锁定场，其取向与幅度依赖全局失谐；通过控制不同Floquet锁定流形之间的转移，将外部扰动映射为可工程化的弛豫响应，并借驱动序列实现可编程调控。

Result: 数值验证了该方法在直流与频率选择性交流传感中的可行性，并表明在存在慢速仪器漂移时，响应的时间编码可提升测量精度。

Conclusion: 结果展示了可编程Floquet响应的潜力，为驱动量子系统中的新型传感策略开辟了道路。

Abstract: Quantum sensing is typically based on phase accumulation and is therefore fundamentally tied to the coherence time of the sensing system. Here we introduce an alternative sensing paradigm based on programmable Floquet locking in periodically driven two-level systems. We show that periodic control generates an effective Floquet locking field whose orientation and amplitude depend on global offset, allowing information to be encoded in the driven population relaxation dynamics, not in the integrated phase. By controlling transfer between distinct Floquet locking manifolds, external perturbations can be mapped onto engineered relaxation responses whose characteristics are programmable through the driving sequence. We numerically demonstrate this approach for both DC and frequency-selective AC sensing, and show that temporal encoding of the response can provide enhanced precision in the presence of slow instrumental fluctuations. These results illustrate the potential of programmable Floquet responses and open the way to exploring new sensing strategies in driven quantum systems.

</details>


### [174] [Bosonic unstructured search: quantum enhancement without entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17163)
*Yu-Xin Wang,Erfan Abbasgholinejad,Supratik Sarkar,Sharoon Austin,Andrew M. Childs,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Mohammad Hafezi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在能量约束下利用多模玻色子系统进行非结构化搜索。针对连续时间与离散相位标记预言机，给出了达到最优查询复杂度的算法与匹配下界，并分析了无损耗与有损耗情形下非高斯性、纠缠的作用，最后提出实验实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非结构化搜索（如Grover）未考虑能量/玻色子数约束。在低玻色子数预算且大规模搜索空间的物理场景（如成像、器件校准）中，需要研究多模玻色子系统在能量约束下的搜索极限，明确最优查询复杂度及所需量子资源。

Method: 考虑N个模式中定位未知标记模式，受时间平均玻色子数预算n_b≫1约束。分析两类预言机：(1)连续时间相移预言机，强度B_0，代价为总预言机时间；(2)离散预言机，固定相位φ_0，代价为查询次数。通过构造算法并证明匹配下界来分析查询复杂度，并在均匀玻色子损耗率γ下推导下界。

Result: 连续时间预言机算法查询复杂度为O(√N/(n_b B_0))；离散预言机在n_b|sin(φ_0/2)|=O(1)时为O(√N/(n_b|sin(φ_0/2)|))，在n_b|sin(φ_0/2)|=Ω(1)时为O(max{1,√(N/(n_b|sin(φ_0/2)|))})。证明了匹配下界，算法渐近最优。无损耗时最优联合缩放需非高斯性但不需要纠缠。均匀损耗率γ下查询下界为Ω(γN/(n_b B_0^2))，当γ=Ω(B_0√(n_b/N))时用相干态探测可达。

Conclusion: 在能量约束的多模玻色子系统中，非结构化搜索的查询复杂度可通过非高斯性实现N与n_b的最优联合缩放，纠缠并非必需；损耗会使性能受限，但特定损耗区间可用相干态达到下界。该框架可通过多程光学显微或超导电路实现，有望在低玻色子数预算下加速成像与器件校准。

Abstract: We consider unstructured search using multimode bosonic quantum systems under energy constraints. The task is to locate an unknown marked mode among $N$ possibilities using arbitrary control, subject to a time-averaged mean-boson-number budget $n_{\text{b}}\gg 1$. We analyze a continuous-time phase-shift oracle of strength $B_{0}$, with cost given by the total oracle time, and a discrete oracle with fixed phase $φ_{0}$, with cost given by the number of queries. We give algorithms that identify the target with query complexity $O(\sqrt{N}/{n_{\text{b}}}B_{0})$ for continuous-time oracles or $O(\sqrt{N}/n_{\text{b}} |{\sin(φ_{0}/{2})}|)$ for discrete oracles with $n_{\text{b}} |{\sin(φ_{0}/{2})}|=O(1)$. In contrast, for discrete oracles with $n_{\text{b}} |{\sin(φ_{0}/{2})}|=Ω(1)$, we provide an algorithm using $O(\max\{1,\sqrt{N/n_{\text{b}}|{\sin(φ_{0}/{2})}|}\})$ queries. We also prove matching lower bounds on the query complexity, showing that these algorithms are asymptotically optimal. Furthermore, we show that, in the absence of loss, non-Gaussianity is necessary to achieve the optimal joint query scaling in $N$ and $n_{\text{b}}$, but entanglement is not. Under uniform boson loss at rate $γ$, we prove a query lower bound of $Ω( γN / n _{\text{b}} B_{0}^{2} )$, attained for $γ= Ω(B_{0} \sqrt{n _{\text{b}}/N})$ using coherent-state probes. Finally, we propose experimental implementations using multipass optical microscopy or superconducting circuits as potential routes to speeding up imaging or device calibration with a low boson-number budget.

</details>


### [175] [Fractional and quantum Latin squares: real solutions of quantum 36 officers problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17170)
*Mykhailo Hontarenko,Karol Życzkowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将拉丁方的符号替换为概率或量子态，联系经典、分数与量子拉丁方及Euler量子36军官问题的实数解；通过四环面张量系数的循环移位拟设得到四体六能级实数AME态，并分析其概率表示与局域酉等价类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典拉丁方可推广为分数拉丁方（符号替换为概率）与量子拉丁方（符号替换为量子态），但两类扩展之间的联系尚不清晰；同时Euler量子36军官问题及多体最大纠缠态的概率图像与相干条件之间的区别需要进一步阐明。

Method: 在四环面张量系数上施加同时循环移位拟设，构造四体六能级实数AME态；通过局域正交基变换得到96个非零振幅的表示；从对应量子正交拉丁方导出两个单方阵列作为分数拉丁方；并与Bruzda独立实数构造及其符号变体进行对比以区分局域酉等价类；进一步将概率表示推广到七体与九体六能级AME态，分析其振幅矩阵中的复Hadamard块。

Result: 得到一个四体六能级实数AME态及其96非零振幅代表；对应量子正交拉丁方导出分数拉丁方；符号差异区分出三个局域酉等价类，其中两类概率相同；七体与九体六能级AME态的振幅矩阵分别揭示48个3^3阶和6个6^4阶复Hadamard块。

Conclusion: 概率表示能够刻画可从概率图像读取的信息，而最大纠缠所需的相干条件需要额外处理；该工作分离了二者并统一了经典、分数与量子拉丁方扩展。

Abstract: A Latin square can be extended by replacing its symbols with probabilities or quantum states. We relate these two extensions through explicit examples, from classical and fractional Latin squares to quantum Latin squares and a real solution of Euler's quantum 36 officers problem. A simultaneous cyclic-shift ansatz on a four-torus of tensor coefficients gives a real absolutely maximally entangled (AME) state of four six-level systems. A local orthogonal change of basis yields a representative with 96 nonzero amplitudes. Two single-party arrays obtained from the corresponding quantum orthogonal Latin square yield fractional Latin squares. Signs matter: comparison with the independent real construction of Bruzda and its sign variant distinguishes three local-unitary classes, two of which have identical probabilities. We extend this probability representation to existing seven- and nine-party AME states with six levels each. Their amplitude matrices reveal 48 complex Hadamard blocks of order $3^3$ and six of order $6^4$, respectively. These constructions separate what can be read from a probability picture from the coherent conditions responsible for maximal entanglement.

</details>


### [176] [Moving quantum sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17176)
*Evan Zhang,Erfan Abbasgholinejad,Yu-Xin Wang,Ali Fahimniya,Daniel J. Spencer,Sean R. Muleady,Mohammad Hafezi,Andrew M. Childs,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子传感器受控运动在局域场探测任务中的度量优势：经典运动的最优探测时间标度为 Θ(N/B)，相干空间叠加将其降为 Θ(√N/B)；扩展到 M 个传感器后，无纠缠相干并行搜索给出 T=Θ(√(N/M)/B)，纠缠进一步给出 T=Θ(√N/(MB))，并匹配下界证明最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在探测未知位置局域场这一任务中，传感头的受控运动（尤其是量子运动）是否带来度量学优势，并厘清经典运动、空间相干性与纠缠各自在空间场探测中的作用分工。

Method: 设定强度已知 B 的局域场占据搜索空间的连续未知区域（占比 1/N），单量子比特传感器受控总时间 T 以最大化探测概率；在"场特征时间远慢于传感器区域间移动时间"的慢场区制下，区分经典运动（确定轨迹）与量子运动（位置相干叠加）。对经典移动传感器推导最优多站点协议并给出精确最大探测概率；将相干空间叠加下的单传感器问题映射为哈密顿量搜索问题；再扩展至 M 个传感器，按可用空间量子资源与传感器间量子资源刻画最优时间标度层级；并用匹配下界验证任意控制与辅助系统下的最优性。

Result: 单经典移动传感器：得到最优多站点协议及精确最大探测概率，成功探测所需询问时间为 Θ(N/B)。允许相干空间叠加：问题映射到哈密顿量搜索，询问时间由 Θ(N/B) 降至 Θ(√N/B)。M 个传感器：无纠缠但相干移动的传感器通过并行搜索互不重叠区域达到 T=Θ(√(N/M)/B)；传感器间纠缠可增强有效场耦合，进一步达到 T=Θ(√N/(MB))。匹配下界表明这些标度在存在任意控制与辅助系统时仍最优。

Conclusion: 结果将空间场探测中的三类资源明确分离：经典运动、空间相干性与纠缠，各自贡献不同的时间标度层级，且上述标度在任意控制下均为最优。

Abstract: We investigate whether controlled motion of a quantum sensor offers a metrological advantage in a field-detection task. We consider a localized field of known strength $B$ occupying an unknown contiguous region making up a fraction $1/N$ of the total search space, with a qubit sensor controlled for a total time $T$ to maximize the probability of detecting the field. We study the regime in which the characteristic timescale of the field is slow compared to the time to move the sensor between any two regions, and we distinguish classical motion, where the sensor follows a definite trajectory, from quantum motion, where the sensor can be in a coherent superposition of locations. For a single classically moving sensor, we derive an optimal multi-stop protocol and obtain the exact maximum detection probability. Allowing coherent spatial superposition maps the single-sensor problem onto Hamiltonian search and reduces the interrogation time for successfully detecting the field from $Θ(N/B)$ to $Θ(\sqrt{N}/B)$. We then extend the problem to $M$ sensors and identify a hierarchy of optimal time scalings for constant success probability determined by the available spatial and inter-sensor quantum resources. Unentangled coherently moving sensors achieve $T=Θ(\sqrt{N/M}/B)$ by searching disjoint regions in parallel, while entanglement among the sensors can further enhance the effective field coupling and achieve $T=Θ(\sqrt{N}/(MB))$. Matching lower bounds establish the optimality of these scalings in the presence of arbitrary controls and ancilla systems. Our results separate the roles of classical motion, spatial coherence, and entanglement in spatial field detection.

</details>


### [177] [Error-Corrected QRAM Queries with One-Fault-Tolerant \texttt{CSWAP} Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17185)
*Shifan Xu,Connor T. Hann,Nathan Wiebe,Steven M. Girvin,Yongshan Ding*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种针对 bucket-brigade QRAM 的低开销容错保护框架，结合擦除噪声下的容错受控-SWAP门与基于路由约束的局域奇偶校验码，在偏置与擦除主导噪声下提升 QRAM 可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QRAM 需要 O(N) 个量子比特来实现 N=2^n 个地址的相干查询，使其对噪声极为脆弱；尽管 bucket-brigade 路由本身具有一定内在抗噪性，但进一步提升 QRAM 查询保真度仍是重要且具挑战性的问题。

Method: 框架包含两个互补组件：(1) 在擦除噪声信道下，基于 [[4,2,2]] 码构造单故障容错的 repeat-until-success 逻辑受控-SWAP 门，通过插入擦除检测与局域恢复纠正每次单擦除，使主导逻辑失败概率在物理擦除率上呈二阶；(2) 利用 bucket-brigade 路由的有效性约束直接在路由态上定义局域奇偶校验，不引入额外数据量子比特，在比特翻转噪声下用最小权重完美匹配并在无噪声校验测量下纠正单个内部故障。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟验证了受保护受控-SWAP 的二次抑制效果，以及解码后 QRAM 路由态快照的深度扩展性改善；两个码可级联实现，内码提供擦除保护，外码可选地纠正内部逻辑比特翻转。

Conclusion: 路由约束与受保护的非-Clifford 路由门相结合，可在偏置及擦除主导噪声下提升 QRAM 的可靠性。

Abstract: Quantum random-access memory (QRAM) enables coherent queries to classical data. However, the \(O(N)\) qubits required for a memory with \(N=2^n\) addresses make QRAM appear vulnerable to noise. Though bucket-brigade routing provides intrinsic noise resilience to reduce the error in QRAM, improving the fidelity of QRAM queries is still an important and challenging problem. We introduce a low-overhead protection framework for bucket-brigade QRAM with two complementary components. First, we propose a one-fault-tolerant repeat-until-success logical controlled-SWAP gate using the \([[4,2,2]]\) code under erasure noise channel. Interleaved erasure checks and local recovery correct every single erasure, so the leading logical failure probability is second order in the physical erasure error rates. Second, we exploit validity constraints of bucket-brigade routing to define local parity checks directly on the router states, without adding extra data qubits to create code redundancy. Under bit-flip noise, minimum-weight perfect matching with noiseless syndrome measurements corrects single interior faults, while open spatial boundaries retain a first-order contribution in the residual bit-flip rate. Monte Carlo simulations confirm quadratic suppression for the protected controlled-SWAP and improved depth scaling for decoded QRAM router-state snapshots. The two codes admit a concatenated realization in which the inner code supplies erasure protection and the outer code optionally corrects interior logical bit flips. Together, these results show how routing constraints and a protected non-Clifford routing gate can improve QRAM reliability under biased and erasure-dominant noise.

</details>


### [178] [Fermionic non-Gaussianity via computable measures and experimentally accessible bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17219)
*Leela Ganesh Chandra Lakkaraju,Naga Dileep Varikuti,Philipp Hauke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入一种费米子非高斯性的几何度量——目标态到费米高斯态流形的保真距离，并构造迭代数值方案，使其在强耦合下同样适用；同时证明一个更精细的算符Jensen不等式，据此导出仅涉及两点和四点费米关联函数的见证量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用费米子的非平凡关联与计算能力均源于其对高斯性的偏离，但几何化地量化这种偏离面临两大挑战：费米高斯态流形非凸，且微扰方法仅适用于弱相互作用。因此需要一种可推广至强耦合的非高斯性度量。

Method: 定义目标态到费米高斯态流形的保真距离作为非高斯性几何度量；构造迭代形式以数值求解该距离；证明一个更精细的算符Jensen不等式，并利用它推导出仅含两点和四点费米关联函数的非高斯性见证量；在一维费米-哈伯德模型上进行数值基准测试。

Result: 数值基准表明，见证量的质量随系统尺寸增大而提升，且在强耦合情形下比反平坦性（antiflatness）更紧致。

Conclusion: 该工作为刻画强相互作用费米子量子模拟器的非高斯性提供了一条可扩展路径。

Abstract: Interacting fermions owe both their non-trivial correlations and their computational power to their departure from Gaussianity. Yet, quantifying that departure geometrically faces two challenges: the Gaussian manifold is non-convex, and perturbative treatments are confined to weak interactions. Here, we introduce a geometric measure of fermionic non-Gaussianity, the fidelity distance of a target state to the manifold of fermionic Gaussian states. We construct an iteration formalism to numerically access it, applicable also at strong coupling. Further, we prove a sharpened operator Jensen inequality, of interest in its own right. We use it to derive a witness that involves only two- and four-point fermionic correlators. Numerical benchmarks on the one-dimensional Fermi--Hubbard model show that the quality of the witness improves with system size and, for strong coupling, is tighter than antiflatness. Our results open a scalable route to charting non-Gaussianity of strongly-interacting fermionic quantum simulators.

</details>


### [179] [Quantum Reference Frames in terms of Tetrads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17225)
*Jeremy Butterfield,Daniel A. Turolla Vanzella*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将相对论中"参考系即四元标架(tetrad)"的思想推广到量子理论，提出量子参考系的状态应是对一组四元标架赋予复振幅，并用时空切空间基底的丛论框架来刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论中参考系被成熟地处理为四元标架（四个正交归一矢量），它编码了类时方向、三个正交空间方向与单位，从而描述一个理想化无穷小"尺与钟"装置的时空位形。作者希望把这一直观推广到量子理论，以刻画量子参考系的态。

Method: 以时空切空间基底的丛（bundle）为基本数学结构。核心方案是：量子参考系的态是对一组四元标架指定复振幅。通过对丛的选择区分两种情形：(i) 给定时空几何时，使用正交归一标架丛（orthonormal frame bundle）；(ii) 不预设几何的时空流形上，使用通常意义下包含所有时空点切空间所有基底的标架丛（frame bundle）。

Result: 建立起一个统一的丛论表述：量子参考系即切空间基底丛上的复振幅赋值；并明确区分了"对单一几何的多视角叠加"与"对几何叠加的多视角叠加"两种情况，分别对应正交归一标架丛与一般标架丛。

Conclusion: 本质文为两篇同伴论文的概念性导引，侧重阐述提案的主要概念，并解释两篇同伴论文之间的关键对比：(i) 单一时空几何下，量子参考系给出对该几何的视角叠加；(ii) 无先验几何的时空流形上，量子参考系可描述对几何叠加的多视角叠加，而非仅是对单一几何的多视角叠加。

Abstract: We adapt to the context of quantum theory the treatment, familiar in relativity theory, of a reference frame as a tetrad. Since a tetrad is a set of four orthonormal vectors at a spacetime point, it encodes a timelike direction, three orthogonal spatial directions, and units; and so it gives a configuration in spacetime (a spatial orientation and state of motion) of an idealized infinitesimal apparatus (``rulers and a clock"). So a natural ``first guess" is that the state of a quantum reference frame should be an assignment of complex amplitudes to a set of tetrads. We develop this idea, in terms of bundles over spacetime of bases of the tangent spaces.
  This paper is a companion to two other papers. It emphasises the main conceptual aspects of our proposal, while the other two papers give physical details. It also explains the main contrast between those two papers. Namely: (i) the first considers a single spacetime geometry, so that a quantum reference frame, on our proposal, gives a superposition of perspectives on that geometry; while (ii) the second considers the spacetime manifold with no geometry given a priori, which will imply that a quantum reference frame, on our proposal, can describe a superposition of perspectives on a superposition of geometries---not ``just" a superposition of perspectives on a single geometry. The main mathematical idea for representing this contrast will be choosing between (i) the orthonormal frame bundle, for a given spacetime geometry; (ii) the frame bundle in the usual sense of containing all bases of all spacetime points' tangent spaces.

</details>


### [180] [Whither quantum advantage? New classical lower bounds for Max-Cut on large-girth 3-regular graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17236)
*Flaviano Morone,Shivang Rawat,Dries Sels*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 对3-正则图的最大割比例下界M_g，本文用VIRAL局部算法在深度p（偶数，p≤32）树上求值，得到优于QAOA的下界，并把渐近下界从0.912提升到0.9127。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大围长3-正则图的最大割比例极限M_g的严格界长期停留在0.912≤lim M_g≤0.92386。局部算法给出下界：围长g≥2p+2时，深度p局部算法在每条边周围看到相同的树，其在树上的期望割比例即为M_g的下界。近期QAOA已改进该下界，作者希望用经典局部算法进一步收紧下界并扩大覆盖的围长范围。

Method: 采用Variational Iterative Rotation Algorithm（VIRAL）这一经典局部算法，在深度p的树上计算期望割比例，对所有偶数深度直至p=32进行求值，从而获得对应围长g=2p+2的M_g下界（最高到g=66）。

Result: 所得下界在每个p≥4时均超过近期QAOA的结果，高出0.005至0.011；覆盖围长范围从g=36扩展到g=66。在g=66处得到M_66≥0.9127，置信水平为99.99999%，从而把渐近下界从0.912提升至0.9127。

Conclusion: 经典局部算法VIRAL在深树上的求值可为3-正则图最大割比例提供强于量子近似优化算法的下界，并显著扩展有效围长范围，将渐近下界由0.912提高到0.9127，缩小了与上界0.92386之间的差距。

Abstract: Every 3-regular graph of girth at least $g$ has a cut containing at least a fraction $M_g$ of its edges, and the best rigorous bounds at large girth are $0.912\le \lim_{g\to\infty}M_g \le 0.92386$. Lower bounds on $M_g$ follow from the performance of local algorithms: on a graph of girth $g \ge 2p+2$ a depth-$p$ local algorithm sees the same tree around every edge, so its expected cut fraction on that tree bounds $M_g$ from below. We apply this strategy to the Variational Iterative Rotation Algorithm (VIRAL), a classical local algorithm, and evaluate it on the depth-$p$ tree for every even depth up to $p=32$. The resulting bounds exceed those recently obtained from the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) for every $p\ge 4$, by $0.005$ to $0.011$, and they extend the range of girths covered from $g=36$ to $g=66$. Notably, at $g=66$ we obtain $M_{66} \ge 0.9127$ at $99.99999\%$ confidence level, which raises the asymptotic lower bound from $0.912$ to $0.9127$.

</details>


### [181] [Quantum Cryptography and Autoformalization in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17286)
*Maor Ben Shahar,Jonathan Morag,Dirk Englund,Kfir Sulimany*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Lean证明助手中构建了基于纠缠的qubit BB84协议端到端安全性定理，仅依赖标准公理消除隐藏假设，覆盖相干攻击、后选择技术与有限码长下的可组合安全性，并借助LLM自动形式化管线进一步降低了密钥率的有限码长惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子密钥分发依赖物理与数学推理，但现有安全性证明常因将各自成立的定理在假设不匹配的情况下拼接而产生隐藏漏洞。作者希望消除这些隐藏假设，为量子密码学的安全性论证建立可复现的形式化标准。

Method: 使用Lean证明助手对基于纠缠的qubit BB84协议进行端到端安全性形式化，仅基于Lean标准公理，避免引入隐藏假设；采用后选择技术（postselection technique）证明其对抗相干攻击的可组合安全性，并考虑有限块长度；证明语言强制体现局域操作与经典通信（LOCC）以保证协议表示的忠实性；整个证明由基于LLM的自动形式化驱动完成。

Result: 完成了覆盖有限码长、对抗相干攻击的qubit BB84可组合安全性端到端形式化定理；在证明完成后，将同一LLM自动形式化管线用作"发现机器"，成功降低了密钥率中的有限码长惩罚。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子密码学的安全性论证树立了可复现的形式化标准，并展示了形式化证明引擎能够加速量子信息科学中的新发现。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution provides secure communication by relying on physics principles and mathematical reasoning. Security proofs across the field can contain gaps, often between individually valid theorems integrated under mismatched assumptions. We produce an end-to-end security theorem formalized in the Lean proof assistant, eliminating hidden hypotheses by building only on Lean's standard axioms. Our proof covers composable security of the entanglement-based qubit BB84 protocol against coherent attacks by the postselection technique, accounting for finite block size, and is written in a language that enforces local operations and classical communication to help ensure a faithful representation of the protocol. The proof itself was enabled by LLM-based autoformalization, and with the proof complete, we used the same pipeline as a discovery machine, reducing the finite-size penalty on the key rate. Our work sets a reproducible standard for security arguments in quantum cryptography and shows how formal proof engines can accelerate discovery across quantum information science.

</details>


### [182] [Optical interferometric tools for probing memory effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17322)
*Saheli Mukherjee,Bivas Mallick,Samyadeb Bhattacharya,Ananda G. Maity,A. S. Majumdar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出干涉测量框架（相移与可见度）用于探测环境效应并见证非马尔可夫动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非马尔可夫记忆效应在量子信息处理、计量学与热力学引擎中广泛相关，因此检测非马尔可夫记忆日益重要。作者希望提供实验可行、简单易实现的探测手段。

Method: 利用干涉测量方法，重点分析相移与干涉可见度；构造合适的见证算符，通过干涉图案中的可测相移检测非马尔可夫性；并证明在非相干CP可分（马尔可夫）动力学下，干涉可见度单调不增。

Result: 见证算符可通过可测相移检测非马尔可夫性；优化可见度的任何回升都是信息回流因而非马尔可夫性的充分标志；并在代表性非马尔可夫动力学例子上验证了基于相位与可见度方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为探测记忆效应提供了简单且实验可行的框架，潜在应用于量子传感与含噪中等规模量子器件。

Abstract: Recent efforts to detect non-Markovian memory have become increasingly important, motivated by the broad relevance of memory effects in quantum information processing, metrology, and thermodynamic engines. Here, we show that interferometric methods, particularly the phase shift and visibility, provide an experimentally accessible framework for probing environmental effects and witnessing non-Markovian dynamics. Specifically, suitably constructed witness operators enable the detection of non-Markovianity through measurable phase shifts in the interference pattern. Furthermore, we prove that the interferometric visibility is monotonically non-increasing under incoherent CP-divisible (Markovian) dynamics. Consequently, any revival of the optimized visibility constitutes a sufficient signature of information backflow and hence non-Markovianity. The effectiveness of the proposed phase-based and visibility approaches is illustrated for representative examples of non-Markovian dynamics. Our results offer a simple and experimentally feasible framework for probing memory effects, with potential applications in quantum sensing and noisy intermediate-scale quantum devices.

</details>


### [183] [Krylov Complexity and Quantum Many-body Scars in Non-Hermitian Kinetically Constrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17328)
*Arkaprava Sil,Rudraksh Kaushik,Souvik Kundu,Prasant Mallik,Sudipto Singha Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非厄米PXP与PPXPP模型的Krylov复杂度、本征态结构与动力学，发现左、右本征态系综在非厄米性增强时表现出定性不同的行为，需分别处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学约束可阻碍封闭量子系统热化并导致非遍历行为，但已有研究多集中于厄米动力学约束模型，非厄米情形探讨不足；且非厄米系统中左、右本征态系综不等价，二者是否具有不同的动力学与本征态性质尚不清楚。

Method: 对典型PXP和PPXPP模型进行非厄米推广，利用Arnoldi算法分别计算左、右本征态系综中的Krylov复杂度、Arnoldi系数分布及本征态结构（纠缠、与真空态重叠）。

Result: 随非厄米性增强：右系综Krylov复杂度呈周期持续增长的振荡，左系综则逐渐饱和；Arnoldi系数的拱形轮廓在两系综中变化不同，左系综在大非厄米性下重新出现类厄米拱形；非厄米PXP模型中右系综保持疤痕结构，左系综出现额外低纠缠、与真空态大重叠的非疤痕本征态。

Conclusion: 非厄米性对左、右系综的Krylov动力学与本征态结构影响不同，刻画非厄米动力学约束系统时必须对左、右本征态系综分别处理。

Abstract: Thermalization in closed quantum systems and its breakdown are central to understanding the emergence of statistical behavior in isolated many-body systems. Kinetic constraints can hinder thermalization by restricting the allowed dynamics and giving rise to non-ergodic behavior. While various aspects of ergodicity breaking in kinetically constrained models have been extensively studied in Hermitian systems, their behavior in non-Hermitian kinetically constrained models remains less explored. In particular, the inequivalence of left and right eigenstate ensembles in non-Hermitian systems raises the question of whether they exhibit qualitatively different dynamical and eigenstate properties. In this work, we study non-Hermitian generalizations of the paradigmatic PXP and PPXPP models, focusing on their Krylov complexity, eigenstate structure, and dynamics. By exploiting the Arnoldi's algorithm, we find distinct behavior of Krylov complexity in the left and right eigenstate ensembles as non-Hermiticity is increased: in the right ensemble, it exhibits persistent oscillations with an increasing period, while in the left ensemble, it gradually saturates. The characteristic arch-shaped profile of the Arnoldi coefficients is also modified differently in the two ensembles, with a Hermitian-like arch re-emerging in the left ensemble at large non Hermiticity. Additionally, in the non-Hermitian PXP model, the right ensemble retains the characteristic scar structure, while the left ensemble develops additional non-scar eigenstates with anomalously low entanglement and large overlap with the vacuum state. These findings reveal that non-Hermiticity can affect Krylov dynamics and eigenstate structure differently in the left and right ensembles, demonstrating the need to treat them separately when characterizing non-Hermitian kinetically constrained systems.

</details>


### [184] [Generating Superposition of Transverse Ising Ground States by Quantum Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17334)
*Koushik Das,Sayan Mondal,Ujjwal Sen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过将最近邻耦合强度提升为作用于中心辅助自旋的算符，并利用绝热演化、中心自旋测量及后选择，制备了横场量子Ising基态的叠加态，涵盖顺磁、铁磁与反铁磁三相；解析给出了磁化强度、自旋关联、纠缠及量子Fisher信息，发现纠缠在横场与Ising相互作用竞争处最大，量子Fisher信息在临界点附近出现多峰并呈Heisenberg标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何借助量子相互作用（而非经典混合）生成量子Ising基态的相干叠加，并刻画这类跨相叠加态的磁学、纠缠及计量学特性。

Method: 将最近邻耦合强度推广为作用于中心辅助自旋的算符；中心自旋置于叠加态，使自旋链在不同有效相互作用强度下相干演化；对中心自旋与多体自旋链的联合系统进行绝热演化，随后测量中心自旋并对特定结果后选择。对自旋1/2与自旋1两种中心系统分别构造两相和三相基态叠加，并解析推导磁化强度、自旋关联、纠缠及量子Fisher信息。

Result: 可制备属于顺磁、铁磁、反铁磁三相的横场量子Ising基态叠加：自旋1/2中心系统可实现任意两相叠加，自旋1中心系统可实现三相叠加。磁化强度与自旋关联在不同叠加扇区表现出显著差异；最近邻纠缠在横场与Ising相互作用竞争处最大，且其行为与磁学观测量不同。量子Fisher信息在组成基态的临界点附近呈现多峰，在所考察参数与系统尺寸下最大值呈Heisenberg标度。

Conclusion: 该方案提供了一种通过中心自旋介导的量子相互作用制备多相基态相干叠加的途径，并揭示了此类叠加态在磁学观测量、纠缠与量子计量方面的独特特征，其中量子Fisher信息的Heisenberg标度表明其在估计Ising相互作用强度方面具有优势。

Abstract: We investigate superpositions of quantum Ising ground states generated through a quantum interaction, in which the nearest-neighbor coupling strength is promoted to an operator acting on a central auxiliary spin. We prepare the central spin in a superposition, which allows the chain to evolve coherently under different effective interaction strengths. The states are prepared by adiabatic evolution of the joint system comprising the central spin and the many-body spin chain, followed by a measurement on the central spin and post-selection of a specific outcome. It is possible to prepare superpositions of transverse-field quantum Ising ground states belonging to the three magnetic phases, namely, the paramagnetic, ferromagnetic, and anti-ferromagnetic phases. For a spin-1/2 central system, it is possible to prepare superpositions of ground states belonging to any two phases, while for the spin-1 case, the superpositions may contain three ground states, each belonging to one of the three phases. We analytically derive expressions for the magnetization, spin correlations, and entanglement of such states. The magnetization and spin correlations show distinct features in different superposition sectors. The nearest-neighbor entanglement is largest where the transverse field and Ising interaction compete, and its behavior differs from that of the magnetic observables. We also derive the quantum Fisher information relevant to estimating the Ising interaction strength. And it exhibits multiple peaks near the critical points of the constituent ground states, with the maximum value showing Heisenberg scaling, for the parameters and system sizes examined.

</details>


### [185] [Periodic-Pulse Control of Collective Emission and Photon Statistics in Interacting Quantum Emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17367)
*Kavalambramalil George Paulson,Fu Li,Hanna Terletska*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究周期性脉冲驱动下两个偶极-偶极耦合发射体的集体发射光谱与光子关联，发现其光谱在主导特征上可高度复现连续波激发结果，而光子关联主要由脉冲间隔决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究周期性脉冲驱动是否能替代连续波激发来调控耦合双发射体系统的集体发射光谱与光子统计，并厘清脉冲参数（脉冲间隔、脉冲宽度、拉比频率）与共振条件、偶极耦合之间的相互作用机制。

Method: 理论计算两个全同偶极-偶极耦合发射体在连续波驱动与周期性脉冲驱动下的集体发射光谱及光子关联函数 g^(2)(t,τ)，区分理想瞬时 π_x 脉冲与有限宽度、高拉比频率 π_x 脉冲两种情形，并分析脉冲间隔 τ_D 的影响。

Result: 周期性瞬时 π_x 脉冲序列产生的发射光谱主导特征与连续波激发高度相似；光谱结构由共振条件、偶极-偶极耦合与脉冲间隔 τ_D 共同决定。有限宽度 π_x 脉冲在拉比频率足够高时可逼近理想瞬时脉冲极限。光子关联 g^(2)(t,τ) 主要受 τ_D 控制，且在短脉冲极限下对共振条件愈发不敏感，与连续波驱动情形不同。

Conclusion: 周期性脉冲驱动是一种灵活手段，可同时剪裁耦合双发射体系统的发射光谱与光子统计特性。

Abstract: We investigate the collective emission spectra and photon correlations of two identical dipole-dipole-coupled emitters under continuous-wave (CW) and periodic pulsed driving. Our results show that a periodic train of instantaneous $π_x$ pulses can generate emission spectra whose dominant features closely resemble those obtained under CW excitation. We find that the structure of the collective emission spectra reflects the interplay among the resonance conditions, dipole-dipole coupling, and the inter-pulse delay $τ_D$. We further demonstrate that finite-duration $π_x$ pulses with sufficiently high Rabi frequencies can closely reproduce the ideal instantaneous-pulse limit. In contrast to the emission spectra, the photon correlations $g^{(2)}(t,τ)$ are governed primarily by $τ_D$ and, unlike under CW driving, become increasingly insensitive to the resonance conditions in the short-pulse limit. These results establish periodic pulsed driving as a versatile means of tailoring both the emission spectra and the photon statistics of coupled two-emitter systems.

</details>


### [186] [A Controlled Null Result for Per-Gate Riemannian Optimization in VQE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17395)
*Polina Belozerova,Pavel Osinenko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在氢链VQE基准上，逐门U(2)黎曼优化相比欧拉角坐标Adam基线无可测实用优势，属受控零结果，并给出四类易致假阳性的失效模式与验证清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQE的可参数化门天然位于酉群乘积流形上，黎曼优化看似是提升训练的合适方案；本文旨在以干净的逐门对照实验检验该猜想是否真能带来实用收益。

Method: 在逐门设置下，每个单量子比特门直接在U(2)上优化，并与在无约束欧拉角坐标图上运行的Adam对比；两者从相同酉初始化，并各自独立进行学习率扫描。在验证过的氢链基准上比较最终误差、成功率与迭代代价，同时改变黎曼度量、回缩（retraction）与动量向量传输等要素做敏感性检验。

Result: 最终误差与成功率上两种方法相互交错，无一致优劣；成功单元格上经典坐标基线迭代数并不更慢；黎曼度量、回缩与动量传输的选择均不改变结论。

Conclusion: 得出受控零结果：该设定下黎曼机制无实用优势；并识别四种可制造假阳性结果的失效模式——平凡乘积态基准、丢弃大部分步长的投影矩阵Adam基线、非酉继承Cayley回缩使能量低于变分下界、学习率最优落在网格边界。据此提出VQE优化器比较的验证清单。

Abstract: Parametrized gates in a variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) live on a product of unitary groups, which makes Riemannian optimization a natural candidate for improving training. We test this candidate in the clean per-gate setting: each single-qubit gate is optimized directly on U (2) and compared with Adam on an unconstrained Euler-angle chart representing the same physical gate, initialized from the same unitaries and tuned with a separate learning-rate sweep. On a validated hydrogen-chain benchmark, we find no measurable practical advantage from the Riemannian machinery. The direct manifold optimizer and the Euler-coordinate baseline are interleaved in final error and success rate; the classical coordinate baseline is not slower in iterations on the successful cells; and the choices of Riemannian metric, retraction, and momentum vector transport do not change the conclusion. We also identify four failure modes that can manufacture an apparent positive result: a trivial product-state benchmark, a projected-matrix Adam baseline that discards most of its own step, a non-unitary inherited Cayley retraction that lets the reported energy pass below the variational minimum, and a learning-rate grid whose optimum sits at its boundary. Each failure mode produced a plausible multi-seed result in our own workflow before being caught. The practical output of this paper is therefore a controlled null result and a validation checklist for VQE optimizer comparisons.

</details>


### [187] [Distributed quantum phase sensing using an SU(1,1) Perelomov coherent state in a Mach-Zehnder interferometer network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17447)
*Seloua Boulifa,Abdallah Slaoui,Hanane EL Hadfi,Rachid Ahl Laamara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了基于N个马赫-曾德尔干涉仪可重构网络的分布式量子传感，通过注入SU(1,1)群的Perelomov相干态，实现对相对相移加权线性组合的同时估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子传感需要在高精度多参数估计中超越散粒噪声极限，并更高效地利用量子资源。作者希望探索纠缠策略相对于可分离方案的性能优势，并为干涉架构优化提供指导。

Method: 采用多参数量子估计理论，构建N个马赫-曾德尔干涉仪的可重构网络；将一个端口注入由SU(1,1)群表示生成的Perelomov相干态，其余模式初始化为真空态，从而在传感器之间建立量子关联；分析最优透射系数与首分束器不对称性的作用。

Result: 该纠缠策略优于标准散粒噪声极限，并渐近达到海森堡极限；首分束器不对称性至关重要，最优透射系数对应最大不平衡；在相同总平均光子数下，相比可分离方案，精度增益介于1到N之间。

Conclusion: 纠缠分布式量子传感网络在实现高精度测量方面具有潜力，纠缠策略系统性优于可分离方案，并为量子计量中的干涉架构优化提供了明确准则。

Abstract: We present a theoretical study of distributed quantum sensing using a reconfigurable network of $N$ Mach--Zehnder interferometers to simultaneously estimate weighted linear combinations of relative phase shifts. The proposed scheme relies on injecting a Perelomov coherent state, generated from the SU(1,1) group representation, into one port of a passive quantum circuit while the remaining modes are initialized in the vacuum state, thereby establishing non-trivial quantum correlations among the sensors. Based on multi-parameter quantum estimation theory, our analysis shows that this entangled strategy outperforms the standard shot-noise limit and asymptotically achieves the Heisenberg limit. Furthermore, we highlight the crucial role of the first beam splitter's asymmetry; specifically, the optimal transmission coefficient that maximizes sensitivity corresponds to its maximum imbalance. Compared to a separable approach utilizing the same total average photon number, the entangled strategy yields a more efficient use of quantum resources, guaranteeing an accuracy gain between $1$ and $N$. This demonstrates that the entangled strategy is systematically superior to its separable counterpart. Ultimately, these results illustrate the potential of entangled distributed quantum sensor networks for high-precision measurements and provide clear guidelines for optimizing interferometric architectures in quantum metrology.

</details>


### [188] [A three-eighths upper bound on the stabilizer-rank exponent of face-center magic states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17500)
*Samuel Bevins*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文显式构造了量子比特面心魔态（face-center magic state）的 m 份拷贝的精确稳定子秩（stabilizer rank）上界 $O(2^{3m/8})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进此前发表的面心魔态稳定子秩指数 $\log_2(3)/4 \approx 0.396$，给出更紧的指数 $3/8 = 0.375$，从而降低基于稳定子秩的魔态开销估计。

Method: 利用两个对称相关的十五项恒等式，在十二份拷贝处合并为一个二十四项分解；再借助该分解的平衡结构构造替换规则，使得每增加八份拷贝、每个现有项的代价仅增加八项。

Result: 得到指数上界 $γ_T \leq 3/8$，即 m 份拷贝的精确稳定子秩为 $O(2^{3m/8})$，优于已知的 $\log_2(3)/4$。

Conclusion: 该显式分解将面心魔态稳定子秩的渐近指数从 $\log_2(3)/4$ 降至 $3/8$，为魔态蒸馏与容错量子计算的开销分析提供了更优的构造。

Abstract: We give an explicit construction proving that the exact stabilizer rank of $m$ copies of the qubit face-center magic state is $O(2^{3m/8})$. Two symmetry-related fifteen-term identities combine into a twenty-four-term decomposition at twelve copies. The balanced structure of this decomposition yields a substitution that adds eight copies at a cost of eight terms per existing term. The resulting bound $γ_T \leq 3/8$ improves the previously published exponent $\log_2(3)/4$.

</details>


### [189] [Chromatic Block-Gibbs p-Bit Decoding of QLDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17520)
*Mert Gökduman,Cyril Guyot,Henry D. Pfister*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于概率比特(p-bit)的量子LDPC码并行译码器，通过局部Gibbs条件随机更新并结合逐步增强的奇偶校验惩罚，在码容量与电路级噪声下达到与主流译码器相当的逻辑错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为QLDPC码译码探索BP类方法之外的随机化替代方案，并推动面向硬件原生的译码实现。

Method: 以可调随机二值单元(p-bit)为核心，利用局部场控制：将预测的错误构型（作为syndrome的解释）按局部Gibbs条件随机更新，同时逐步加强奇偶校验惩罚，驱使搜索收敛到syndrome有效的构型；通过对译码图着色实现并行块更新，并借助独立副本与重启提供轨迹多样性；最终返回搜索过程中遇到的最优候选（类似集成译码器的候选选择策略）。

Result: 在给定更新预算下，码容量噪声与电路级噪声两种场景的逻辑错误率均与领先译码器相当；但其总变量重采样次数可能显著多于基于BP方法的messages更新次数。

Conclusion: 约束退火式p-bit动力学可作为一种有竞争力的随机QLDPC译码方案，并值得进一步研究硬件原生实现。

Abstract: We introduce a parallel decoder for quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes based on probabilistic bits (p-bits), tunable stochastic binary units controlled by local fields. The idea is that predicted error configurations, interpreted as explanations of the observed syndrome, are updated stochastically using local Gibbs conditionals, while a progressively strengthened parity penalty drives the search toward syndrome-valid configurations. A coloring of the decoding graph enables parallel block updates, and independent replicas and restarts provide trajectory diversity. The best candidate encountered during the search is returned, mirroring the candidate-selection strategy of recent ensemble decoders. At the stated update budgets, the resulting logical error rates are on par with leading decoders under both code-capacity and circuit-level noise. While the total number of variable resamplings can be substantially larger than the message-update count of BP-based alternatives, the numerical results establish constraint-annealed p-bit dynamics as a competitive stochastic alternative for QLDPC decoding and motivate further study of hardware-native implementations.

</details>


### [190] [Coherent optical control of a thousand qubits in a three-dimensional trapped-ion crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17583)
*Yue Li,Yuxiang Huang,Yi Li,Xu Cheng,Zihan Xie,Qingyuan Mei,Chang-Ling Zou,Ya Wang,Xinhua Peng,Yiheng Lin,Jiangfeng Du*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在含1200余个离子的三维库仑晶体中，将约3600个集体振动模式冷却至单声子占据以下，实现光学量子比特的全局初始化、相干操控与集体探测，获得21.6 ms系综光学相干时间，并把光学跃迁频率不确定度由单离子的389 Hz降至千余离子的12 Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流量子平台多局限于一到二维，而离子三维库仑晶体可自发组装成无需重排的密集量子比特阵列；但此类晶体的光学跃迁会耦合到近乎全部3N个集体模式，形成热占据的准连续声子浴，使相干光学操控难以实现。

Method: 采用横跨振动谱五个倍频程以上（约50 kHz至2.1 MHz）的连续冷却带，将1200余个离子晶体中的约3600个模式冷却至单声子占据以下，并以此为光学量子比特寄存器，实现全局初始化、相干操控和集体探测。

Result: 实现21.6 ms的系综光学相干时间；光学跃迁频率不确定度从单离子的389 Hz降至千余离子的12 Hz，接近预期的投影噪声标度。

Conclusion: 该方法可扩展至更大晶体并引入声子介导的多体耦合，为纠缠增强精密测量与三维量子模拟开辟路径，尤其可结合空间整形照明实现区域分辨的集体控制。

Abstract: Leading quantum platforms are largely limited to one or two dimensions, whereas three-dimensional Coulomb crystals of trapped ions self-assemble into dense qubit arrays with no rearrangement overhead. Coherent optical control of such crystals has nonetheless been out of reach, because the optical transition couples to nearly all $3N$ collective modes, i.e., a thermally occupied quasi-continuum bath. Here we cool about 3,600 modes of a crystal of over 1,200 ions below single-phonon occupation, using a continuous cooling band that spans more than five octaves in vibrational spectrum, from about 50 kHz to 2.1 MHz. The crystal then serves as a register of optical qubits, allowing global initialization, coherent manipulation and collective detection. We demonstrate ensemble optical coherence of 21.6 milliseconds and reduce the optical-transition frequency uncertainty from 389 Hz for a single ion to 12 Hz for over a thousand ions, approaching the expected projection-noise scaling. Our approach could be extended to even larger crystals with phonon-mediated many-body couplings, opening a route towards entanglement-enhanced precision metrology and three-dimensional quantum simulation, particularly with spatial-shaped illumination for region-resolved collective control.

</details>


### [191] [Universal entanglement detection with geometrically optimal witnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17592)
*Xuanran Zhu,Keyi Huang,Chao Zhang,Xinfang Nie,Qi Zhao,Dawei Lu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，用于构造可识别任意纠缠结构的几何最优纠缠见证，实现了二体、多体及高维纠缠的普适探测，并在四量子比特核自旋系统中验证了实验可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为任意量子系统构造最优纠缠见证十分困难，主要障碍在于高维量子态空间的结构复杂性。现有方法在噪声鲁棒性、探测普适性和构造效率方面存在不足。

Method: 利用目标量子态相对于特定纠缠结构在Hilbert-Schmidt空间中的几何性质，构造几何最优见证；在所有非平凡见证中，使目标态与不具备目标纠缠结构的态集合之间具有最大可区分度。

Result: 实现了二体、多体及高维纠缠的普适探测；在四量子比特核自旋量子系统上验证了实验可行性，相比现有方法具有更强的噪声鲁棒性、更广的探测普适性和更高的构造效率。

Conclusion: 该框架为量子系统中纠缠的表征与理解提供了强大而通用的工具，有助于推动纠缠检测的发展。

Abstract: Constructing optimal entanglement witnesses for arbitrary quantum systems remains a formidable challenge, hindered by the structural complexity of high-dimensional quantum state spaces. In this work, we overcome this barrier by introducing a unified framework for constructing geometrically optimal witnesses capable of identifying arbitrary entanglement structures. By leveraging the geometric properties of a target quantum state relative to a specific entanglement structure in Hilbert-Schmidt space, these witnesses are constructed in an efficient and effective manner. Among all nontrivial witnesses, they provide maximal distinguishability between the target state and the set of states without the desired entanglement structure. As a result, our approach enables the universal detection of bipartite, multipartite, and high-dimensional entanglement. To demonstrate its experimental feasibility, we apply the framework to a four-qubit nuclear spin quantum system. Compared to existing methods, our approach offers enhanced noise robustness, broader detection universality, and improved construction efficiency, providing a powerful and versatile tool for advancing the characterization and understanding of entanglement in quantum systems.

</details>


### [192] [Fast charging of quantum batteries assisted by nonlinear couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17599)
*Y. -J. Shen,Y. -C. Shen,C. -H. Chu,H. H. Jen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论证明非线性充电器-电池相互作用可显著加速量子电池充电，在弱驱动、强驱动及无外驱条件下均能缩短充电时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 充电器介导的量子电池在量子层面实现高效能量转移与存储具有潜在优势，但如何加速其充电过程、突破经典对应物的性能限制仍需探索。

Method: 理论上构建含非线性充电器-电池耦合的量子电池模型，分别在弱驱动、强驱动与最优驱动体制下分析非线性耦合对充电时间和稳态功（ergotropy）的影响，并与线性耦合情形对比。

Result: 弱驱动下，足够强的非线性耦合可超越退相位效应并显著缩短充电时间；在强驱动和最优驱动（使线性耦合稳态功最大的驱动强度）下，非线性耦合产生超越线性体制的新充电时间最优值，并伴随近似临界阻尼动力学；即使无外驱，仅靠非线性耦合也能大幅缩短充电时间。

Conclusion: 非线性充电器-电池耦合是加速量子电池充电的有效资源，拓展了相互作用驱动的量子优势在能量转移与存储中的应用范围。

Abstract: Charger-mediated quantum batteries offer promising opportunities for efficient energy transfer and storage at the quantum level, with potential advantages beyond their classical counterparts. Here, we theoretically demonstrate that nonlinear charger-battery interactions can substantially accelerate the charging of quantum batteries. In the weak-driving regime, sufficiently strong nonlinear coupling can dominate over the effect of dephasing and significantly reduce the charging time. In the strong- and optimal-driving regimes, where the optimal driving strength maximizes the steady-state ergotropy in the linear-coupling regime, nonlinear coupling gives rise to a new optimum in the charging time beyond that of the linear-coupling regime, accompanied by nearly critically damped dynamics. Remarkably, even in the absence of external driving, nonlinear coupling alone can substantially reduce the charging time, providing an alternative route toward fast battery charging. Our results establish nonlinear charger-battery coupling as an effective resource for accelerating quantum-battery charging and broaden the scope of interaction-enabled quantum advantages in energy transfer and storage.

</details>


### [193] [From Syndromes to Computation: A General Framework for Logical Operations in Quantum Error-Correcting Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17612)
*Hunter Nelson,Arshag Danageozian,ChunJun Cao,Sophia E. Economou,Edwin Barnes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出以交换Pauli旋转的稳定子伴随式来组织逻辑门设计的通用框架，给出任意角度下严格保码空间条件，统一描述CSS/非CSS码及Clifford/非Clifford操作，并在BB、Mirror qLDPC及递归超立方等码上构造出显式低权逻辑门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算既需要纠错码保护信息，又需要码上可实现且物理上可行的逻辑门。现有逻辑门设计往往针对特定码类或特定门，缺乏统一的稳定子层面描述，因此需要一个能同时覆盖CSS与非CSS码、Clifford与非Clifford操作、并给出可构造性判据的一般框架。

Method: 将逻辑门设计归结为交换Pauli旋转的稳定子伴随式约束：推导任意旋转角度下精确的保码空间条件；对"等伴随式"特化，把Clifford门设计化为稳定子因子分解，得到距离d码的生成元权重上界⌈d/2⌉，并推广到Clifford层级更高的逻辑旋转。进一步用Clifford代数基做解析构造，把伴随式约束转化为显式代数关系以实现逻辑可寻址。

Result: 在二元轮带（BB）与非CSS Mirror qLDPC码上给出显式低权门：[[16,4,4]] BB码上的寻址T门、[[64,8,8]] BB码上的T型旋转、[[36,6,6]] Mirror码上的CCZ、CS、T型操作；在递归超立方码上解析构造寻址CNOT、SWAP、受控门与CCZ型门；在中段量子Reed-Muller码上独立构造出达到等伴随式权重界的C^r Z门。

Conclusion: 稳定子伴随式可作为连接量子码稳定子结构、其可直接支持的逻辑计算、以及物理实现容错性质之间的构造性桥梁，为逻辑门设计与可寻址性提供统一且系统的途径。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computation requires error-correcting codes that not only protect quantum information but also support useful logical operations with practical physical realizations. Here we develop a general framework in which the stabilizer syndromes of commuting Pauli rotations directly organize logical-gate design. We derive an exact codespace-preservation condition valid at arbitrary rotation angles, providing a common stabilizer-level description for CSS and non-CSS codes and for both Clifford and non-Clifford operations. A particularly useful equal-syndrome specialization reduces Clifford gate design to stabilizer factorization, yields a general generator weight bound of $\lceil d/2\rceil$ for distance-$d$ codes, and systematically extends to logical rotations higher in the Clifford hierarchy. Applied to bivariate-bicycle (BB) and non-CSS Mirror qLDPC codes, the framework gives explicit low-weight Clifford and controlled-phase gates.
