These results show that the impulsive and rotating-wave approximations cannot be assumed to improve simultaneously and should be tested when quantitatively interpreting multidimensional spectra. PULSUS provides a direct frequency-domain framework for carrying out that comparison.

</details>


### [230] [Finite-energy two-way assisted capacities: Exact formulas for multimode loss and fading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18731)
*Stefano Pirandola,Gaetana Spedalieri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文借助局部量子操作与双向经典通信，通过sector teleportation精确求解多模玻色损耗及衰落信道下的量子、纠缠分发与密钥生成容量，涵盖纯损耗、频率相关损耗、恒定与随机衰落等情形，并给出强逆界与能量分配准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模玻色损耗与衰落信道是光量子通信的核心模型，但以往对其双向量子与密钥容量的精确刻画多局限于单模或简化假设，缺乏对多模、频率相关损耗、随机衰落以及衰落是否可观测等实际情形的统一精确分析，也缺乏能量约束下的容量基准与能量分配指导。

Method: 采用sector teleportation技术，在平均光子数约束与功率约束下允许任意自适应量子协议，求解多模纯损耗信道的精确双向量子容量与密钥容量；将其推广至连续频率相关损耗并确定光学模式间的最优光子分配；针对随机变化损耗，区分信道实现于传输前公开与传输后公开两种情形求解有限能量精确容量；对未观测衰落推导容量上界并给出on/off信道的显式公式；同时建立强逆界及其精确阈值。

Result: 获得了多模纯损耗信道在平均光子数约束下的精确双向量子与密钥容量；对连续频率相关损耗得到最优光子分配，并在无限带宽恒定损耗下给出正比于功率平方根的闭式容量；对随机衰落，在公开信道实现下分别给出传输前后已知的精确有限能量容量，对未观测衰落给出容量界及on/off信道显式公式；建立了强逆界及确定性损耗、已观测衰落和有限个透射率下未观测衰落的精确阈值，且这些阈值在平均能量约束下通常超过相应有限能量容量。

Conclusion: 该工作为含多模信号与可变损耗的能量约束光量子通信提供了精确容量刻画与基准，并为能量分配提供了指导。

Abstract: We study quantum communication, entanglement distribution, and secret-key generation through multimode bosonic loss and fading channels, assisted by local quantum operations and two-way classical communication. Using sector teleportation, we determine the exact two-way quantum and secret-key capacities of multimode pure-loss channels under a mean photon-number constraint, allowing arbitrary adaptive quantum protocols. We extend these results to continuous frequency-dependent loss under a power constraint and identify the optimal allocation of photons among optical modes. For constant loss over an unlimited bandwidth, we obtain a closed-form capacity proportional to the square root of power. For randomly varying loss, we determine exact finite-energy capacities when the channel realization is publicly revealed, distinguishing knowledge before and after transmission. When fading remains unobserved, we derive capacity bounds and explicit formulas for an on/off channel. We also establish strong-converse bounds, with exact thresholds for the deterministic loss models considered here, observed fading, and unobserved fading with finitely many possible transmissivities. Under mean energy constraints, these thresholds generally exceed the corresponding finite-energy capacities. These results guide energy allocation and provide benchmarks for energy-constrained optical quantum communication with multimode signals and variable loss.

</details>


### [231] [Quantum-enhanced mechanical sensing with machine-learning estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18776)
*Finn Müßigbrodt,Andraž Omahen,Q. Rumman Rahman,Giulio Corazza,Yiwen Chu,Matteo Fadel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将微克级声学谐振腔的高次谐波体声波模式作为相位不敏感的量子增强传感器，利用transmon量子比特经最优控制制备至多7个声子的Fock态，并在弱共振信号作用后解析声子数分布，从而在信号振幅灵敏度上相对任意经典探测态获得7.0 dB增益，并进一步用神经网络实现无解析模型的贝叶斯估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量机械振荡器可直接耦合力、加速度与引力，是探测新物理和宏观尺度量子力学的重要探针。对于相位未知的信号，要突破标准量子极限需要非高斯量子态，但此类态的超计量学优势此前仅限于离子等较轻系统，尚未在宏观机械系统中实现。

Method: 以高次谐波体声波谐振腔的微克级声学模式为传感对象；利用transmon量子比特通过最优控制将模式制备为至多7个声子的Fock态；在弱共振信号作用后测量声子数分布，并进行贝叶斯分析以提取Fisher信息。同时，基于测得的传感器响应训练神经网络，在无需系统解析模型的情况下执行贝叶斯估计。

Result: 贝叶斯分析给出的Fisher信息为真空值的5倍；相对任意经典探测态（不论其能量）在信号振幅灵敏度上获得7.0 dB增益，当前受限于态制备的不保真度。神经网络方法在达到相同灵敏度增益的同时，将系统偏差降低一半。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了非高斯态制备的声学谐振腔可作为量子增强探针，用于高频引力波、暗物质以及波函数坍缩的探测。

Abstract: Massive mechanical oscillators couple directly to forces, accelerations and gravity, making them attractive probes of new physics and of quantum mechanics at macroscopic scales. Pushing sensitivities beyond the standard quantum limit for signals of unknown phase requires non-Gaussian quantum states, whose metrological advantage has so far been confined to lighter systems such as trapped ions. Here we operate a microgram-scale acoustic mode of a high-overtone bulk acoustic wave resonator as a phase-insensitive quantum-enhanced sensor. A transmon qubit prepares the mode in Fock states of up to seven phonons by optimal control, and resolves its phonon-number distribution after interaction with a weak resonant signal. Bayesian analysis yields a Fisher information five times the vacuum value, a 7.0 dB gain in signal-amplitude sensitivity over any classical probe state, irrespective of its energy, currently limited by state preparation infidelities. We then train a neural network on the measured sensor response to perform Bayesian estimation without any analytical model of the system, showing the same sensitivity gain with half the systematic bias. Our results establish acoustic resonators prepared in non-Gaussian states as quantum-enhanced probes for high-frequency gravitational waves, dark matter and wave-function collapse.

</details>


### [232] [Deterministic and programmable generation of mechanical multipartite entangled states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18778)
*Isabelle Franzen,Max-Emanuel Kern,Q. Rumman Rahman,Igor Kladarić,Stefano Marti,Yiwen Chu,Zhi-Yuan Wei,Matteo Fadel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用超导transmon量子比特与高次谐波体声波谐振器的耦合，通过最优控制工程化的等距操作，确定性地在机械声子模式间制备了多体纠缠态（W态、GHZ态、簇态），并实现了关联噪声传感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模机械谐振器在单器件内提供了大量密集、长寿命的玻色模式，适用于量子模拟、量子信息处理和量子增强传感；而这些应用多依赖多体纠缠，但确定性机械纠缠此前基本局限于模式对，因此需要扩展到多体纠缠的可编程、确定性制备。

Method: 采用高次谐波体声波谐振器（HBAR）与超导transmon量子比特耦合的系统；通过顺序施加最优控制工程化的量子比特与各模式之间的等距（isometry）操作，生成键维为二的矩阵乘积态（MPS）；随后用量子态层析进行全面表征，并利用W态与GHZ态互补的退相干响应开展关联噪声传感。

Result: 确定性地制备了W态、GHZ态和簇态等矩阵乘积态，并通过量子态层析验证了最多四个模式的真多体纠缠；在传感方面，利用存储的W态和GHZ态的互补退相干响应，从局域退相干中分离出模式间相位关联，并对集体退相干给出了定量边界。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了在机械系统中制备多体纠缠态的可扩展方法，在多参数传感以及利用大质量物体检验量子力学方面具有应用前景。

Abstract: Multimode mechanical resonators offer a large set of densely packed, long-lived bosonic modes within a single compact device, making them attractive for quantum simulation, quantum information processing, and quantum-enhanced sensing. Many such applications rely on multipartite entanglement, yet deterministic mechanical entanglement has so far been largely limited to pairs of modes. Here we demonstrate the deterministic and programmable preparation of multipartite entangled states among the phonon modes of a high-overtone bulk acoustic wave resonator coupled to a superconducting transmon qubit. By sequentially applying optimal-control-engineered isometries between the qubit and successive modes, we generate matrix product states of bond dimension two, including $W$, Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ), and cluster states. We fully characterize these states via quantum state tomography and certify genuine multipartite entanglement among up to four modes. We further demonstrate correlated-noise sensing by exploiting the complementary decoherence responses of the stored $W$ and GHZ states, isolating inter-mode phase correlations from local decoherence and placing quantitative bounds on collective dephasing. Our results establish a scalable approach to preparing multipartite entangled states in mechanical systems, with promising applications in multiparameter sensing and tests of quantum mechanics with massive objects.

</details>


### [233] [Relative-Entropy Geometry and Convergence in Nonconvex Wiretap Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18785)
*Farzin Salek,Masahito Hayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出并分析了一种用于经典与量子保密通信中概率单纯形上非凸优化问题的熵下降法，通过相对熵恒等式导出通道无关的优化界与固定步长镜像下降迭代，并建立了收敛性与全局误差界，最后通过数值实验验证了方法有效性及随机预处理的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率单纯形上的非凸优化问题在经典与量子保密通信中具有重要应用，优化变量刻画随机编码器，需联合优化输入分布与随机预处理。现有方法通常依赖degradedness或less noisiness等假设，缺乏对任意有限经典通道的通用收敛性保证，因此需要一种不依赖这些假设的熵下降方法。

Method: 方法基于精确的相对熵恒等式，导出通道无关的majorization界，并构造具有固定单位步长的显式镜像下降迭代。针对严格正转移矩阵，采用平方根参数化与Kurdyka–Łojasiewicz论证分析轨迹有限长度与序列收敛；针对less-noisy通道，给出限制凸表述以获得全局收敛与显式O(1/t)目标误差界。进一步将相对熵构造推广至非对易量子输出，推导协变编码器的单调交替更新及固定块上的相关预处理。

Result: 对任意有限经典通道，证明了定量下降、目标值收敛及累积点在支撑面上的平稳性，无需degradedness或less noisiness假设。对严格正转移矩阵，证明变换轨迹有限长度与整个序列收敛（含单纯形边界极限）。对less-noisy通道，得到全局收敛与显式O(1/t)目标误差界。数值实验与解析基准及约束优化对比，揭示多个吸引值，并证明随机预处理带来严格改进。

Conclusion: 该熵下降法为概率单纯形上非凸优化提供了统一的收敛性理论框架，适用于经典与量子保密通信中的随机编码器优化，且不依赖传统通道假设；数值结果验证了方法的有效性、多吸引子现象以及随机预处理的严格增益。

Abstract: We develop and analyze an entropic descent method for a nonconvex optimization problem on probability simplices arising in classical and quantum secure communication. The optimization variables describe a randomized encoder, allowing its input distribution and stochastic preprocessing to be optimized jointly. An exact relative-entropy identity yields a channel-independent majorization bound and an explicit mirror-descent iteration with a fixed unit step size. We establish quantitative descent, convergence of objective values, and stationarity of accumulation points on their support faces for arbitrary finite classical channels, without assuming degradedness or less noisiness. For strictly positive transition matrices, a square-root parametrization and a Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz argument establish finite length of the transformed trajectory and convergence of the entire sequence, including limits on the simplex boundary. For less-noisy channels, a restricted convex formulation admits global convergence with an explicit \(O(1/t)\) objective-error bound. We extend the relative-entropy construction to noncommuting quantum outputs and derive monotone alternating updates for covariant encoders, including correlated preprocessing over a fixed block. Numerical experiments compare the method with analytic benchmarks and constrained optimization, reveal multiple attracting values, and demonstrate strict improvements from stochastic preprocessing.

</details>


### [234] [$\mathsf{EFI}\Leftrightarrow\mathsf{1PRS}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18799)
*Rishabh Batra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文去除了Cavalar等人构造中的非一致性，证明EFI对与扩张型1PRS等价，并由此得到EFI对的第一个一致性元复杂度刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EFI对被视为量子密码学的"最小"假设，近期工作建立了其与非一致扩张单拷贝伪随机态生成器（1PRS）的等价及非一致元复杂度刻画。本文旨在去除该构造中的非一致性，建立EFI对与扩张1PRS的等价。

Method: 通过去除Cavalar等人[CCC+26]构造中的非一致性，重新构造EFI对与扩张1PRS之间的等价关系，并结合Batra与Jain[BJ24]关于统计可验证单向态生成器与承诺等价的结果。

Result: 证明了EFI对与扩张1PRS等价；作为推论，得到了EFI对的第一个一致性元复杂度刻画；结合[BJ24]的结果，给出了经典中单向函数、承诺与伪随机生成器等价关系的量子类比。

Conclusion: 该工作消除了EFI对与1PRS等价性中的非一致性限制，统一了量子密码学中若干基本原语的等价关系，为量子密码学的最小假设提供了更强的一致性理论基础。

Abstract: A series of recent works has established EFI pairs as the "minimal" assumption for quantum cryptography. Recently, Cavalar et al. [CCC+26] showed that EFI pairs are equivalent to non-uniform expanding single-copy pseudorandom state generators (1PRS), and obtained a non-uniform meta-complexity characterization of EFI pairs. We remove the non-uniformity in their construction and prove that EFI pairs are equivalent to expanding 1PRS. As a corollary, we obtain the first uniform meta-complexity characterization of EFI pairs. Together with the equivalence of statistically verifiable one-way state generators and commitments proved by Batra and Jain [BJ24], this gives a quantum analogue of the classical equivalence between one-way functions, commitments, and pseudorandom generators.

</details>


### [235] [On the coincidence of discrete and continuous definitions of Entanglement of Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18816)
*Haobo Ma,Maksim E. Shirokov,Wenlin Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明可分双分体Hilbert空间上的任意Borel纯态系综（平均纠缠有限）都可由一个可数系综逼近，二者重心相同且额外平均纠缠任意小，从而在无穷维情形下离散与连续版本的Formation of Entanglement一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠形成（Entanglement of Formation）的离散定义（可数系综）与连续定义（一般Borel系综）在无穷维双分体系统上是否一致此前尚不明确，需要建立两者的等价性。

Method: 将Borel纯双分体态系综等同于Hilbert–Schmidt算子空间单位球上的Borel概率测度；把测度支撑集划分为适当的胞腔，用每个胞腔的条件重心替代该胞腔，并递归构造得到可数系综。证明在AI辅助下完成。

Result: 任意有限平均纠缠的Borel纯态系综都存在可数系综，具有相同重心且额外平均纠缠任意小；由此离散与连续版本的Formation of Entanglement在整个无穷维双分体量子系统态空间上（允许取无穷值）重合。

Conclusion: 离散与连续的Entanglement of Formation在无穷维双分体系统中一致，该结果通过测度重心逼近的递归构造得到。

Abstract: It is proved that every Borel ensemble of pure states on a separable bipartite Hilbert space with finite average entanglement admits a countable ensemble with the same barycenter and arbitrarily small additional average entanglement. Consequently, the discrete and continuous versions of the Entanglement of Formation coincide on the whole space of states of infinite-dimensional bipartite quantum system (with infinite values allowed).
  The main idea of the proof (realized with the AI-assistance) is the identification of a Borel ensemble of pure bipartite states with a Borel probability measure on the unit sphere of the space of Hilbert--Schmidt operators followed by dividing the support of this measure into appropriate cells and replacing each cell by its conditional barycenter. The resulting countable ensemble is obtained by a recursive construction.

</details>


### [236] [Gaussian entanglement and quantum communication: geodesic optimization and multimode bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18827)
*Hemant K. Mishra,Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了计算高斯限制下相对熵纠缠与Rains纠缠的框架，通过矩阵几何均值下的凸几何表示将高斯Rains纠缠化为在PPT高斯态上最小化相对熵，得到唯一最优参考态与可加性，并给出多项式比特复杂度的凸主序算法，用以界定纠缠提纯、密钥提取及量子/私密通信速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 损耗与噪声限制了光在纠缠分发、量子信息传输和密钥建立中的应用。高斯态虽可由均值与协方差矩阵紧凑描述，但其纠缠度量仍需全局优化，缺乏可计算且具理论保证的框架。

Method: 提出计算高斯限制版相对熵纠缠与Rains纠缠的框架：利用积分表示揭示矩阵几何均值下的凸几何结构，将高斯Rains纠缠约化为在正部分转置（PPT）高斯态上最小化相对熵；构造凸主序算法，具备显式几何收敛率且对忠实正则化参数一致，对纯输入态有效；结合受控数值积分与近似凸子问题求解，给出比特复杂度界。

Result: 获得唯一最优参考态及独立高斯输入态的可加性；算法比特复杂度对每个固定有理协方差矩阵在精度比特上为多项式，并显式依赖协方差规模。借助隐形传态模拟、能量分配与无源基损耗核的凸优化，在输入能量约束下给出量子与私密通信界（允许任意双向经典辅助协议）；二模与三模算例与可达速率比较，包括匹配纯损耗基准。此外区分高斯限制量与无限制量，并给出计算后者、误差可控的光子数截断方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为高斯纠缠度量的可计算评估提供了具备收敛与复杂度保证的统一框架，可用于界定纠缠与密钥的可提取量及通信速率，并将高斯限制结果与非高斯（无限制）对应量相区分。

Abstract: Loss and noise limit the use of light to distribute entanglement, transmit quantum information, and establish secret keys. Gaussian states have a compact description in terms of means and covariance matrices, but their entanglement still requires global optimization. We develop a framework for computing Gaussian-restricted versions of the relative entropy of entanglement and Rains entanglement. An integral representation reveals a convex geometry under matrix geometric means and reduces Gaussian Rains entanglement to relative entropy minimized over Gaussian states with positive partial transpose. We obtain unique optimal reference states and additivity for independent Gaussian input states. Our convex-majorization algorithm has an explicit geometric convergence rate, uniform in the faithful regularization parameter and valid for pure input states. Controlled numerical integration and approximate convex subproblem solutions give a bit-complexity bound that is polynomial in accuracy bits for each fixed rational input covariance matrix, with explicit dependence on covariance size. These calculations bound extractable entanglement and, through the relative entropy of entanglement, extractable secret key. Teleportation simulation, energy allocation, and a convex optimization of passive-basis loss cores give bounds on quantum and private communication under an input-energy constraint, allowing arbitrary two-way classically assisted protocols. Two- and three-mode examples compare the bounds with achievable rates, including matching pure-loss benchmarks. Finally, we separate the Gaussian-restricted quantities from their unrestricted counterparts and give a photon-number truncation method with controlled error for computing the latter.

</details>
